## 1680007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ…。<line-break>その顔だと、気づいておらぬようだな。<line-break>　<line-break>レムオンは、幼いころから<line-break>ティアナに惹かれていた。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh... From that look on your face, I see you hadn't noticed. Lemghon has been drawn to Tiana since childhood.<end>"
KO: "후후후…. 그 표정을 보니 눈치채지 못한 모양이군. 레무온은 어릴 때부터 티아나에게 마음이 있었어.<end>"

## 1680009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "普段、冷静な男なだけに<line-break>逆上すると<line-break>何をしでかすかわからない。<line-break>無用な心配とは思うが、<line-break>気に留めておいてほしい。<end>"
EN: "Because he is usually so composed, there is no telling what he might do if he loses his head. I may be worrying needlessly, but I want you to bear it in mind.<end>"
KO: "평소 침착한 남자인 만큼 이성을 잃으면 무슨 짓을 할지 몰라. 쓸데없는 걱정일지도 모르지만 기억해 두었으면 한다.<end>"

## 1680010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よく来てくれた、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Thank you for coming, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "잘 와줬다, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1680011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そなたを頼りにしておる。<line-break>ティアナを守ってやってほしい。<line-break>　<line-break>あのタルテュバの一件以来…<line-break>レムオン、それにアトレイアのことが<line-break>気にかかるのだ。<end>"
EN: "I am relying on you. I want you to protect Tiana. Ever since that business with Tartuba, I have been concerned about Lemghon―and Atleia as well.<end>"
KO: "그대를 의지하고 있다. 티아나를 지켜 주었으면 한다. 타르튜바 사건 이후… 레무온, 그리고 아트레이아 일이 마음에 걸리는구나.<end>"

## 1680012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふたりの、ティアナに対する感情は<line-break>それぞれに単純ではない。<line-break>　<line-break>それゆえ、我々には思いもよらぬ<line-break>行動に出るやもしれぬ。<line-break>ティアナを守ってやってほしい。<end>"
EN: "Their feelings for Tiana are complicated, each in a different way. That may lead them to act in ways none of us could foresee. Please protect Tiana.<end>"
KO: "두 사람이 티아나에게 품은 감정은 각기 단순하지 않다. 그렇기에 우리로서는 상상도 못 할 행동에 나설지도 모른다. 티아나를 지켜 줬으면 한다.<end>"

## 1680013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最近のレムオンの動きに<line-break>気になることはないか？<end>"
EN: "Have you noticed anything troubling in Lemghon's recent movements?<end>"
KO: "요즘 레무온의 움직임 중 신경 쓰이는 건 없나?<end>"

## 1680014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "レムオンは、幼いころから<line-break>ティアナに惹かれていた。<end>"
EN: "Lemghon has been drawn to Tiana since childhood.<end>"
KO: "레무온은 어릴 때부터 티아나에게 끌리고 있었다.<end>"

## 1680016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "普段、冷静な男なだけに<line-break>逆上すると<line-break>何をしでかすかわからない。<line-break>無用な心配とは思うが、<line-break>気に留めておいてほしい。<end>"
EN: "Because he is usually so composed, there is no telling what he might do if he loses his head. I may be worrying needlessly, but I want you to bear it in mind.<end>"
KO: "평소 침착한 남자인 만큼 이성을 잃으면 무슨 짓을 할지 몰라. 쓸데없는 걱정일지도 모르지만 기억해 두었으면 한다.<end>"

## 1680017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それから、ティアナの方も、<line-break>何か様子がおかしかった。<line-break>気づかってやってくれ。<line-break>フフ…、娘を気づかうのは<line-break>本来は母親の役目なのだろうがな。<end>"
EN: "And Tiana herself seemed somehow out of sorts. Look after her for me. Heh... I suppose worrying over a daughter should rightfully be her mother's duty.<end>"
KO: "그리고 티아나도 어딘가 상태가 이상했다. 잘 살펴줘. 후후… 딸을 돌보는 건 원래 어머니의 역할이겠지만.<end>"

## 1680018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "近ごろ、ティアナについて<line-break>気になることはないか？<line-break>　<line-break>舞踏会で様子がおかしかった。<line-break>もともと気の強い娘だが<line-break>あのような形相は見たことがない。<end>"
EN: "Have you noticed anything troubling about Tiana lately? She was behaving strangely at the ball. She has always been strong-willed, but I have never seen such a look on her face.<end>"
KO: "요즘 티아나에 대해 신경 쓰이는 건 없나? 무도회에서 모습이 이상했어. 원래 기가 센 아이지만 그런 표정은 본 적이 없다.<end>"

## 1680019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…確かに気の回しすぎかもしれぬ。<end>"
EN: "Perhaps I am reading too much into it.<end>"
KO: "…확실히 너무 신경 쓴 걸지도 모르겠군.<end>"

## 1680020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アトレイアも変わった。<line-break>明るくなった。<line-break>　<line-break>最近、空中庭園でよく見かけるが、<line-break>笑顔であいさつをするようになった。<end>"
EN: "Atleia has changed as well. She is much more cheerful. I often see her in the Hanging Gardens these days, and now she greets me with a smile.<end>"
KO: "아트레이아도 변했어. 밝아졌지. 요즘 공중정원에서 자주 보는데 웃으며 인사하게 됐어.<end>"

## 1680021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ…、あの娘の人生には<line-break>少し、関わりがあってな。<line-break>うれしいことだ。<end>"
EN: "Heh... I have had some small part in that girl's life. That makes me glad.<end>"
KO: "후후… 그 아이의 인생에는 조금 인연이 있어서 말이지. 기쁜 일이야.<end>"

## 1680022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>か。<line-break>今朝、空中庭園で<line-break>アトレイアに会って驚いた。<line-break>目を見張るほど美しくなった。<line-break>…そう思わぬか？<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. I met Atleia in the Hanging Gardens this morning, and she took me by surprise. She has grown remarkably beautiful. Do you not agree?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>인가. 오늘 아침 공중정원에서 아트레이아를 만나 놀랐지. 눈이 휘둥그레질 만큼 아름다워졌더군. …그렇지 않나?<end>"

## 1680023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0恋をすれば<line-break>女は変わる。<line-break>美しくなる。<line-break><value:$28>、<line-break>心当たりはないか？<end>誰かがアトレイアを<line-break>孤独の闇から<line-break>解放してくれたのだろう。<line-break>その者に<line-break>感謝しておくとしよう。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Love changes a woman. It makes her beautiful. Does that bring anyone to mind, <value:$28>?<end>Someone must have freed Atleia from the darkness of her solitude. I shall have to thank that person.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0사랑을 하면 여자는 변한다. 아름다워지지. <value:$28>, 짐작 가는 사람이 없나?<end>누군가가 아트레이아를 고독의 어둠에서 해방해 준 모양이군. 그 사람에게 감사해 두도록 하지.<end>"

## 1680024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>か。<line-break>長い間、私をわずらわせていた問題が<line-break>ひとつ片付きそうだ。<line-break>レムオン…、あの男も終わりだ。<line-break>坊やの皮肉が聞けなくなるのは、<line-break>さびしい気もするが…。<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. It seems one of the problems that has plagued me for so long is about to be resolved. Lemghon... that man is finished. Though I may rather miss the boy's barbed remarks.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>인가. 오랫동안 날 괴롭히던 문제가 하나 해결될 것 같군. 레무온… 그 남자도 이제 끝이다. 꼬맹이의 빈정거림을 못 듣게 되는 건 좀 쓸쓸하지만….<end>"

## 1680025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの誇り高い男が堕ちていくのを<line-break>この玉座から見守ってやるとしよう。<end>"
EN: "I shall watch from this throne as that proud man falls.<end>"
KO: "그 자존심 높은 남자가 타락해 가는 모습을 이 왕좌에서 지켜보도록 하지.<end>"

## 1680026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>か。<line-break>レムオン坊やとはうまくいっておるか？<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. Are you getting along with young Lemghon?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>인가. 레무온 도련님과는 잘 지내고 있나?<end>"

## 1680027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ…、<line-break>あの男、他人と仲よくやれるような<line-break>人間ではない。<line-break>屋敷を追い出されない以上、<line-break>この上なく、うまくいっていると<line-break>見るべきだろう。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh... That man is hardly capable of getting along with anyone. If he has not thrown you out of his manor, I would say matters could not be going better.<end>"
KO: "후후후… 그 남자는 남과 사이좋게 지낼 수 있는 인간이 아니다. 저택에서 쫓겨나지 않은 이상 이보다 더 잘될 수 없을 정도라고 봐야겠지.<end>"

## 1680028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その調子でレムオンの信用を得るのだ。<line-break>そして、油断させるのだ。<line-break>…ああいう男は、一度崩れればもろい。<end>"
EN: "Keep at it and earn Lemghon's trust. Then lull him into complacency. A man like that is fragile once he begins to crack.<end>"
KO: "그 기세로 레무온의 신뢰를 얻어라. 그리고 방심하게 만들어. …그런 남자는 한 번 무너지면 약해.<end>"

## 1680029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>よ。<line-break>期待している。<line-break>フリントのこと、忘れるな。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, I have high hopes for you. Do not forget Flint.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>. 기대하고 있다. 플린트 일을 잊지 마라.<end>"

## 1680030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>そなたいくつになる？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, how old are you?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 그대는 몇 살인가?<end>"

## 1680031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか、ティアナと同じか。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>我が娘ティアナは、<line-break>王宮の外の人間を知らぬ。<line-break>周りに年齢の近い者も少ない。<end>"
EN: "I see. The same age as Tiana. My daughter knows no one outside the royal palace, and there are few people her age around her.<end>"
KO: "그런가, 티아나와 같군. 내 딸 티아나는 왕궁 밖 사람을 모른다. 주변에 나이가 비슷한 자도 적지.<end>"

## 1680032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そなたならば、<line-break>よい話相手になるだろう。<line-break>　<line-break>だが、注意するがいい。<line-break>ティアナは、この私でさえ<line-break>手を焼くほど、したたかな娘だ。<end>"
EN: "You would make a good companion for her. But be warned: Tiana is shrewd enough to give even me trouble.<end>"
KO: "그대라면 좋은 말상대가 되겠지. 하지만 조심해라. 티아나는 나조차 애를 먹을 만큼 만만치 않은 아이다.<end>"

## 1680033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>か。<line-break>ゼネテスはどうしておる？<line-break>総司令らしく振る舞っておるか？<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. How is Xenetes faring? Is he conducting himself like a commander-in-chief?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>인가. 제네테스는 어떻게 지내나? 총사령관답게 행동하고 있나?<end>"

## 1680034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ…、厳しい評価だ。<line-break>まあ、よい。<line-break>最初から期待しておらぬ。<line-break>だが、ゼネテスは<line-break>そなたを高く買っているようだ。<end>"
EN: "Heh... A harsh assessment. No matter. I expected nothing of him from the start. Xenetes, however, seems to think highly of you.<end>"
KO: "후후… 엄한 평가군. 뭐, 됐다. 처음부터 기대하지 않았으니까. 하지만 제네테스는 자네를 높이 평가하는 모양이야.<end>"

## 1680035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その期待に応えてやってほしい。<line-break>そなたにならば、できるはずだ。<end>"
EN: "I hope you will live up to his expectations. I believe you can.<end>"
KO: "그 기대에 부응해 주었으면 한다. 그대라면 할 수 있을 것이다.<end>"

## 1680036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>か。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>ゼネテスとはうまくやっているか？<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. Are you getting along with Xenetes?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>인가. 제네테스와는 잘 지내고 있나?<end>"

## 1680037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれは器用で頭もいいが、<line-break>立ち回りがヘタだ。<line-break>…敵を作りやすい。<end>"
EN: "He is capable and clever, but he handles himself poorly. He makes enemies all too easily.<end>"
KO: "그 녀석은 손재주도 좋고 머리도 좋지만 처신이 서툴러. …적을 만들기 쉽지.<end>"

## 1680038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、そなたには<line-break>人を惹きつける力がある。<line-break>　<line-break>その魅力が<line-break>ゼネテスを救うこともあるだろう。<line-break>力になってやってほしい。<end>"
EN: "But you have a gift for drawing people to you. That quality may yet save Xenetes. I want you to stand by him.<end>"
KO: "하지만 자네에게는 사람을 끌어당기는 힘이 있어. 그 매력이 제네테스를 구할 때도 있겠지. 힘이 되어 줬으면 한다.<end>"

## 1680039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フリントの子、<line-break><value:$28>…。<line-break>　<line-break>…いい目をしている。<line-break>無限の可能性を感じさせる目だ。<end>"
EN: "Flint's child, <value:$28>... You have a fine look in your eyes. It speaks of limitless potential.<end>"
KO: "플린트의 아이, <value:$28>…. …좋은 눈을 하고 있군. 무한한 가능성을 느끼게 하는 눈이다.<end>"

## 1680040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もし、そなたが平穏を望むならば<line-break>その望みは捨てた方がよい。<line-break>　<line-break>そなたに秘められた可能性が<line-break>そなたをそっとしては<line-break>おかぬだろう。<end>"
EN: "If you desire a peaceful life, you would do well to abandon that hope. The potential within you will not allow you to live undisturbed.<end>"
KO: "그대가 평온을 원한다면 그 바람은 버리는 편이 좋다. 그대에게 숨겨진 가능성이 그대를 가만두지 않을 테니.<end>"

## 1680041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フリントの子、<line-break><value:$28>…。<line-break>　<line-break>…いい目をしている。<line-break>無限の可能性を感じさせる目だ。<end>"
EN: "Flint's child, <value:$28>... You have a fine look in your eyes. It speaks of limitless potential.<end>"
KO: "플린트의 아이, <value:$28>…. …좋은 눈을 하고 있군. 무한한 가능성을 느끼게 하는 눈이다.<end>"

## 1680042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もし、そなたが平穏を望むならば<line-break>その望みは捨てた方がよい。<line-break>　<line-break>そなたに秘められた可能性が<line-break>そなたをそっとしては<line-break>おかぬだろう。<end>"

## 1680043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>か。<line-break>旅はどうだ？<line-break>　<line-break>今は存分に世界を見て回ることだ。<line-break>そなたには、それが許されている。<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. How are your travels? For now, see as much of the world as you can. You have that freedom.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>인가. 여행은 어떠냐? 지금은 마음껏 세상을 둘러보아라. 그대에게는 그것이 허락되어 있다.<end>"

## 1680044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は死ぬまで、いや、<line-break>死んだあともこの王宮を<line-break>出ることはないだろう。<line-break>もはや、それを不幸とは思わぬが、<line-break>そなたを見ていると<line-break>うらやましくもある。<end>"
EN: "I shall never leave this royal palace―not until I die, nor even after death. I no longer consider that a misfortune, yet when I look at you, I cannot help but feel a little envy.<end>"
KO: "나는 죽을 때까지, 아니, 죽은 뒤에도 이 왕궁을떠나지 못하겠지. 이제 그것을 불행이라 생각하진 않지만, 너를 보고 있으면부럽기도 하구나.<end>"

## 1680045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>そなたいくつになる？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, how old are you?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 그대는 몇 살인가?<end>"

## 1680046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか、ティアナと同じか。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>我が娘ティアナは、<line-break>王宮の外の人間を知らぬ。<line-break>周りに年齢の近い者も少ない。<end>"
EN: "I see. The same age as Tiana. My daughter knows no one outside the royal palace, and there are few people her age around her.<end>"
KO: "그런가, 티아나와 같군. 내 딸 티아나는 왕궁 밖 사람을 모른다. 주변에 나이가 비슷한 자도 적지.<end>"

## 1680047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そなたならば、<line-break>よい話相手になるだろう。<line-break>　<line-break>だが、注意するがいい。<line-break>ティアナは、この私でさえ<line-break>手を焼くほど、したたかな娘だ。<end>"
EN: "You would make a good companion for her. But be warned: Tiana is shrewd enough to give even me trouble.<end>"
KO: "그대라면 좋은 말상대가 되겠지. 하지만 조심해라. 티아나는 나조차 애를 먹을 만큼 만만치 않은 아이다.<end>"

## 1680048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "七竜家当主の方々との<line-break>会談のお時間でございます。<end>"
EN: "It is time for your meeting with the heads of the Seven Dragon Houses.<end>"
KO: "칠룡가 당주분들과 회담하실 시간입니다.<end>"

## 1680049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>よ。<line-break>また来るとよい。<end>"
EN: "I see. Do come again, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그런가. <value:$28>여, 또 오거라.<end>"

## 1680050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>そなたには礼を言わねばならぬ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, I owe you my thanks.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 너에게는 감사를 해야겠구나.<end>"

## 1680051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナがずいぶんと<line-break>世話になっているそうではないか。<end>"
EN: "I hear you have done a great deal for Tiana.<end>"
KO: "티아나가 꽤 신세를 지고 있다더군.<end>"

## 1680052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王宮を抜け出しての密会とは、<line-break>ティアナがのぼせ上がるのも<line-break>無理はない。<line-break>そなたは、思いのほか<line-break>女心をつかむのがうまいようだ。<end>"
EN: "Sneaking out of the royal palace for a secret rendezvous―no wonder Tiana is so taken with you. You have more of a way with women than I expected.<end>"
KO: "왕궁을 빠져나와 밀회라니, 티아나가 푹 빠지는 것도 무리는 아니군. 자네는 생각보다 여심을 사로잡는 데 능숙한 모양이야.<end>"

## 1680053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そなたを責める気は毛頭ない。<line-break>あの子は、誰とでも気兼ねなく<line-break>付き合っているようだが、本当は…<line-break>本当は、心を開くことを<line-break>知らなかった不幸な娘。<line-break>そのティアナが心を開こうとしている。<end>"
EN: "I have no intention of blaming you. She may seem perfectly at ease with everyone, but the truth is... the truth is, she was an unhappy girl who never learned to open her heart. And now Tiana is trying to do just that.<end>"
KO: "…그대를 탓할 생각은 조금도 없다. 그 아이는 누구와도 스스럼없이 지내는 듯 보이지만 사실은… 마음을 여는 법을 몰랐던 불행한 아이였지. 그 티아나가 마음을 열려고 하고 있다.<end>"

## 1680054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ゼネテスを婿に選んだのは、<line-break>早計だったかもしれぬ。<end>"
EN: "Perhaps I was too hasty in choosing Xenetes as her husband.<end>"
KO: "…제네테스를 사위로 고른 건 성급했을지도 모르겠군.<end>"

## 1680055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ…、はっきり言うがいい。<line-break>ゼネテスを次期国王につけ<line-break>ファーロス家が王位を簒奪する。<line-break>雌狐エリスが、そう企んでいると<line-break>言うのであろう？<end>"
EN: "Heh heh... Go on, say it plainly. The vixen Eris means to put Xenetes on the throne as the next king and have House Pharos usurp the throne. That is what you mean to say, is it not?<end>"
KO: "후후후… 똑똑히 말해 보거라. 제네테스를 차기 국왕으로 세워 파로스 가문이 왕위를 찬탈한다. 암여우 에리스가 그렇게 꾸미고 있다고 말하려는 것이겠지?<end>"

## 1680056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "悪い話ではないが、<line-break>ティアナを不幸にしてまで<line-break>実現する価値はない。<line-break>ティアナの婿は<line-break>ファーロス家の者でなくとも<line-break>よいのだ。<end>"
EN: "It is not an unattractive prospect, but it is not worth making Tiana miserable. Her husband need not come from House Pharos.<end>"
KO: "나쁜 이야기는 아니지만 티아나를 불행하게 하면서까지 실현할 가치는 없어. 티아나의 남편은 파로스 가문 사람이 아니어도 된다.<end>"

## 1680057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ただ、ティアナを幸せにできる<line-break>男であれば…。<end>"
EN: "He need only be a man who can make Tiana happy...<end>"
KO: "그저 티아나를 행복하게 해 줄 남자라면….<end>"

## 1680058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "七竜家当主の方々との<line-break>会談のお時間でございます。<end>"
EN: "It is time for your meeting with the heads of the Seven Dragon Houses.<end>"
KO: "칠룡가 당주분들과 회담하실 시간입니다.<end>"

## 1680059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか。<line-break><value:$28>よ。<line-break>これからもティアナを頼む。<line-break>私の命より大事な娘だ。<end>"
EN: "Very well. <value:$28>, please continue to look after Tiana. She is dearer to me than life itself.<end>"
KO: "그렇군. <value:$28>여. 앞으로도 티아나를 부탁한다. 내 목숨보다 소중한 딸이다.<end>"

## 1680060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>か。<line-break>よく来た。<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. Welcome.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>인가. 잘 왔다.<end>"

## 1680061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王妃様、シドア候が<line-break>面会を求めております。<end>"
EN: "Your Majesty, Marquis Sidoa requests an audience.<end>"
KO: "왕비님, 시도아 후작이 면회를 청하고 있습니다.<end>"

## 1680062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待たせておけ。<end>"
EN: "Let him wait.<end>"
KO: "기다리게 해.<end>"

## 1680063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "３時間前からお待ちですが。<end>"
EN: "He has already been waiting for three hours.<end>"
KO: "3시간 전부터 기다리고 계십니다.<end>"

## 1680064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "構わぬ。<line-break>その程度の能しかない男だ。<end>"
EN: "No matter. That is the extent of the man's ability.<end>"
KO: "상관없다. 그 정도 재주밖에 없는 남자다.<end>"

## 1680065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "４時間でも５時間でも待つだろう。<line-break>七竜家の当主であろうと、<line-break>いまや私に逆らうことはできない。<end>"
EN: "He will wait four hours, or five if need be. He may be the head of one of the Seven Dragon Houses, but he cannot defy me now.<end>"
KO: "네 시간이고 다섯 시간이고 기다리겠지. 칠룡가의 당주라 해도 이제는 내게 거역할 수 없다.<end>"

## 1680066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ…、望んでこうなったのではない。<line-break>私は権力を求めて<line-break>王室へ嫁いだのではないのだ。<end>"
EN: "Heh... I never asked for any of this. I did not marry into the royal family in pursuit of power.<end>"
KO: "후후… 원해서 이렇게 된 게 아니야. 나는 권력을 원해 왕실에 시집간 게 아니다.<end>"

## 1680067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "陛下はファーロス家の力が<line-break>目当てだった。<line-break>仕方ない。大貴族の娘なのだからな。<line-break>だが、王室に入った当初、<line-break>あれほど陛下が冷たくされたのは…<line-break>つらかった…。<end>"
EN: "His Majesty wanted the power of House Pharos. That was only to be expected; I was the daughter of a great noble house. But when I first entered the royal family, His Majesty treated me so coldly... It was painful.<end>"
KO: "폐하는 파로스 가문의 힘이 목적이었어. 어쩔 수 없지. 대귀족의 딸이니까. 하지만 왕실에 들어온 처음에 폐하께서 그렇게 차갑게 대하셨던 건… 괴로웠다….<end>"

## 1680068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だからこそ、ティアナを<line-break>身ごもったときの喜びは大きかった。<line-break>　<line-break>ティアナこそ、<line-break>陛下と私とをつなぐ<line-break>きずなになるはずだった。<end>"
EN: "That was why I felt such joy when I conceived Tiana. She was to be the bond that united His Majesty and me.<end>"
KO: "그래서 티아나를 가졌을 때의 기쁨은 컸다. 티아나야말로 폐하와 나를 이어 주는 인연이 될 거라 생각했지.<end>"

## 1680069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、やがて<line-break>王宮に政変が起こった。<line-break>…テジャワの変と呼ばれている。<line-break>ナグイゼ伯テジャワは、<line-break>国王を廃し、先代の王の娘、<line-break>幼いアトレイアを王位につけ…<end>"
EN: "But in time, there was a political upheaval at the royal court. It is known as the Tejava Incident. Count Tejava of Nagize sought to depose the king and place young Atleia, daughter of the previous king, upon the throne...<end>"
KO: "하지만 이윽고 왕궁에서 정변이 일어났다. …데자와의 변이라 불리지. 나구이제 백작 데자와는 국왕을 폐하고 선왕의 딸인 어린 아트레이아를 왕위에 앉히고…<end>"

## 1680070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "政権をほしいままにしようと<line-break>したのだ。<end>"
EN: "The aim was to seize control of the government and wield power at will.<end>"
KO: "정권을 제멋대로 휘두르려 했던 것이다.<end>"

## 1680071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は、誰よりも先に陰謀に気づき、<line-break>これを阻止するため奔走した。<end>"
EN: "I was the first to uncover the conspiracy, and I did everything in my power to stop it.<end>"
KO: "나는 누구보다 먼저 음모를 알아차리고 이를 막기 위해 동분서주했다.<end>"

## 1680072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それまで、侍女としか口をきいたことの<line-break>なかった私が、独断で兵を動かし、<line-break>陰謀に加担した者全員を検挙した。<line-break>だがそれは、有力貴族全体を<line-break>敵に回すことを意味した。<end>"
EN: "Until then, I had spoken to no one but my ladies-in-waiting. Yet acting entirely on my own, I mobilized the troops and arrested every conspirator. In doing so, however, I made enemies of all the great nobles.<end>"
KO: "그때까지 시녀와밖에 이야기해 본 적 없던 내가 독단으로 병사를 움직여 음모에 가담한 자들을 모두 검거했다. 하지만 그건 유력 귀족 전체를 적으로 돌린다는 뜻이었다.<end>"

## 1680073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "当時、七竜家と呼ばれる有力貴族は<line-break>王室と肩を並べるほどの<line-break>力を誇っていた。<line-break>正面から戦っても勝ち目はない…。<line-break>そこで、各家の不和を誘うため<line-break>謀略に通じるようになった。<end>"
EN: "In those days, the great nobles known as the Seven Dragon Houses wielded power rivaling that of the royal family. I could never have defeated them in open conflict. So I learned the arts of intrigue, setting the houses against one another.<end>"
KO: "당시 칠룡가라 불린 유력 귀족들은 왕실과 어깨를 나란히 할 정도의 힘을 자랑했다. 정면으로 싸워서는 승산이 없어…. 그래서 각 가문 사이의 불화를 유도하려고 모략에 능해졌다.<end>"

## 1680074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "一方、クーデターの失敗を知った<line-break>前王妃は、娘のアトレイアを道連れに<line-break>服毒自殺を謀った。<end>"
EN: "Meanwhile, when the former queen learned that the coup had failed, she tried to poison herself and take her daughter Atleia with her.<end>"
KO: "한편 쿠데타 실패를 안 전 왕비는 딸 아트레이아를 데리고 음독자살을 시도했다.<end>"

## 1680075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は、アトレイアを救うため、<line-break>毒にも詳しくなった。<line-break>　<line-break>家族を守りたいがため、<line-break>必死で戦った。<line-break>…そして、これがその結果だ。<end>"
EN: "To save Atleia, I became well versed in poisons. I fought desperately, wanting only to protect my family. And this is the result.<end>"
KO: "나는 아트레이아를 구하기 위해 독에도 밝아졌다. 가족을 지키려 필사적으로 싸웠다. …그리고 이것이 그 결과다.<end>"

## 1680076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "貴族は力を失った。<line-break>ティアナは母を嫌悪している。<line-break>そして、私の夫は…<line-break>国王セルモノーは<line-break>政治から離れ、<line-break>すべてにおいてあの調子だ。<end>"
EN: "The nobles have lost their power. Tiana despises her mother. And my husband... King Selmono has withdrawn from politics, and he is like that in all things.<end>"
KO: "귀족은 힘을 잃었다. 티아나는 어머니를 혐오하고 있다. 그리고 내 남편… 국왕 셀모노는 정치에서 물러나 모든 일에 늘 저런 상태다.<end>"

## 1680077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…いっそ、あの政変で殺されておけば<line-break>みな、幸せだったのかもしれぬ。<line-break>そう思いもしたものだ。<line-break>最善だったとは思わぬ。<line-break>だが、後悔はすまい…。<line-break>今はそう思っている。<end>"
EN: "There were times when I thought perhaps everyone would have been happier if I had been killed in that upheaval. I do not believe it was the best course. But I will not regret it. That is how I see it now.<end>"
KO: "…차라리 그 정변 때 죽었더라면 모두가 행복했을지도 모른다고 생각한 적도 있다. 최선이었다고는 생각하지 않는다. 하지만 후회하지는 않겠다…. 지금은 그렇게 생각하고 있다.<end>"

## 1680078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、そろそろ<line-break>シドア候の<line-break>愚痴に耳を貸してくれようか。<line-break><value:$28>、<line-break>はずしてくれぬか？<line-break>　<line-break>また来るがよい。<end>"
EN: "Well, I suppose it is time I listened to Marquis Sidoa's grievances. <value:$28>, would you leave us? Come and see me again.<end>"
KO: "자, 이제 슬슬 시도아 후작의 푸념을 들어 줘야겠군. <value:$28>, 자리를 비켜 주겠나? 또 오도록 해.<end>"

## 1680079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "陛下、<line-break>ようこそお越しくださいました。<end>"
EN: "Your Majesty, welcome.<end>"
KO: "폐하, 잘 오셨습니다.<end>"

## 1680080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王妃に逆らえる者など<line-break>このロストールにはおらぬ。　　<line-break>　<line-break>ふふ、その王妃のお呼びとあれば<line-break>足を運ばぬわけにはいくまい？<end>"
EN: "No one in Rostorl would dare defy the queen. Heh. And when the queen herself summons me, how could I refuse to come?<end>"
KO: "왕비에게 거스를 수 있는 자는 이 로스톨에 없다. 후후, 그 왕비의 부름이라면 발걸음을 하지 않을 수 없겠지?<end>"

## 1680081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "陛下、本日はともに食事でもと…。<line-break>私が料理をいたしました。<line-break>　<line-break>すぐに運ばせましょう。<line-break>しばし、お待ちください。<end>"
EN: "Your Majesty, I thought we might dine together today. I prepared the meal myself. I shall have it brought in at once. Please wait just a moment.<end>"
KO: "폐하, 오늘은 함께 식사라도…. 제가 요리했습니다. 곧 가져오게 하겠습니다. 잠시 기다려 주십시오.<end>"

## 1680082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いかんな。王妃は忙しい身。<line-break>国の柱ではないか。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>王妃に料理などさせておいては<line-break>国政はどうなる？<line-break>余のような頼りない王に任せる気か？<end>"
EN: "That will not do. The queen is far too busy. You are the pillar of this nation, are you not? If you spend your time cooking, what will become of the affairs of state? Do you mean to leave them to an undependable king like me?<end>"
KO: "안 되지. 왕비는 바쁜 몸이야. 나라의 기둥 아닌가. 왕비에게 요리나 시켜서 국정은 어쩌려고? 나 같은 못 미더운 왕에게 맡길 셈인가?<end>"

## 1680083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おたわむれを。<line-break>陛下なくして<line-break>国政が立ち行きましょうか？<line-break>夫のために料理をするのは妻の役目。<line-break>私にとって、陛下のために<line-break>料理することは楽しみでございます。<end>"
EN: "You jest, Your Majesty. How could the state function without you? It is a wife's duty to cook for her husband, and it gives me great pleasure to cook for you.<end>"
KO: "농담도 참. 폐하 없이 국정이 돌아가겠습니까? 남편을 위해 요리하는 것은 아내의 역할. 제게는 폐하를 위해 요리하는 일이 즐거움입니다.<end>"

## 1680084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "結婚以来、そなたの料理に　　　　　<line-break>余が手をつけたことは<line-break>一度もなかったが、<line-break>それでも、余のために料理を<line-break>するのが楽しいか…。奇特なことよ。<line-break>ふふ、エリスは女の鑑よ。よい妻だ。<end>"
EN: "Not once since our marriage have I touched your cooking, yet you still take pleasure in cooking for me? How remarkably devoted. Heh. Eris, you are a model among women. A fine wife indeed.<end>"
KO: "결혼한 뒤로 네 요리에 내가 손댄 적은 한 번도 없었는데 그래도 날 위해 요리하는 게 즐겁단 말인가…. 참 기특하군. 후후, 에리스는 여인의 모범이야. 좋은 아내다.<end>"

## 1680085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、そこまでされると<line-break>かえって、人は勘ぐるものでな。<line-break>特にファーロスの雌狐の料理とあれば。<end>"
EN: "But when one goes to such lengths, it only invites suspicion―especially when the food was prepared by the Vixen of Pharos.<end>"
KO: "하지만 그렇게까지 하면 오히려 사람은 의심하게 마련이지. 특히 파로스의 여우가 만든 요리라면 더더욱.<end>"

## 1680086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "毒をお懸念でしたら、<line-break>私が陛下の毒味をつとめましょう。<end>"
EN: "If Your Majesty fears poison, I shall taste the food for you.<end>"
KO: "독이 염려되신다면 제가 폐하의 기미를 맡겠습니다.<end>"

## 1680087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふっ、戯れ言よ。<line-break>余にはまだ利用価値がある。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>ティアナとゼネテスが結婚するまでは<line-break>余に死なれては困るであろうからな。<line-break>…毒など考えられぬことじゃ。<end>"
EN: "Hmph. I was only jesting. I still have my uses. Until Tiana and Xenetes are married, my death would be most inconvenient for you. Poison is quite out of the question.<end>"
KO: "흥, 헛소리다. 짐은 아직 이용 가치가 있다. 티아나와 제네테스가 결혼할 때까지는 짐이 죽으면 곤란할 테니 말이지. …독 같은 건 생각할 수 없는 일이다.<end>"

## 1680088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、料理を運ばせましょう。<end>"
EN: "Then I shall have the meal brought in.<end>"
KO: "그럼 요리를 내오게 하죠.<end>"

## 1680089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それには及ばぬ。食欲がないのだ。<line-break>王妃よ。これ以上、用がないのなら<line-break>余は退出してもよいかな。<end>"
EN: "There is no need. I have no appetite. My queen, if you have no further business with me, may I withdraw?<end>"
KO: "그럴 필요 없다. 식욕이 없구나. 왕비여, 더 볼일이 없다면 짐은 물러가도 되겠느냐.<end>"

## 1680090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…よろしゅうございます、陛下。<end>"
EN: "...Very well, Your Majesty.<end>"
KO: "…좋습니다, 폐하.<end>"

## 1680091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふふ、下がれでよい。<line-break>王妃がこのロストールの主<line-break>なのだからな。ははは…。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. You may simply order me to leave. After all, it is the queen who rules Rostorl. Ha ha ha...<end>"
KO: "후후후, 물러가라 해도 된다. 왕비가 이 로스톨의 주인이니까. 하하하….<end>"

## 1680092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "遠慮することはない。<line-break>入るがよい。<end>"
EN: "There is no need to stand on ceremony. Come in.<end>"
KO: "사양할 것 없다. 들어오너라.<end>"

## 1680093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気にすることはない。<line-break>…慣れた。<line-break>　<line-break>…さすがに嫁いですぐの時は<line-break>つらかったがな。<end>"
EN: "Pay it no mind. I am used to it now. Though I admit, it was painful just after I married.<end>"
KO: "신경 쓸 것 없다. …익숙해졌다. …그래도 시집온 직후엔 힘들었지만.<end>"

## 1680094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、事情はわかっていると思う。　　<line-break>残り物ですまぬが、<line-break>料理を食べてくれぬか？<line-break>食べ物を捨てられぬ性分でな。<line-break>ひとりで食べてもよいが<line-break>それは、さすがにさびしい。<end>"
EN: "Heh. I expect you understand what happened. Forgive me for offering you leftovers, but would you share the meal with me? I cannot bear to waste food. I could eat it alone, but that would be rather lonely.<end>"
KO: "후후, 사정은 알고 있으리라 생각한다. 남은 음식이라 미안하지만 요리를 먹어 주겠나? 음식을 버리지 못하는 성격이라서 말이지. 혼자 먹어도 되지만 그건 역시 쓸쓸하군.<end>"

## 1680095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は料理には自信があるのだ。<line-break>娘時分に、将来の夫のためと<line-break>みっちりと仕込まれたのでな。<line-break>裁縫も機織りも一生懸命覚えた。<line-break>意外であろう？<end>"
EN: "I am quite confident in my cooking. As a girl, I was thoroughly trained in it for the sake of my future husband. I also worked hard to learn sewing and weaving. Surprising, is it not?<end>"
KO: "나는 요리에 자신이 있다. 처녀 시절 미래의 남편을 위해 철저히 배웠거든. 바느질도 베 짜기도 열심히 익혔다. 의외지?<end>"

## 1680096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ、やさしいのだな。<line-break>それならば、ともに食べてくれるか？<line-break>ファーロスの雌狐の料理を。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh. How kind of you. Then will you dine with me and sample the Vixen of Pharos's cooking?<end>"
KO: "후후후, 상냥하구나. 그렇다면 함께 먹어 주겠나? 파로스의 여우가 만든 요리를.<end>"

## 1680097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "礼を言う。<line-break>さっそく料理を運ばせよう。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. I shall have the meal brought in at once.<end>"
KO: "고맙다. 바로 요리를 내오게 하지.<end>"

## 1680098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今日は…すまぬな。<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "About today... I'm sorry, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "오늘은… 미안하군, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1680099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よ、<value:$28>じゃねえか。<line-break>元気かい？<end>"
EN: "Hey, if it isn't <value:$28>. How've you been?<end>"
KO: "여어, <value:$28> 아니야. 잘 지냈어?<end>"

## 1680100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "普段はこっちじゃ飲まないんだが<line-break>料理がイケてるんでな。<line-break>たまに来てるんだ。<line-break>料理っていやあ、実はエリスの叔母貴は<line-break>かなり、料理がうまいんだぜ。<line-break>意外だろ？<end>"
EN: "I don't usually drink here, but the food's good, so I stop by now and then. Speaking of food, Aunt Eris is actually one hell of a cook. Surprised?<end>"
KO: "평소엔 이쪽에서 안 마시지만 요리가 괜찮아서 가끔 와. 요리 이야기가 나와서 말인데 에리스 숙모는 꽤 요리를 잘해. 의외지?<end>"

## 1680101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "叔母貴の料理を食う機会があってさ。<line-break>あんまり、うまいんでな。<line-break>俺は叔母貴の分まで食っちまったよ。<end>"
EN: "I had a chance to try her cooking once. It was so good, I ended up eating her share too.<end>"
KO: "숙모 요리를 먹을 기회가 있었는데 말이야. 너무 맛있어서 숙모 몫까지 먹어 버렸어.<end>"

## 1680102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec168-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0ま、なんだ。結婚したら、<line-break>嫁さんの作るもんをうまいうまいって<line-break>食ってやれる男になれよ。<line-break>なんでって…、<line-break>ま、なんでもだ。<end>ま、なんだ。お前さんも<line-break>誰かを好きになるだろうけれど<line-break>好きになる相手は考えなくちゃな。<line-break>失敗すると、つらいと思うぜ。<line-break>ま、恋愛ってのは、そんなに<line-break>割り切れたもんでもないんだろうがな。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Well, when you get married, be the kind of man who eats whatever his wife makes and tells her how good it is. Why? Well... never mind.<end>Well, you'll fall for someone someday, but you'd better think carefully about who. Choose badly and it'll hurt. Not that love's something you can reason out so neatly, I suppose.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0뭐랄까. 결혼하면 아내가 해 준 음식을 맛있다 맛있다 하면서 먹어 줄 수 있는 남자가 돼라. 왜냐고… 뭐, 그냥 그렇다.<end>뭐랄까. 너도 누군가를 좋아하게 되겠지만 좋아할 상대는 잘 생각해야 해. 잘못 고르면 괴로울 거다. 뭐, 연애란 게 그렇게 딱 잘라 말할 수 있는 것도 아니겠지만.<end>"

## 1680103

## 1690001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec169-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "新しい領主様は、<line-break>どのようなお方なのだろう…。<line-break>一度も見たことがないのだよ。　　<end>"
EN: "I wonder what our new ruler is like... I've never even caught a glimpse.<end>"
KO: "새 영주님은 어떤 분일까…. 한 번도 본 적이 없어.<end>"

## 1690002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec169-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "悪代官のボルボラがいなくなって、<line-break>この町にも平和が戻りました。<line-break>みんなで勝ちとった平和です。<end>"
EN: "With that corrupt magistrate Bolbora gone, peace has returned to our town. We all won this peace together.<end>"
KO: "악덕 대관 볼보라가 사라지고 이 마을에도 평화가 돌아왔습니다. 모두가 함께 쟁취한 평화입니다.<end>"

## 1690003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec169-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "新しい領主様は、<line-break>特に何もしてくださらないけど<line-break>領民を苦しめたりしないからいいわ。　<end>"
EN: "Our new lord may not do much for us, but at least the people aren't made to suffer.<end>"
KO: "새 영주님은 특별히 해 주는 건 없지만 영민을 괴롭히지는 않으니 좋아.<end>"

## 1690004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec169-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "新たなノーブルはいかがかしら。<line-break>ボルボラに怯えながら暮らしていたのが<line-break>ウソみたいでしょう？<end>"
EN: "How do you like the new Noble? It's hard to believe we used to live in fear of Bolbora, isn't it?<end>"
KO: "새로운 노블은 어때요? 볼보라를 두려워하며 살던 게 거짓말 같죠?<end>"

## 1690005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec169-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ボルボラがいなくなったから、<line-break>パパもママも笑うようになったんだ。<line-break>だから毎日たのしーんだ。<line-break>今日もいい子になったリルビーに<line-break>遊んでもらおっと！<end>"
EN: "Now that Bolbora's gone, Mom and Dad smile again. That means every day's so much fun! And today, I'll have that Lilubee, who's a good kid now, play with me again!<end>"
KO: "볼보라가 없어져서 아빠도 엄마도 웃게 됐어. 그래서 매일 즐거워. 오늘도 착한 아이가 된 릴비랑 놀아야지!<end>"

## 1690006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec169-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ボルボラがいなくなったから、<line-break>よその人と話してもいいって<line-break>ママが言ったんだ。だ・か・ら…　　<line-break>ノーブルの町へ<line-break>ようこそ！<line-break>…なんちゃって。<end>"
EN: "Now that Bolbora's gone, Mom says I'm allowed to talk to strangers. Sooo... welcome to the town of Noble! Just kidding.<end>"
KO: "볼보라가 없어졌으니 다른 사람이랑 이야기해도 된다고 엄마가 그랬어. 그·러·니… 노블 마을에 어서 오세요! …농담이야.<end>"

## 1690007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec169-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぼくはボルボラの手下として、<line-break>町の人にひどいことをしてました。<line-break>今はとーっても深く反省してますです。<line-break>世の中に悪が栄えたためしなしって<line-break>言いますが、本当なんですね。<end>"
EN: "I did terrible things to the townspeople as one of Bolbora's lackeys. I'm ever so very deeply sorry now, yes I am. They say evil never prospers, and it really is true.<end>"
KO: "저는 볼보라의 부하로서 마을 사람들에게 나쁜 짓을 했었습니다. 지금은 아~주 깊이 반성하고 있습니다. 세상에 악이 번성한 적은 없다고들 하는데 정말이네요.<end>"

## 1690008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec169-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えへへへ…、ノーブルはいい町でしょ。<line-break>悪い代官ボルボラがいなくなったから<line-break>ボクいい子になりました。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh... Noble's a nice town, isn't it? Now that the wicked magistrate Bolbora is gone, I'm a good kid.<end>"
KO: "에헤헤… 노블은 좋은 마을이지. 나쁜 대관 볼보라가 없어졌으니까 나 착한 아이 됐어.<end>"

## 1690009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec169-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ボルボラのかわりに新しい領主様が<line-break>決まってね、あたしはどんな人か<line-break>知らないけど、悪い人じゃないんだろうね。　<end>"
EN: "A new lord's been chosen to replace Bolbora. I don't know what sort of person was chosen, but probably no one bad.<end>"
KO: "볼보라 대신 새 영주님이 정해졌대. 난 어떤 사람인지 모르지만 나쁜 사람은 아니겠지.<end>"

## 1690010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec169-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノーブルはね、長いことボルボラの悪行に　　<line-break>苦しめられてきたんだよ。<line-break>でも、今は平和ないい町さね。<line-break>この平和は、あたしたちがボルボラを<line-break>追い出すことによって勝ち取ったんだよ。<line-break>もっともレムオン様の手助けもあったけどね。<end>"
EN: "Noble suffered for years because of all the terrible things Bolbora did. But now it's a good, peaceful town. We won this peace by driving Bolbora out ourselves. Mind you, we did have Lemghon's help.<end>"
KO: "노블은 오랫동안 볼보라의 악행에 시달려 왔어. 하지만 지금은 평화로운 좋은 도시지. 이 평화는 우리가 볼보라를 쫓아내며 얻어 낸 거야. 물론 레무온 님의 도움도 있었지만.<end>"

## 1690011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec169-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あたし、逃げ回ってるわけじゃないの。<line-break>今までがまんしてたから、いろんな人と<line-break>お話しできるのがうれしくて、うれしくて…。<line-break>あなたも外の世界のできごとや<line-break>あたしの知らないことをたくさん教えてね。<end>"
EN: "I'm not running away. I've been holding back all this time, so being able to talk to all kinds of people makes me so, so happy... Tell me lots of things too, okay? About what's happening in the outside world, and all the things I don't know.<end>"
KO: "나 도망다니는 게 아니야. 지금까지 참아왔으니까 여러 사람과 이야기할 수 있는 게 너무 기쁘고 또 기뻐서…. 너도 바깥세상에서 있었던 일이나 내가 모르는 걸 많이 가르쳐 줘.<end>"

## 1690012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec169-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう、ビクビクしなくていいの。　　　　　　<line-break>だから、話しかけられないように<line-break>逃げ回らなくていいんだわ。<end>"
EN: "I don't have to be afraid anymore. So I don't have to keep running away to stop people from talking to me.<end>"
KO: "이제 겁먹지 않아도 돼. 그러니 누가 말을 걸까 봐 도망다니지 않아도 되는 거야.<end>"

## 1690013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec169-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "悪名高いボルボラに代わって、<line-break>この地を治めることになった<line-break>ノーブル伯のことはご存知ですかな？　　<line-break>ロストールで聞いた噂では、<line-break>先代エリエナイ公の隠し子とか…。<line-break>あくまで噂ですがね。<end>"
EN: "Have you heard of the Count of Noble, who has taken over the rule of these lands from the notorious Bolbora? Rumor in Rostorl has it that the count is an illegitimate child of the previous Duke Elienai... But it is only a rumor, of course.<end>"
KO: "악명 높던 볼보라 대신 이 땅을 다스리게 된 노블 백작을 아십니까? 로스톨에서 들은 소문으로는 선대 에리에나이 공의 숨겨진 자식이라던데…. 어디까지나 소문입니다만.<end>"

## 1690014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec169-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ボルボラがいなくなって、<line-break>この町もずいぶん活気が出てきましたな。<line-break>昔は雰囲気が暗い町だったのですよ。<line-break>今は昔の悪夢を振り払うみたいに、<line-break>明るくなっています。<end>"
EN: "With Bolbora gone, this town has really come alive. It used to be such a gloomy place. Now it seems to be brightening up, as though it were shaking off the nightmare of the past.<end>"
KO: "볼보라가 사라지고 이 마을도 제법 활기를 되찾았습니다. 예전엔 분위기가 어두운 마을이었죠. 지금은 옛 악몽을 떨쳐 내듯 밝아지고 있습니다.<end>"

## 1690015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec169-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの日、町の奴らは、<line-break>ボルボラに立ち向かって行ったが　　　　　<line-break>俺はできなかった…。<line-break>もちろん爺さんの意見が正しいと<line-break>思ったのも事実だ！<line-break>でも、ほんとうは勇気がないだけなんだ…。<end>"
EN: "That day, the townspeople went to face Bolbora, but I couldn't... It's true―I did think my grandfather was right! But the truth is, I just didn't have the courage...<end>"
KO: "그날 마을 사람들은 볼보라에게 맞섰지만 나는 그러지 못했어…. 물론 할아버지 의견이 옳다고 생각한 것도 사실이야! 하지만 사실은 용기가 없었던 것뿐이야….<end>"

## 1690016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec169-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんた冒険者かい…。<line-break>俺をひとりにしておいてくれよ。　　　　　<line-break>頼むよ…。<end>"
EN: "Are you an adventurer...? Just leave me alone. Please...<end>"
KO: "당신 모험자인가…. 날 혼자 내버려 둬. 부탁이야….<end>"

## 1690017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec169-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "新しい領主様がボルボラのような　　　<line-break>悪人でも、我々は負けませんよ。<line-break>もう泣き寝入りはしませんから。<end>"
EN: "Even if our new lord is as wicked as Bolbora, we won't be beaten. We won't suffer in silence anymore.<end>"
KO: "새 영주님이 볼보라 같은 악인이라 해도 우리는 지지 않을 겁니다. 이제는 울며 참지 않을 거니까요.<end>"

## 1690018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec169-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう、ボルボラのモンスターに<line-break>怯える必要もありません。<line-break>重税をとられ苦しむこともありません。<line-break>我々の勇気と団結力が勝利したのです。<line-break>ああ、言いたいことを言える生活、<line-break>なんとすばらしいものなのか！<end>"
EN: "We no longer have to fear Bolbora's monsters, or suffer under crushing taxes. Our courage and unity have won the day. Ah, how wonderful it is to live where we can speak our minds!<end>"
KO: "이제 보르보라의 몬스터를 두려워할 필요도 없습니다. 무거운 세금에 시달릴 일도 없습니다. 우리의 용기와 단결력이 승리한 것입니다. 아아, 하고 싶은 말을 할 수 있는 생활이라니 얼마나 훌륭한가!<end>"

## 1690019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec169-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これはこれはご主人様、<line-break>お帰りなさいませ。<line-break>さ、さ、どうぞ、お入りください。<end>"
EN: "Why, Master! Welcome home. Please, come right in.<end>"
KO: "아이고 주인님, 돌아오셨습니까. 자자, 어서 들어오십시오.<end>"

## 1700001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec170-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "新しい領主様が決まったそうだよ。<line-break>どうだ、<value:$28>。<line-break>これを機に町へ戻っては？<line-break>新しい領主様も、今さらお前の罪を<line-break>問うことはないと思うぞ。<end>"
EN: "I hear they've chosen a new lord. What do you say, <value:$28>? Why not take this chance to come back to town? I don't imagine the new lord will hold you to account for your crime after all this time.<end>"
KO: "새 영주님이 정해졌대. 어때, <value:$28>. 이참에 마을로 돌아오는 건? 새 영주님도 이제 와서 네 죄를 묻진 않을 것 같은데.<end>"

## 1700002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec170-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、まさか、<value:$28>！<line-break>お前、処刑されたんじゃ…。<line-break>私は悪い夢でも見ているのか？<end>"
EN: "I-it can't be, <value:$28>! Weren't you executed...? Am I having some kind of nightmare?<end>"
KO: "서, 설마, <value:$28>! 너, 처형된 게 아니었나…. 내가 악몽이라도 꾸는 건가?<end>"

## 1700003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec170-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>は今、<line-break>冒険者をしているんですってね。　　　<line-break>チャカもそうなの？<line-break>長老様は文句を言うかもしれないけど、<line-break>たいていの人は、あなたたち姉弟が<line-break>帰ってきてくれるのを待っているのよ。<end>"
EN: "I hear you're an adventurer now, <value:$28>. Is Chaka one too? The elder may object, but most people are waiting for you and your brother to come home.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>은 지금 모험자를 하고 있다면서? 차카도 그래? 장로님은 불평하실지도 모르지만, 대부분의 사람들은 너희 남매가 돌아오길 기다리고 있어.<end>"

## 1700004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec170-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ、<value:$28>…。　　　　　<line-break>こうして平和な町になったのも<line-break>あなたたち姉弟のおかげよ。<line-break>あなたたちが立ち上がらなかったら、<line-break>今でも怯えながら生きていたと思うわ。<line-break>本当にありがとうね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>... This town is at peace now because of you and your brother. If you two hadn't taken a stand, I think we'd still be living in fear. Thank you, truly.<end>"
KO: "어머, <value:$28>…. 이렇게 평화로운 마을이 된 것도 너희 남매 덕분이야. 너희가 나서지 않았다면 지금도 두려움에 떨며 살고 있었을 거야. 정말 고마워.<end>"

## 1700005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec170-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あっ！　<value:$28>お姉ちゃん！<line-break>ぼくね、チャカお兄ちゃんとの約束を守って　<line-break>きちんとパパのお手伝いをしてるよ。<line-break>だから、チャカお兄ちゃんに<line-break>コリンはいい子にしてるって言っておいてね。<line-break>やくそくだよ！<end>"
EN: "Oh! Big Sis <value:$28>! I kept my promise to Chaka. I've been helping Papa, just like I said I would. So tell Chaka that Colin's been a good boy, okay? Promise!<end>"
KO: "앗! <value:$28> 누나! 나 있지, 차카 형과 한 약속을 지켜서 제대로 아빠 일을 돕고 있어. 그러니까 차카 형에게 코린은 착하게 지내고 있다고 말해 줘. 약속이야!<end>"

## 1700006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec170-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あっ！　<value:$28>お姉ちゃん！<line-break>ず～っと、どこに行ってたの？<line-break>チャカお兄ちゃんも元気にしてる？<end>"
EN: "Oh! Big Sis <value:$28>! Where have you been all this time? Is Chaka doing well too?<end>"
KO: "아! <value:$28> 언니! 계속 어디 갔었어? 차카 오빠도 잘 지내?<end>"

## 1700007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec170-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こ、こ、こ、これは<value:$28>様！<line-break>へへへ…今日もお美しいですね。<line-break>も、もちろん悪いことなんてしてません。<line-break>みなさんのお手伝いをしたり、<line-break>子供と遊んだりしてます。<line-break>もう悪い子じゃないですから。<end>"
EN: "O-oh! <value:$28>! Heh heh... You're looking lovely as ever today. I-I haven't been doing anything bad, honest. I've been helping everyone out and playing with the children. I'm not a bad kid anymore.<end>"
KO: "이, 이, 이건 <value:$28> 님! 헤헤헤… 오늘도 아름다우시네요. 무, 물론 나쁜 짓 같은 건 안 했습니다. 여러분을 돕거나 아이들과 놀아주고 있어요. 이제 나쁜 아이가 아니니까요.<end>"

## 1700008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec170-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あ、あ、あなた様は！<line-break>数々の無礼をお許しください。<line-break>もう弱い者いじめなんてしません。<line-break>町の人たちにもいっしょうけんめい<line-break>あやまって許してもらいました。<line-break>だから…許してください。<end>"
EN: "Y-y-you! Please forgive me for all the disrespect I've shown you. I don't pick on people weaker than me anymore. I did my very best to apologize to everyone in town, and they forgave me. So... please forgive me too.<end>"
KO: "다, 당신은! 그동안의 무례를 용서해 주세요. 이제 약한 사람을 괴롭히지 않겠습니다. 마을 사람들에게도 열심히 사과해서 용서받았어요. 그러니까… 용서해 주세요.<end>"

## 1700010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec170-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もしかして、<value:$28>かい？<line-break>姿を見せないと思ったら　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>冒険者になってたのだねえ。<line-break>あんたたちのおかげで町は平和になったよ。<line-break>それなのに長老様は、あんたたちは死んだって<line-break>言ったんだよ。ひどい話じゃないか。<end>"
EN: "Is that you, <value:$28>? I was wondering why you hadn't been around. So you've taken up adventuring. Thanks to all of you, the town's peaceful now. And yet the elder said you'd all died. Isn't that awful?<end>"
KO: "혹시 <value:$28>이니? 안 보인다 했더니 모험자가 되었구나. 너희 덕분에 도시는 평화로워졌어. 그런데도 장로님은 너희가 죽었다고 했단다. 너무한 얘기 아니니.<end>"

## 1700011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec170-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう、誰とでも自由にお話できるし、<line-break>こわいモンスターもいないのね。<line-break>とーっても、うれしいの！<end>"
EN: "Now I can talk to anyone I want, and there aren't any scary monsters. I'm so, so happy!<end>"
KO: "이제 누구와도 자유롭게 이야기할 수 있고 무서운 몬스터도 없네. 정말 정말 기뻐!<end>"

## 1700012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec170-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お姉ちゃんたちのおかげで、<line-break>ボルボラはいなくなったんでしょ。<line-break>お姉ちゃん、ありがとう。<end>"
EN: "Bolbora's gone thanks to you and the others, right? Thank you so much!<end>"
KO: "언니들 덕분에 볼보라가 없어진 거지? 언니, 고마워.<end>"

## 1700013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec170-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "新しい領主はエリエナイ公の妹君とか。　　<line-break>前領主のボルボラのような<line-break>ひどい人間でなければよいのですが。<line-break>ボルボラを代官に任命したのも<line-break>エリエナイ公ですし、あの方の人を見る目は<line-break>あまり信用できませんからね。<end>"
EN: "I hear the new ruler is Duke Elienai's younger sister. I only hope she isn't as dreadful as the former ruler, Bolbora. Then again, it was Duke Elienai who appointed Bolbora as governor, so I don't put much faith in the duke's judgment of character.<end>"
KO: "새 영주는 엘리에나이 공의 여동생이라던데. 전 영주 볼보라 같은 지독한 인간만 아니면 좋겠습니다. 볼보라를 대관으로 임명한 것도 엘리에나이 공이니, 그분의 사람 보는 눈은 썩 믿기 어렵거든요.<end>"

## 1700014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec170-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなた、以前、<line-break>この町に住んでおられましたな。　　<line-break>見たことがありますよ。<line-break>今は冒険者ですか。<line-break>どうです、生まれ故郷の変わり様は。<line-break>驚いたでしょう。<end>"
EN: "You used to live in this town, didn't you? I remember seeing you before. Are you an adventurer now? What do you make of how your hometown has changed? It must have been quite a shock.<end>"
KO: "당신, 예전에 이 마을에 살았지요. 본 적이 있습니다. 지금은 모험자입니까. 어떻습니까, 고향이 달라진 모습은. 놀랐겠지요.<end>"

## 1700015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec170-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺は恐かったんだ…。<line-break>ボルボラも、奴のモンスターも…<line-break>そして何より、<value:$28>！<line-break>お前が恐ろしかったんだ…<line-break>許してくれ…、頼む、頼むよ…。<end>"
EN: "I was afraid... of Bolbora, of his monsters... but most of all, of you, <value:$28>! You terrified me... Forgive me... Please, I'm begging you...<end>"
KO: "난 무서웠어…. 볼보라도, 놈의 몬스터도… 그리고 무엇보다 <value:$28>! 네가 무서웠어…. 용서해 줘… 부탁이야….<end>"

## 1700016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec170-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そんなバカな！<line-break>お前とチャカは反乱の責任をとって<line-break>処刑されたんじゃなかったのか！<line-break>あわわわ…<line-break>た、助けてくれっ！<line-break>頼む、頼む…。<end>"
EN: "Th-that's impossible! Weren't you and Chaka executed for the rebellion?! A-ah... H-help me! Please, I'm begging you...<end>"
KO: "그, 그럴 리가! 너와 차카는 반란 책임을 지고 처형된 거 아니었나! 아아… 사, 살려 줘! 부탁이야, 부탁해….<end>"

## 1700017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec170-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "以前、ここに住んでおられた方の<line-break>畑を耕しているのですが、<line-break>手入れが行き届いていて感心しています。<line-break>きっと、その方は畑も農作業も<line-break>愛しておられたのでしょう。<line-break>私も負けないように働きますぞ。<end>"
EN: "I've been tending the field left by the person who lived here before, and I'm impressed by how well it was cared for. Whoever it was must have truly loved the land and the work. I'll do my best to live up to that.<end>"
KO: "전에 이곳에 사셨던 분의 밭을 갈고 있는데 손질이 아주 잘 되어 있어 감탄했습니다. 분명 그분은 밭도 농사일도 사랑하셨겠지요. 저도 지지 않도록 열심히 하겠습니다.<end>"

## 1700018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec170-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私はボルボラがいなくなってから　<line-break>引っ越してきましたので、<line-break>事情をよく知らないのですよ。<line-break>この家も、私の畑も、誰か別の人が<line-break>使っていたそうですね。<end>"
EN: "I only moved here after Bolbora was gone, so I don't know much about the circumstances. Apparently, someone else used to live in this house and work what is now my field.<end>"
KO: "저는 볼보라가 사라진 뒤 이사 와서 사정을 잘 모릅니다. 이 집도 제 밭도 예전엔 다른 사람이 쓰던 곳이라더군요.<end>"
