## 1710001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待ちやがれ！<end>"
EN: "Hold it right there!<end>"
KO: "기다려, 이 자식!<end>"

## 1710002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ディンガルの敗残兵だな？<line-break>貴様らの首をロストールに売れば<line-break>恩賞にあずかれる。<end>"
EN: "You're defeated soldiers from Dyneskal, aren't you? We can claim a reward from Rostorl for your heads.<end>"
KO: "딩갈의 패잔병이군? 네놈들 목을 로스톨에 팔면 포상을 받을 수 있겠지.<end>"

## 1710003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すまねえが、死んでもらうぜ。<end>"
EN: "Sorry, but you're going to have to die.<end>"
KO: "미안하지만 죽어 줘야겠어.<end>"

## 1710004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くそっ！　やるじゃねえか。<line-break>こうなったら本気でいくぜ！<end>"
EN: "Damn! You're pretty good. Fine, no more holding back!<end>"
KO: "젠장! 제법이잖아. 이렇게 된 이상 진심으로 간다!<end>"

## 1710005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、まずいぞ。<line-break>逃げるんだ！<end>"
EN: "Th-this is bad. Run!<end>"
KO: "이, 이건 위험해. 도망쳐!<end>"

## 1710006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え？　え？　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>うわっ！？<line-break>待ってくれ～！<end>"
EN: "Huh? What? Aagh! Wait for me!<end>"
KO: "어? 어? 우왓!? 기다려 줘~!<end>"

## 1710007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そなた、強いな。<end>"
EN: "You are strong.<end>"
KO: "그대, 강하군.<end>"

## 1710008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "奴らを捕らえよ。<end>"
EN: "Seize them.<end>"
KO: "놈들을 붙잡아라.<end>"

## 1710009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ははっ！<end>"
EN: "Yes, sire!<end>"
KO: "하핫!<end>"

## 1710010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "余は、ドワーフ王国の国王ジンガ。<line-break>そなたの戦いぶり、見せてもらった。<line-break>見事。<end>"
EN: "I am Jinga, King of the Dwarf Kingdom. I witnessed your skill in battle. Most impressive.<end>"
KO: "나는 드워프 왕국의 국왕 징가다. 네 싸움 솜씨, 잘 봤다. 훌륭하군.<end>"

## 1710011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "敗残兵を保護しろと？　よかろう。<line-break>戦争を持ちこまれるのは許せんが、<line-break>落ち武者狩りを見逃すわけにはいかん。<line-break>これからは見張りの目を厳しくして、<line-break>二度とあのような真似はさせん。<line-break>それでよいかな？<end>"
EN: "You ask me to protect the defeated soldiers? Very well. I will not allow war to be brought here, but neither can I turn a blind eye to the hunting down of defeated soldiers. I shall strengthen the watch and see that nothing of the sort happens again. Will that satisfy you?<end>"
KO: "패잔병을 보호하라고? 좋다. 전쟁을 끌고 오는 건 용납 못 하지만 패잔병 사냥을 못 본 척할 순 없지. 앞으로 감시를 강화해 다시는 그런 짓을 못 하게 하겠다. 그걸로 되겠나?<end>"

## 1710012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クッ…フワッハッハッハ！<line-break>このドワーフ王に対して、臆せずに<line-break>敗残兵の保護を言いつけるとはな…。<line-break>強き人間よ、名を名乗れ。<line-break>…その姿、兵卒ではなさそうだが？<end>"
EN: "Heh... Bwahahahaha! To order the Dwarf King, without a trace of fear, to protect defeated soldiers... Strong human, state your name. Judging by your appearance, you're no foot soldier, are you?<end>"
KO: "큭… 후와하하하! 이 드워프 왕을 상대로 겁도 없이 패잔병 보호를 명하다니…. 강한 인간이여, 이름을 대라. …그 모습, 병졸은 아닌 듯한데?<end>"

## 1710013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なるほど、冒険者か。<line-break>では、さらばだ<value:$28>。<line-break>旅の無事を祈っておるぞ。<end>"
EN: "Ah, an adventurer. Then farewell, <value:$28>. May your journey be a safe one.<end>"
KO: "그렇군, 모험자인가. 그럼 잘 가거라, <value:$28>. 여행길의 무사를 빌겠다.<end>"

## 1710014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "聞けば、お主がドワーフ王に<line-break>敗残兵の保護を願い出てくれた<line-break>そうではないか。<line-break>お主のおかげで、多くの兵が<line-break>安全に逃げ延びることができた。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "I hear you asked the Dwarf King to grant refuge to our defeated soldiers. Thanks to you, many of them escaped to safety.<end>"
KO: "듣자 하니 자네가 드워프 왕에게 패잔병 보호를 부탁했다더군. 자네 덕분에 많은 병사가 무사히 도망칠 수 있었다.<end>"

## 1710015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "兵に代わって礼をいう。<line-break>この通りじゃ。<end>"
EN: "On behalf of those soldiers, you have my deepest thanks.<end>"
KO: "병사들을 대신해 감사를 표하네. 이렇게 부탁하네.<end>"

## 1710016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ドワーフの王国内でも、かなりの数の<line-break>落ち武者狩りが出たらしい。<line-break>多くの兵の命を、失ってしもうた…。<end>"
EN: "It seems quite a few people took to hunting down routed soldiers, even within the Dwarf Kingdom. We lost many soldiers...<end>"
KO: "드워프 왕국 안에서도 꽤 많은 패잔병 사냥이 있었던 모양이야. 많은 병사들의 목숨을 잃었지….<end>"

## 1710017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、ベルゼーヴァ卿が<line-break>わしを叱責しようとお待ちじゃ。<line-break>すまぬが、ついてきてくれぬか。<line-break>わしにとってはお主は、<line-break>まぁ孫のようなものよ。<line-break>それが保険になるからの。<end>"
EN: "Now then, Lord Beelzeva is waiting to give me a proper dressing-down. Sorry to ask, but would you come with me? You're rather like a grandchild to me, you know. And that should serve as a bit of insurance.<end>"
KO: "자, 벨제바 경이 나를 꾸짖으려고 기다리고 있네. 미안하지만 함께 가 주지 않겠나. 내게 자네는 뭐랄까, 손자 같은 존재라네. 그게 보험이 되어 줄 테니까.<end>"

## 1710018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アキュリュースの船着き場に<line-break>女性がいるはずだ。<line-break>彼女に話しかけてこう言うのだ。　<line-break>帝国はあの陽より輝く、とな。<line-break>あとはその女性が案内してくれる。<line-break>では、しばしさらばだ。<end>"
EN: "There should be a woman at Aqyurius Wharf. Speak to her and say, “The Empire shines brighter than that sun.” She will guide you from there. Until we meet again, farewell.<end>"
KO: "아큐류스 선착장에 여자가 있을 거다. 그 여자에게 말을 걸고 이렇게 말해라. '제국은 저 태양보다 빛난다.' 그러면 그 여자가 안내해 줄 거야. 그럼 잠시 작별이다.<end>"

## 1710019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "朱雀将軍アンギルダン。<line-break>…君には失望させられた。<end>"
EN: "Vermilion Bird General Angeerdan. You have disappointed me.<end>"
KO: "주작장군 앙길단. …그대에게는 실망했다.<end>"

## 1710020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この戦につぎ込んだ時間と予算と物資。<line-break>君はそれらすべてを無にしたのだ。<line-break>言いわけはあるかね？<end>"
EN: "All the time, funds, and supplies committed to this war―you have brought them all to nothing. Have you any excuse?<end>"
KO: "이 전쟁에 쏟아부은 시간과 예산과 물자. 자네는 그 모든 것을 물거품으로 만들었네. 변명할 말이 있나?<end>"

## 1710021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "かつては赤い巨星の異名をとる名将。<line-break>その名声にのぼせ上がって<line-break>いささか、調子にのりすぎたのでは？ <line-break>日頃から、規則を無視する<line-break>おごりたかぶった振る舞い。<line-break>その結果がこれですか、朱雀将軍？<end>"
EN: "Once, you were a renowned commander known as the Red Giant Star. Has that fame gone to your head? Perhaps you grew rather too sure of yourself. You have always behaved with arrogance, flouting the rules―and this is the result, Vermilion Bird General?<end>"
KO: "한때는 붉은 거성이라 불리던 명장. 그 명성에 취해 조금 너무 우쭐해진 것 아닙니까? 평소부터 규칙을 무시하는 오만한 행동. 그 결과가 이겁니까, 주작장군?<end>"

## 1710022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッ…、落ち着いたものだな。<line-break>さすがは朱雀将軍とでも言うべきか。<line-break>それとも、恥知らずなだけか…。<line-break>だが、あれほどの失態を演じておいて<line-break>このままその地位にあり続けようと<line-break>思うほど恥知らずでもあるまい。<end>"
EN: "Hmph. Quite composed, aren't you? Perhaps that is only to be expected of the Vermilion Bird General. Or perhaps you simply have no shame. Still, even you cannot be shameless enough to expect to retain your position after a blunder of this magnitude.<end>"
KO: "후… 침착하군. 역시 주작장군이라 해야 하나. 아니면 그저 뻔뻔한 건가…. 하지만 그토록 큰 실태를 저질러 놓고도 계속 그 자리에 남으려 할 만큼 뻔뻔하진 않겠지.<end>"

## 1710023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…朱雀将軍アンギルダン・ゼイエン。<line-break>君を南方攻略軍司令官から解任する。<line-break>　<line-break>今後は、西方攻略軍司令、<line-break>白虎将軍ジラークの指揮下に<line-break>入ってもらう。<end>"
EN: "Vermilion Bird General Angeerdan Zeien. You are relieved of command of the Southern Campaign Army. From now on, you will serve under White Tiger General Zsilak, commander of the Western Campaign Army.<end>"
KO: "…주작장군 앙길단 제이엔. 그대를 남방 공략군 사령관에서 해임한다. 앞으로는 서방 공략군 사령, 백호장군 지라크의 지휘 아래들어가도록 하라.<end>"

## 1710024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君の犯した失敗からすれば、<line-break>寛大すぎるほどの処置だ。<line-break>まさか異存はあるまいな？<end>"
EN: "Given your failure, this is more lenient than you deserve. You'll accept it without objection, I presume?<end>"
KO: "네가 저지른 실수를 생각하면 지나치게 관대한 처분이다. 설마 이의는 없겠지?<end>"

## 1710025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はい。<end>"
EN: "Yes.<end>"
KO: "네.<end>"

## 1710026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今後はおとなしくして、　　　　　<line-break>規則に忠実にね。<line-break>アンギルダン将軍。<line-break>ジラーク将軍は、<line-break>規律に厳しいこと、この上ないわ。<line-break>せいぜい、励んでください。<end>"
EN: "From now on, behave yourself and follow the rules, General Angeerdan. General Zsilak is an exceedingly strict disciplinarian. Do try your best.<end>"
KO: "앞으로는 얌전히, 규칙에 충실하게 지내세요. 앙길단 장군. 지라크 장군은 규율에 엄격하기로는 둘째가라면 서러운 분이니까요. 부디 힘내세요.<end>"

## 1710027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…よし。下がっていい。<end>"
EN: "Very well. You may go.<end>"
KO: "…좋아. 물러가도 된다.<end>"

## 1710028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "長生きのコツはな、腹を立てんことよ。<line-break>わしは本来なら死刑よ。ただ、<line-break>わしには脱走の前科が２回あるでな。<line-break>若造め、あそこでわしを挑発して、<line-break>怒って奴に襲いかかったところで<line-break>バッサリという筋書きよ。<end>"
EN: "The secret to a long life is not losing your temper. By rights, I should have been sentenced to death. But I've escaped twice before, you see. That young whelp meant to provoke me into attacking him, then cut me down on the spot. That was the plan.<end>"
KO: "오래 사는 비결은 화내지 않는 게야. 나는 원래라면 사형이지. 다만 탈옥 전과가 두 번 있어서 말이야. 그 젊은 놈은 거기서 나를 도발하고, 내가 화가 나 달려들면 베어 버리겠다는 각본이지.<end>"

## 1710029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ヘタに死刑宣告をしてから逃げられて、<line-break>また他国で傭兵でもやられる前に、<line-break>わしを始末しようとしたわけじゃな。<line-break>ベルゼーヴァ宰相殿は<line-break>双剣の使い手。<line-break>しかも、魔法もかなりできる。<end>"
EN: "He wanted me disposed of before he could formally sentence me to death and risk my escaping again to serve as a mercenary in some other country. Chancellor Beelzeva is a master of twin swords, and no mean hand at magic either.<end>"
KO: "섣불리 사형선고를 했다가 도망쳐 다른 나라에서 또 용병이라도 하기 전에 나를 처리하려 한 거군. 벨제바 재상은 쌍검의 사용자. 게다가 마법도 상당히 잘한다.<end>"

## 1710030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ザギヴは魔道アカデミーを首席で卒業。<line-break>いにしえの強力な呪文も使える。<line-break>ふたり相手ではわしでも勝てぬわ。<line-break>だから、お主を連れていった。<line-break>わしは、お主のことだけを考えて、<line-break>やっとこらえておられた。<end>"
EN: "Zagiv graduated at the top of her class from the Academy of Magic and can cast powerful ancient spells. Even I couldn't defeat the two of them together. That's why I brought you along. Only by keeping you in mind did I manage to hold myself back.<end>"
KO: "자기브는 마도 아카데미를 수석으로 졸업했고 고대의 강력한 주문도 쓸 수 있다. 둘을 상대하면 나도 이기지 못해. 그래서 널 데려간 거다. 나는 네 생각만 하며 겨우 참을 수 있었어.<end>"

## 1710031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしが悟ったことを教えてやろう。　　<line-break>大事なことは生き延びることじゃ。<line-break>意地も何も生き延びてこそじゃ。<line-break>今日は、本当に感謝しとる。<line-break>おかげで命を拾うたわ。<end>"
EN: "Let me tell you what I've learned: what matters is staying alive. Pride and all the rest mean nothing unless you survive. I truly am grateful to you today. You saved my life.<end>"
KO: "내가 깨달은 걸 가르쳐 주마. 중요한 건 살아남는 것이다. 오기도 뭐도 살아남아야 의미가 있지. 오늘은 정말 고맙다. 덕분에 목숨을 건졌어.<end>"

## 1710033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おお、<value:$28>。<line-break>来たか。<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. You've come.<end>"
KO: "오, <value:$28>. 왔나.<end>"

## 1710034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こちらは？<end>"
EN: "And this is?<end>"
KO: "이쪽은?<end>"

## 1710035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしの戦友だ。<end>"
EN: "A comrade-in-arms of mine.<end>"
KO: "내 전우다.<end>"

## 1710036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ということは、<line-break>ここに入る権限はない。<end>"
EN: "Then your comrade has no authority to enter.<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 이곳에 들어올 권한은 없다.<end>"

## 1710037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんと？<end>"
EN: "What?<end>"
KO: "뭐라고?<end>"

## 1710038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そもそも卿からして、<line-break>ここに入る権限はないはずだが？<end>"
EN: "For that matter, you shouldn't even be allowed in here, should you?<end>"
KO: "애초에 경도 이곳에 들어올 권한은 없을 텐데?<end>"

## 1710039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジラーク、<line-break>わしは話があってきたのだ。<line-break>　<line-break>このままではいたずらに<line-break>兵を損じるばかりだぞ。<end>"
EN: "Zsilak, I came to speak with you. If this continues, we will only squander more soldiers' lives.<end>"
KO: "지라크, 나는 할 말이 있어서 왔다. 이대로는 공연히 병사만 잃게 된다.<end>"

## 1710040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "西方攻略の指揮は<line-break>私に一任されている。<line-break>余計な指図はやめていただきたい。<end>"
EN: "I have been entrusted with sole command of the western campaign. I would ask you to stop interfering.<end>"
KO: "서방 공략의 지휘는 나에게 일임되어 있다. 쓸데없는 지시는 삼가 줬으면 한다.<end>"

## 1710041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よ、余計じゃと…。<end>"
EN: "You... call that interference?<end>"
KO: "쓸, 쓸데없다고….<end>"

## 1710042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "卿は昔、ディンガルから脱走したあと、<line-break>アキュリュースの傭兵隊長を<line-break>していたそうではないか。<line-break>卿の策に<line-break>利敵行為がないとは言えぬ。<end>"
EN: "After deserting Dyneskal, you once served as an Aqyurius mercenary captain, did you not? I cannot rule out the possibility that your strategy may aid the enemy.<end>"
KO: "경은 예전에 딘갈에서 탈주한 뒤 아큐류스의 용병대장을 했다고 하지 않았나. 경의 계책에 적을 이롭게 하는 행위가 없다고는 할 수 없지.<end>"

## 1710043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "利敵行為じゃと？<line-break>見損なうな、ジラーク！<end>"
EN: "Aid the enemy? Do not misjudge me, Zsilak!<end>"
KO: "적을 이롭게 하는 짓이라고? 나를 얕보지 마라, 지라크!<end>"

## 1710044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "腹を立てるのは愚者の業だ、<line-break>アンギルダン卿。私はあくまで<line-break>可能性について言及しているだけだ。<line-break>また、軍の中にそう見る向きが<line-break>ないとも言えぬ。そうなると統制上<line-break>問題が生じることになる。<end>"
EN: "Anger is the province of fools, Lord Angeerdan. I am merely noting a possibility. Nor can I say that no one in the army shares that suspicion. That would create problems of discipline.<end>"
KO: "화를 내는 건 어리석은 자의 소행이오, 앙길단 경. 나는 어디까지나 가능성에 대해 언급하고 있을 뿐이오. 또한 군 안에 그렇게 보는 이가 전혀 없다고도 할 수 없소. 그렇게 되면 통제에 문제가 생기게 되지.<end>"

## 1710045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "強制手段をとる必要が生じるまえに、<line-break>お帰り願おう。<end>"
EN: "I must ask you to leave before force becomes necessary.<end>"
KO: "강제 수단을 써야 할 상황이 되기 전에 돌아가 주시오.<end>"

## 1710046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ。ジラークめ！<line-break>…なるほど、ベルゼーヴァ卿がわしを　<line-break>ジラークのもとへ回したわけじゃ。<line-break>さすがのわしでも、奴のそばにおると、<line-break>せっかく先日抑えた怒りが<line-break>また煮え上がって来おるわ。<end>"
EN: "Damn that Zsilak! So that's why Lord Beelzeva sent me to Zsilak. Even I can scarcely be near the man without the anger I only just mastered the other day boiling up all over again.<end>"
KO: "큭, 지라크 녀석! …과연 벨제바 경이 나를 지라크에게 보낸 이유가 있군. 아무리 나라도 녀석 곁에 있으면 지난번 겨우 눌러놓은 분노가 다시 끓어오른다.<end>"

## 1710047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジラークの方も、わしが奴の権限を<line-break>脅かすべく、ベルゼーヴァ卿が<line-break>遣わしたと思うて神経質になっておる。<line-break>東の死神カルラは、ジラークと違うて、<line-break>まあなんというか…食わせ者じゃ。<line-break>きっとわしと馬が合う。<end>"
EN: "Zsilak is on edge as well. He thinks Lord Beelzeva sent me to undermine his authority. But Karula, the Death God of the East, is nothing like Zsilak. How shall I put it... she's a wily one. I'm sure we'll get along.<end>"
KO: "지라크도 내가 자기 권한을 위협하려고 벨제바 경이 보낸 자라고 생각해 신경을 곤두세우고 있다. 동쪽의 사신 카를라는 지라크와 달리, 뭐랄까… 만만치 않은 인물이야. 분명 나와 잘 맞겠지.<end>"

## 1710048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしとカルラとが手を組めば<line-break>必ずや帝国に対し造反すると<line-break>ベルゼーヴァ卿は思うたのだろうよ。<line-break>人の和を信じずして<line-break>帝国に未来はないぞ、<line-break>ベルゼーヴァ卿！！<end>"
EN: "Lord Beelzeva must have thought that if Karula and I joined forces, we would surely rebel against the Empire. The Empire has no future if it puts no faith in concord among its people, Lord Beelzeva!<end>"
KO: "나와 칼라가 손을 잡으면 반드시 제국에 반기를 들 거라고 벨제바 경은 생각했겠지. 사람의 화합을 믿지 않고서 제국에 미래는 없다, 벨제바 경!!<end>"

## 1710049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おお、<value:$28>、よく来た。<line-break>今から、アキュリュース攻めらしいぞ。<line-break>　<line-break>無謀にもジラークは、<line-break>あのミズチの棲まう湖を<line-break>正面から突破する腹づもりのようじゃ。<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. Good of you to come. It seems the assault on Aqyurius is about to begin. Zsilak, in his recklessness, means to cross the lake where the Mizuchi dwells in a frontal attack.<end>"
KO: "오, <value:$28>, 잘 왔다. 이제 아큐류스 공략이 시작되는 모양이다. 무모하게도 지라크는 미즈치가 사는 호수를 정면 돌파할 생각인 듯하다.<end>"

## 1710050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何の策もなしにあの湖を渡るなど<line-break>正気の沙汰ではないが、敵の傭兵部隊は<line-break>正規の軍に比べて統率がとれておらぬ。<line-break>なんとかミズチがいる湖を越え<line-break>集団戦闘に持ち込めれば、<line-break>勝機をつかめるやもしれぬ。<end>"
EN: "Trying to cross that lake without a plan is sheer madness. But the enemy mercenaries lack the discipline of a regular army. If we can somehow cross the lake despite the Mizuchi and draw them into a pitched battle, we may yet have a chance.<end>"
KO: "아무 대책 없이 그 호수를 건너는 건 미친 짓이지만 적의 용병부대는 정규군보다 통제가 약하다. 어떻게든 미즈치가 있는 호수를 건너 집단전으로 끌고 간다면 승산이 있을지도 몰라.<end>"

## 1710051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "時間のようじゃ。<line-break>行くぞ！　<value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "Looks like it's time. Let's go, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "시간이 된 모양이군. 간다, <value:$28>!<end>"

## 1710052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やはりミズチの棲む湖を越えるのは<line-break>無理であったか。<line-break>この船ももう長くは持つまい…。<end>"
EN: "So crossing the Mizuchi's lake was beyond us after all. This ship will not hold together much longer...<end>"
KO: "역시 미즈치가 사는 호수를 건너는 건 무리였나. 이 배도 이제 오래 버티지 못하겠군….<end>"

## 1710053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>お主、泳ぎは得意か？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, are you a strong swimmer?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 자네 수영은 잘하나?<end>"

## 1710054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何をふるえておる！<line-break>生き延びるためじゃ、来い！<end>"
EN: "Why are you trembling? We must do this to survive. Come!<end>"
KO: "뭘 떨고 있나! 살아남으려면 와라!<end>"

## 1710055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぶふぅ～。<line-break>大丈夫か？　<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Hwoof... Are you all right, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "부후~. 괜찮아? <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1710056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もはや無事なのはわしらだけか。<line-break>しかし、やるだけのことは<line-break>やらねばなるまいの。<line-break>ゆくぞ、<value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "Are we the only ones left unharmed? Still, we must do what we can. Come, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "이제 무사한 건 우리뿐인가. 그래도 할 수 있는 건 해봐야겠지. 간다, <value:$28>!<end>"

## 1710057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いたぞ！<line-break>やっぱりあのヘビ、<line-break>越えられてやがった！<end>"
EN: "There it is! That damned snake made it across after all!<end>"
KO: "있다! 역시 그 뱀, 넘어와 버렸잖아!<end>"

## 1710058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それをここで始末するのが<line-break>私たちの仕事ですからね。<line-break>さ、行きましょう！<end>"
EN: "And it's our job to finish it off here. Come, let's go!<end>"
KO: "그걸 여기서 처리하는 게 우리 일이니까요. 자, 갑시다!<end>"

## 1710059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ど、どうする俺…？<line-break>なんか、あいつら<line-break>めっちゃ強そうだな…。　<line-break>ええい、めんどくせぇ！<line-break>こうなったら<line-break>当たって砕けちまうか！！<end>"
EN: "W-what am I supposed to do...? Those guys look really strong... Ah, to hell with it! I might as well charge in and take my chances!<end>"
KO: "어, 어떡하지 나…? 저 녀석들 엄청 강해 보이는데…. 에잇, 귀찮아! 이렇게 된 이상 부딪쳐 보는 수밖에!!<end>"

## 1710060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おやめなさい！！<end>"
EN: "Stop at once!<end>"
KO: "그만하세요!!<end>"

## 1710061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ディンガルの将軍と<line-break>お見受けします。<end>"
EN: "You appear to be a general of Dyneskal.<end>"
KO: "딩갈의 장군으로 보입니다.<end>"

## 1710062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いかにも！<line-break>朱雀将軍アンギルダンと申す。<line-break>そなたは？<end>"
EN: "Indeed! I am Angeerdan, the Vermilion Bird General. And you are?<end>"
KO: "그렇다! 주작장군 앙길단이라 한다. 그대는?<end>"

## 1710063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私はイークレムン。　　　　　　<line-break>水の神殿の巫女です。<line-break>　<line-break>自衛行為こそしますが、<line-break>私どもの町は争いを望みません。<line-break>武器をお収めください。<end>"
EN: "I am Iiklmn, a priestess of the Water Temple. We will defend ourselves, but our town does not seek conflict. Please lower your weapons.<end>"
KO: "저는 이크레문. 물의 신전 무녀입니다. 자위는 하지만 저희 마을은 싸움을 원하지 않습니다. 무기를 거두어 주세요.<end>"

## 1710064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございます。<end>"
EN: "Thank you.<end>"
KO: "감사합니다.<end>"

## 1710065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "つい先程も、この岸に　　　　　<line-break>そちらの兵の方が<line-break>ふたりほど漂着されました。<line-break>今、傷の手当が終わったところです。<line-break>まもなくこちらに来るでしょう。<end>"
EN: "Just a short while ago, two of your soldiers washed ashore here. We have just finished tending their wounds. They should be here shortly.<end>"
KO: "조금 전에도 이 해안에 그쪽 병사 두 명 정도가 표류해 왔습니다. 지금 막 상처 치료가 끝났습니다. 곧 이쪽으로 올 겁니다.<end>"

## 1710066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんと…。<line-break>かたじけない、感謝いたす！<end>"
EN: "What...? You have my deepest gratitude!<end>"
KO: "뭐라고…. 고맙다, 감사하네!<end>"

## 1710067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、アンギルダン将軍…！<end>"
EN: "G-General Angeerdan...!<end>"
KO: "아, 앙길단 장군…!<end>"

## 1710068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うむ。無事で何よりじゃ。<line-break>安心せい。お主らの働きは<line-break>よくわかっておるわ。<end>"
EN: "Good. I am glad to see you safe. Be at ease. I am well aware of your efforts.<end>"
KO: "음. 무사해서 다행이구나. 안심하거라. 너희의 활약은 잘 알고 있다.<end>"

## 1710069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "湖のミズチたちには、　　　　　　　<line-break>みなさんを襲うことのないよう、<line-break>私から言い含めておきます。<line-break>できれば、もうこれ以上、<line-break>お互いに誰も傷つけることのないよう<line-break>お引き取りいただきたいのですが。<end>"
EN: "I will instruct the Mizuchi in the lake not to attack any of you. If possible, I would ask that you withdraw, so that no one on either side need be hurt again.<end>"
KO: "호수의 미즈치들에겐 여러분을 공격하지 않도록 제가 잘 이야기해 두겠습니다. 가능하면 더 이상 서로 누구도 다치지 않도록 물러가 주셨으면 합니다만….<end>"

## 1710070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我が軍も、今はこれ以上、<line-break>刃をふるうことはかないませぬ。<line-break>　<line-break>かさねがさね、かたじけない。<line-break>慈愛に満ちたご配慮、ありがたく<line-break>甘えさせていただき申す。<end>"
EN: "My army is in no condition to wield its blades any further. Once again, you have my gratitude. We shall humbly accept your compassionate offer.<end>"
KO: "우리 군도 지금은 더 이상 칼을 휘두를 수 없습니다. 거듭 송구합니다. 자애로운 배려에 감사히 기대겠습니다.<end>"

## 1710071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では失礼いたします。<line-break>みな様に清き水の加護があらんことを。<end>"
EN: "Then I shall take my leave. May the blessing of pure water be upon you all.<end>"
KO: "그럼 실례하겠습니다. 여러분에게 맑은 물의 가호가 있기를.<end>"

## 1710072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "赤い…鎧…。<line-break>アンギルダン様…。<end>"
EN: "Red... armor... Lord Angeerdan...<end>"
KO: "붉은… 갑옷…. 앙길단 님….<end>"

## 1710073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや、何か懐かしい感じのする<line-break>娘じゃったのでな。<end>"
EN: "No, there was simply something strangely familiar about that girl.<end>"
KO: "아니, 어쩐지 그리운 느낌이 드는 아가씨여서 말이지.<end>"

## 1710074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ば、バカなことを申すな！<line-break>そら、本陣に戻るぞっ。<end>"
EN: "D-don't talk nonsense! Come, we're returning to the main camp!<end>"
KO: "바, 바보 같은 소리 마라! 자, 본진으로 돌아가자.<end>"

## 1710075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…それで、卿らは<line-break>おめおめと逃げ帰ってきたと、<line-break>こういうことかね？<end>"
EN: "So... you fled back here in disgrace. Is that what happened?<end>"
KO: "…그래서 경들은 꼴사납게 도망쳐 돌아왔다는 건가?<end>"

## 1710076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さすがはアンギルダン将軍。<line-break>恥も外聞もなく生に執着させれば<line-break>大陸一ですな。<end>"
EN: "Just what one would expect of General Angeerdan. When it comes to clinging to life without shame or dignity, no one on the continent can match you.<end>"
KO: "역시 앙길단 장군. 체면도 염치도 없이 삶에 집착하게 하면 대륙 제일이군요.<end>"

## 1710077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "多くの船が大破、あるいは　　　　<line-break>退却を余儀なくされた状況下では<line-break>それが最善と判断したまで。<line-break>やはりアキュリュースを<line-break>攻めるのであれば、まずはミズチを<line-break>攻略するのが肝要かと思うが…。<end>"
EN: "With so many ships wrecked or forced to retreat, I merely judged withdrawal to be our best course. If we are to attack Aqyurius, surely we must defeat the Mizuchi first...<end>"
KO: "많은 배가 크게 파손되거나 퇴각할 수밖에 없는 상황에서 그것이 최선이라고 판단했을 뿐이다. 역시 아큐류스를 공격하려면 먼저 미즈치를 공략하는 게 중요하다고 생각하지만….<end>"

## 1710078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなことは卿に言われずとも<line-break>わかっている！！<line-break>　<line-break>…もうよい、下がれ。<line-break>今後の作戦は後日<line-break>追って指示する。<end>"
EN: "I do not need you to tell me that! Enough. You are dismissed. Further orders will follow in due course.<end>"
KO: "그런 건 경이 말하지 않아도 알고 있다!! …됐다, 물러가라. 이후 작전은 나중에 다시 지시하겠다.<end>"

## 1710079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アンギルダン将軍…。<line-break>我々のために…<line-break>申しわけありません。<end>"
EN: "General Angeerdan... You did that for us... I'm sorry.<end>"
KO: "앙길단 장군… 저희를 위해… 죄송합니다.<end>"

## 1710080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フハハ。<line-break>なに、わしとてあの状況は<line-break>逃げたくてたまらんかったからのう。<line-break>おぬしらが気にすることはないわい。<line-break>さあ、また次の戦がある。<line-break>戻ってゆっくり休むがいい。<end>"
EN: "Ha ha! Come now, I was desperate to get out of there myself. None of you need worry about it. Another battle lies ahead, so go back and get some proper rest.<end>"
KO: "후하하. 뭐, 나도 그 상황에서는 도망치고 싶어 견딜 수가 없었으니 말이야. 자네들이 신경 쓸 것 없네. 자, 또 다음 전투가 있다. 돌아가 푹 쉬도록 하게.<end>"

## 1710081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、おぬしもじゃ。<line-break>また次の戦いがある。<line-break>ゆっくり体を休めるがよかろう。　<line-break>…しかし、船から湖に飛びこむ時の<line-break>おぬしのこわばった顔…。<line-break>あれは、傑作じゃったのう。<end>"
EN: "That goes for you too, <value:$28>. Another battle lies ahead, so get plenty of rest. But the way your face tensed up when you jumped from the ship into the lake... That was priceless.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 너도 마찬가지다. 또 다음 싸움이 있어. 느긋하게 몸을 쉬게나. …그런데 배에서 호수로 뛰어들 때 네 굳은 얼굴…. 그건 정말 걸작이었어.<end>"

## 1710082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フハハハハ。<line-break>では、またな。<end>"
EN: "Ha ha ha ha! Until next time.<end>"
KO: "후하하하하. 그럼 또 보자.<end>"

## 1710083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おお、<value:$28>か。<line-break>アキュリュース攻めはしばし休止じゃ。<line-break>　<line-break>ジラークも、前回のミズチによる被害で<line-break>さすがに冷静さを取り戻したらしい。<line-break>何やら作戦を練っているようじゃ。<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. The assault on Aqyurius is on hold for now. It seems even Zsilak has regained his composure after the damage Mizuchi caused last time. He appears to be drawing up some kind of plan.<end>"
KO: "오, <value:$28>인가. 아큐류스 공략은 잠시 중단이다. 지라크도 지난번 미즈치에게 입은 피해로 마침내 냉정을 되찾은 모양이야. 무언가 작전을 짜고 있는 것 같군.<end>"

## 1710084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "先日の娘か…。<line-break>老いぼれの昔話、聞く気があるか？<end>"
EN: "That girl from the other day... Care to hear an old codger's tale?<end>"
KO: "며칠 전 그 아가씨인가…. 늙은이의 옛날이야기를 들어 볼 텐가?<end>"

## 1710085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バロルとたもとを分かって、<line-break>アキュリュ－スの傭兵隊長を<line-break>しとるとき、わしは恋に落ちた。　　<line-break>ルフェイと言った。<line-break>先日の娘と同じように<line-break>水の神殿の巫女じゃった。<end>"
EN: "After parting ways with Balor, I was serving as captain of Aqyurius's mercenaries when I fell in love. Her name was Lufei. Like that girl from the other day, she was a priestess of the Water Temple.<end>"
KO: "발로르와 결별하고 아큐류스의 용병대장을 하고 있을 때, 나는 사랑에 빠졌지. 루페이라고 했다. 얼마 전 그 처녀처럼 물의 신전의 무녀였어.<end>"

## 1710086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、こちらは傭兵風情、<line-break>ふたりは引き裂かれそうになった。<line-break>わしはルフェイに逃げようと言った。<line-break>しかし、ルフェイはダメだと言った。<line-break>それきり、<line-break>わしらは会えんようになった。<end>"
EN: "But I was a mere mercenary, and it looked as though we would be torn apart. I asked Lufei to run away with me, but she said no. After that, we could no longer see each other.<end>"
KO: "하지만 이쪽은 일개 용병이라 두 사람은 갈라놓이게 됐지. 나는 루페이에게 도망치자고 했어. 하지만 루페이는 안 된다고 했다. 그 뒤로 우리는 만나지 못했지.<end>"

## 1710087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最後にルフェイと会ったのは、<line-break>アキュリュースにバロルの手先、<line-break>円卓騎士ネモが攻めてきたときじゃ。<line-break>強力な敵じゃった。<line-break>しかし、わしがやられかけた時、<line-break>ルフェイが助けに来た。<end>"
EN: "The last time I saw Lufei was when Nemo, Balor's agent and a Knight of the Round Table, attacked Aqyurius. He was a formidable foe. But when I was nearly done for, Lufei came to my aid.<end>"
KO: "마지막으로 루페이를 만난 건 아큐류스에 발로르의 부하, 원탁기사 네모가 쳐들어왔을 때야. 강력한 적이었지. 하지만 내가 당할 뻔했을 때 루페이가 구하러 왔다.<end>"

## 1710088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "彼女は自分の魔法力のすべてを使って<line-break>ネモを撃退した。<line-break>アキュリュースもわしも助かった。　<line-break>だが、彼女は…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "She used every last drop of her magical power to drive Nemo back. She saved Aqyurius, and she saved me. But she...<end>"
KO: "그녀는 자신의 모든 마력을 써서 네모를 물리쳤다. 아큐류스도 나도 살아났지. 하지만 그녀는….<end>"

## 1710089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "先日の娘が、そのルフェイに　　　<line-break>どことなく似ておってな。<line-break>少しばかし、思い出しておったのじゃ。<end>"
EN: "That girl from the other day reminded me of Lufei somehow. I found myself thinking back.<end>"
KO: "얼마 전 그 아가씨가 루페이와 어딘지 모르게 닮아서 말이야. 잠시 옛일을 떠올리고 있었네.<end>"

## 1710090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何じゃ、またあの娘の話か？<line-break>似とるといっても、雰囲気だけじゃ。　<line-break>わしらは、お互いに年じゃったしな。<line-break>プレゼントも、放浪中に見つけた<line-break>人魚の鱗で作ったイヤリングだけじゃ。<line-break>手作りじゃからこの世にふたつとない。<end>"
EN: "What, are we talking about that girl again? There may be a resemblance, but it's only in the impression she gives. We were both old by then, you know. And the only present was a pair of earrings made from mermaid scales I found during my wanderings. They were handmade, so there isn't another pair like them in the world.<end>"
KO: "뭐야, 또 그 여자 얘기냐? 닮았다고 해도 분위기뿐이야. 우리도 서로 나이가 있었고. 선물도 방랑 중 찾은 인어 비늘로 만든 귀걸이 하나뿐이었지. 직접 만든 거라 세상에 둘도 없어.<end>"

## 1710091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…思えば、あのころはまだまだ<line-break>若かったんじゃな。<line-break>そんな恥ずかしいまねも平気でできた。<end>"
EN: "Looking back, I was still young in those days. I could do something that embarrassing and think nothing of it.<end>"
KO: "…생각해 보면 그때는 아직 젊었구먼. 그런 부끄러운 짓도 아무렇지 않게 했으니.<end>"

## 1710092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前……見たことあるぞっ。<line-break>ディンガルのアンギルダン将軍の　　　<line-break>側近だった奴だろっ！？<line-break>ここを通すわけにはいかないっ！<line-break>アキュリュースは、清らかな水の町っ！<line-break>貴様らに汚させはしないぞっ！<end>"
EN: "You... I've seen you before. You were an aide to General Angeerdan of Dyneskal, weren't you? I can't let you through! Aqyurius is a city of pure water. I won't let your kind defile it!<end>"
KO: "너… 본 적 있어! 딘갈의 앙길단 장군 측근이었지!? 여길 통과시킬 순 없어! 아큐류스는 맑은 물의 도시야! 너희한테 더럽히게 두지 않겠어!<end>"

## 1710093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おい、お前！<line-break>ディンガルの密偵か！？　　　　　<line-break>密偵なのか！？<line-break>この船はアキュリュースへ渡る<line-break>唯一の足だ。お前をここから先へと<line-break>通すわけにはいかないぞ！<end>"
EN: "Hey, you! Are you a Dyneskal spy? Are you? This boat is the only way across to Aqyurius. I can't let you go any farther!<end>"
KO: "어이, 너! 딩갈의 첩자냐!? 첩자인 거냐!? 이 배는 아큐류스로 건너가는 유일한 수단이다. 널 여기서 더 보내 줄 수는 없어!<end>"

## 1710094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここまでだ。<line-break>ディンガルの兵隊さんよ。　　<line-break>　<line-break>さあ、今ここで<line-break>命を奪ったりはしない。<line-break>おとなしく引き下がるんだな。<end>"
EN: "That's far enough, Dyneskal soldier. I won't kill you here and now. Turn around and leave quietly.<end>"
KO: "여기까지다, 딘갈 병사들. 지금 당장 목숨을 빼앗진 않겠다. 얌전히 물러가라.<end>"

## 1710095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、本当に<line-break>いい天気ですこと。<end>"
EN: "Oh, what lovely weather.<end>"
KO: "아아, 정말 좋은 날씨네요.<end>"

## 1710096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…いい天気ですねえ。<end>"
EN: "...Lovely weather, isn't it?<end>"
KO: "…날씨가 좋네요.<end>"

## 1710097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ、いい天気ですね<end>"
EN: "Yes, it is lovely weather.<end>"
KO: "네, 날씨가 좋네요.<end>"

## 1710098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "帝国はあの陽より輝く<end>"
EN: "The Empire shines brighter than that sun.<end>"
KO: "제국은 저 태양보다 빛난다<end>"

## 1710099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ええ。<line-break>本当に、いい天気ですねえ…。<end>"
EN: "...Yes. It really is lovely weather...<end>"
KO: "…네. 정말 좋은 날씨네요….<end>"

## 1710100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あら、そうですか。<end>"
EN: "Oh, is that so?<end>"
KO: "어머, 그런가요.<end>"

## 1710101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こちらです。来てください。<end>"
EN: "This way, please. Follow me.<end>"
KO: "이쪽입니다. 따라오세요.<end>"

## 1710102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ、気に入らねえな。<line-break>せっかく動けなくなってる敵を　　　<line-break>介抱するなんて…。<line-break>じゃ、俺たちが戦場で命がけで<line-break>やってることはいったい何なんだよ！<line-break>くそっ！<end>"
EN: "Damn it, I don't like this. Why tend to enemies when we've finally got them helpless? What the hell are we risking our lives for out there, then? Damn it!<end>"
KO: "큭, 마음에 안 드네. 모처럼 움직이지 못하게 된 적을 간호하다니…. 그럼 우리가 전장에서 목숨 걸고 하는 일은 대체 뭐야! 젠장!<end>"

## 1710103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "敵味方関係なく介抱してやがる。　<line-break>こいつ、バカじゃないのか？<line-break>　<line-break>奴らが回復したら、またこの町に<line-break>攻めてくるだろうさ。<line-break>これじゃあ堂々巡りじゃないか。<end>"
EN: "That fool's tending to everyone, friend and foe alike. Once the enemy recovers, they'll just attack this town again. We'll be right back where we started.<end>"
KO: "적군 아군 가리지 않고 간호하고 있잖아. 이 자식, 바보 아냐? 놈들이 회복하면 또 이 마을을 공격해 올 텐데. 이래선 끝없는 반복이잖아.<end>"

## 1710104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ミズチに船から突き落とされ…、<line-break>気付いたら、ここでこうして<line-break>あの少女の介抱を受けている。<line-break>俺はこの町を攻めに来たのに…<line-break>なぜ、こんなことをしてくれる？<line-break>敵の介抱など…、信じられん。<end>"
EN: "Mizuchi knocked me off the ship, and when I came to, I was here, being tended by that girl. I came to attack this town, so why would she do this for me? Tending to an enemy... I can scarcely believe it.<end>"
KO: "미즈치에게 배에서 떠밀려 떨어지고… 정신을 차리니 여기서 저 소녀의 간호를 받고 있다. 나는 이 마을을 공격하러 왔는데… 왜 이런 짓을 해 주는 거지? 적을 간호하다니… 믿을 수 없군.<end>"

## 1710105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この、あふれる感謝の思いはなんだ…。<line-break>胸の奥底から、温かい気持ちになる…。<line-break>これが信仰の力、これが人の愛か。<end>"
EN: "What is this gratitude welling up inside me? A warmth is rising from deep within me. So this is the power of faith. This is human love.<end>"
KO: "이 넘치는 감사의 마음은 뭐지…. 가슴 깊은 곳에서 따뜻한 기분이 들어…. 이것이 신앙의 힘, 이것이 사람의 사랑인가.<end>"

## 1710106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "戦争だから。命令だから。<line-break>私は敵味方で人を区別して、<line-break>敵は殺したりもしてきました。<line-break>でも、敵味方なんて関係なく<line-break>命に手を差し伸べる、そんな<line-break>少女がいるだなんて…。<end>"
EN: "It was war. I was following orders. I've treated people differently depending on whether they were friend or foe, and I've even killed my enemies. But to think there could be a girl who reaches out to save lives, regardless of which side they're on...<end>"
KO: "전쟁이니까. 명령이니까. 저는 적과 아군을 구분해 적을 죽이기도 했습니다. 하지만 적아를 가리지 않고 생명에 손을 내미는 그런 소녀가 있다니….<end>"

## 1710107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よう、<value:$28>。<line-break>…おっと、恨みっこなしだぜ。<line-break>　<line-break>お互い仕事次第で敵味方だ。<line-break>今回は味方同士なんだから<line-break>仲よくやろうや。<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>. Now, no hard feelings. Depending on the job, we could be allies or enemies. This time we're on the same side, so let's get along.<end>"
KO: "여, <value:$28>. …아, 서로 원망은 없기다. 일에 따라 적도 되고 아군도 되는 거니까. 이번엔 같은 편이니 잘 지내자고.<end>"

## 1710108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>。<line-break>ひとつだけアドバイスしてやるよ。<line-break>　<line-break>いいか、常に自分を見失うな。<line-break>他人に流されて<line-break>同じように行動してると後悔するぞ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, let me give you one piece of advice. Whatever happens, don't lose sight of yourself. If you just follow the crowd and do what everyone else does, you'll regret it.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>. 하나만 충고하지. 항상 자신을 잃지 마. 남에게 휩쓸려 똑같이 행동하다간 후회하게 될 거야.<end>"

## 1710109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よぉ。<value:$28>だっけ？<line-break>俺はツェラシェル。よろしく。<line-break>　<line-break>ま、傭兵は傭兵同士<line-break>仲よくやっていこうぜ。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Hey. You're <value:$28>, right? I'm Zeraschel. Good to meet you. Well, we're both mercenaries, so let's get along.<end>"
KO: "안녕. <value:$28>였지? 나는 체라셸이야. 잘 부탁해. 뭐, 용병끼리 사이좋게 지내자고.<end>"

## 1710110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人の命の尊さに、ディンガルも<line-break>アキュリュースも関係ありません。<line-break>　<line-break>ミズチたちに湖に浮かんでいた<line-break>ケガ人を運んでもらって、<line-break>こうして手当てをしています。<end>"
EN: "The worth of a human life has nothing to do with whether someone is from Dyneskal or Aqyurius. The Mizuchi carried the wounded who were floating in the lake here, and I'm tending to them.<end>"
KO: "사람 목숨의 소중함에 딩갈도 아큐류스도 관계없습니다. 미즈치들에게 호수에 떠 있던 부상자들을 데려오게 해서 이렇게 치료하고 있습니다.<end>"

## 1710111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの大蛇がねぇ？<line-break>あれ、人を喰わねぇのかな。<end>"
EN: "That giant serpent, eh? Doesn't it eat people?<end>"
KO: "그 큰 뱀 말이야? 사람을 잡아먹진 않나.<end>"

## 1710112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ミズチは精霊に近い水の妖精です。<line-break>何も食べずに生きているんです。<line-break>　<line-break>人を食べて生きているのは、<line-break>人を殺して、お金をもらっている<line-break>あなたたちの方です！<end>"
EN: "The Mizuchi are water fairies, akin to spirits. They don't need to eat at all. You're the ones who feed on people―you kill them for money!<end>"
KO: "미즈치는 정령에 가까운 물의 요정입니다. 아무것도 먹지 않고 살아갑니다. 사람을 먹고 사는 건 사람을 죽이고 돈을 받는 당신들 쪽이에요!<end>"

## 1710113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おお、こわ。<line-break>しかし、こりゃ一本取られたね。<line-break>　<line-break>じゃ、これ以上怒られないように<line-break>健康な俺は退散しましょうかね。<end>"
EN: "Ooh, frightening. Still, you've got me there. Well, since I'm fit and healthy, I suppose I'll make myself scarce before I get scolded any more.<end>"
KO: "오, 무서워라. 그래도 이건 한 방 먹었네. 더 혼나기 전에 멀쩡한 나는 이만 사라져야겠군.<end>"

## 1710114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すいません。<line-break>こんな所で声を荒げたりして…。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry. I shouldn't have raised my voice in a place like this.<end>"
KO: "죄송합니다. 이런 곳에서 목소리를 높여서….<end>"

## 1710115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え？　このイヤリング…ですか？<line-break>きれいでしょう？<line-break>母の形見なんです。<end>なんでも、人魚の鱗から作った<line-break>イヤリングだそうです。<line-break>母が父から送られたとか…。<end>"
EN: "Oh? This earring? It's beautiful, isn't it? It's a memento of my mother.<end>Apparently, it was made from a mermaid's scale. I was told my father gave it to my mother.<end>"
KO: "네? 이 귀걸이… 말인가요? 예쁘죠? 어머니의 유품이에요.<end>인어의 비늘로 만든 귀걸이라고 해요. 어머니가 아버지께 받은 거라던데…<end>"

## 1710116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私の両親は、私が赤ん坊の時に<line-break>亡くしているので、<line-break>何も覚えていないんです。<line-break>でも、これをしていると<line-break>父や母が近くにいるような感じが<line-break>するので、いつもしているんです。<end>"
EN: "My parents died when I was a baby, so I don't remember anything about them. But when I wear this, it feels as though my father and mother are close by. That's why I always keep it on.<end>"
KO: "제 부모님은 제가 아기였을 때 돌아가셔서 아무것도 기억하지 못해요. 하지만 이걸 하고 있으면 아버지와 어머니가 가까이 있는 듯해서 늘 하고 있어요.<end>"

## 1710117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もうしばらくしたら、ここにいる<line-break>みなさんも普通に歩けるはずです。<line-break>それまでは私はここにいます。<end>"
EN: "Everyone here should be able to walk normally again before long. Until then, I'll stay here.<end>"
KO: "조금만 더 있으면 여기 계신 분들도 평범하게 걸을 수 있을 거예요. 그때까지 저는 여기에 있겠습니다.<end>"

## 1710118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、こんにちは、<line-break><value:$28>さん。<line-break>また、来てくださったんですね。　　　<line-break>戦いは、悲しみとケガ人を生むばかり。<line-break>私にできることは、こうやって<line-break>手当てをすることくらい…。<end>"
EN: "Oh, hello, <value:$28>. You've come back again. Fighting only brings sorrow and leaves people wounded. All I can do is tend to them like this.<end>"
KO: "아, 안녕하세요, <value:$28> 씨. 또 와 주셨군요. 싸움은 슬픔과 부상자만 낳아요. 제가 할 수 있는 건 이렇게 치료하는 것 정도뿐….<end>"

## 1710119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいかげんにしてほしいもんだな。<line-break>そうやってあんたが今日助けた命が、<line-break>明日戦場で俺たちの命を奪うんだ。<line-break>いっそ、そいつらを<line-break>あの大蛇に喰わせてくれよ。<end>"
EN: "I've had enough of this. The lives you save today will be taking ours on the battlefield tomorrow. You might as well feed the lot of them to that giant serpent.<end>"
KO: "이제 좀 적당히 했으면 좋겠군. 그렇게 당신이 오늘 구한 목숨이, 내일 전장에서 우리 목숨을 위협한다고. 차라리 그들을저 큰 뱀한테 먹이로 줘 버려.<end>"

## 1710120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんて…<line-break>なんてひどいことを言うんですか！！<end>"
EN: "How can you say something so cruel?!<end>"
KO: "무슨… 그런 심한 말을 하는 겁니까!!<end>"

## 1710121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おお恐い。ハハハ…。<end>"
EN: "Ooh, frightening. Ha ha ha...<end>"
KO: "오, 무서워라. 하하하….<end>"

## 1710122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも……あの傭兵さんの<line-break>言う通りなのかもしれません。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>今日助けた命が<line-break>明日、誰かの命を奪う…。<line-break>戦争とは、なんて悲しいのでしょう。<end>"
EN: "But... perhaps that mercenary was right. A life saved today may take another tomorrow. War is such a sad thing.<end>"
KO: "하지만… 그 용병이 한 말이 맞을지도 모르겠어요. 오늘 구한 목숨이 내일 누군가의 목숨을 빼앗는다… 전쟁이란 정말 슬프군요.<end>"

## 1710123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、負傷者の救助は続けます。<line-break>それがディンガルの人であれ、<line-break>アキュリュースの人であれ…。<end>"
EN: "Even so, I will continue rescuing the wounded, whether they are from Dyneskal or Aqyurius.<end>"
KO: "하지만 부상자 구조는 계속할 겁니다. 그 사람이 딘갈 사람이든 아큐류스 사람이든….<end>"

## 1710124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "先日、ディンガルの兵たちが<line-break>無理矢理この下へと入っていったのです。　<line-break>　<line-break>それからしばらくしたら、ものすごい音と<line-break>振動がして…兵たちは帰ってきていません。<line-break>この下で何がおきたのでしょうか…。<end>"
EN: "The other day, Dyneskal's soldiers forced their way down below. A little while later, there was a tremendous noise and violent shaking... The soldiers still haven't returned. What could have happened down there?<end>"
KO: "얼마 전 딘갈 병사들이 억지로 이 아래로 들어갔습니다. 한참 뒤 엄청난 소리와 진동이 났고… 병사들은 돌아오지 않았어요. 아래에서 무슨 일이 있었던 걸까요….<end>"

## 1710125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この下へ行かれるのですか…？<line-break>どうか、気をつけてください……。<end>"
EN: "You're going down there...? Please, be careful.<end>"
KO: "이 아래로 가시려는 건가요…? 부디 조심하세요……<end>"

## 1710126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　無限のソウルを持つ者よ…、　　　　<line-break>　　　　汝も所詮は無知なる人の子か…。<line-break>　　　この先にあるは水の精霊神の座なり。<line-break>　　　触れること、あたわざるものなり…。<line-break>　　　　　　　　我が名は海王。　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　この先への道は断たせてもらった。<line-break>　　　　世界に、晴れることなき闇を<line-break>　　　もたらすわけにはいかぬゆえな…。<end>"
EN: "Bearer of the Infinite Soul... Are you, too, merely an ignorant child of man? Beyond this point lies the seat of the Water Spirit God. It cannot be touched. My name is the Sea King. I have closed the way forward, for I cannot allow darkness that will never lift to fall upon the world.<end>"
KO: "무한의 소울을 지닌 자여…. 너 역시 결국 무지한 인간의 자식인가…. 이 앞은 물의 정령신의 자리. 함부로 닿을 수 없는 곳이다…. 내 이름은 해왕. 이 앞길은 내가 끊어 두었다. 세계에 영원히 걷히지 않을 어둠을 불러올 수는 없기 때문이다….<end>"

## 1710127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2この亀裂…。<line-break>さっきの声が言ってたのはこれね。<line-break>これじゃ進めそうもないわね。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>3なんじゃ、これはまたえらく大胆に<line-break>道を断ってくれたもんじゃのう。<line-break>これではさすがに進めぬか。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>4これはすごいわ。<line-break>道を断つにも限度があるかも。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>5だあぁぁぁぁ！？<line-break>ここまでやるか普通？<line-break>しゃあないな。引き返すか。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>6これじゃ先に進めないね。<line-break>こんな亀裂、飛び越せないよ。<line-break><value:$28>、戻ろう。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>7これでは先に進めないわね。<line-break>戻るしかないわ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>9あ～あ、<line-break>これはまたわかりやすすぎるくらい<line-break>先に進めないじゃん。戻ろっか。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>12これじゃあ、先に進めないな。<line-break>仕方ない、戻るとしようや。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>13…これでは、<line-break>先には進めんな。<line-break>引き返すぞ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>10姉ちゃん！　見ろよこれ。<line-break>まったく、むちゃくちゃするよな。<line-break>しゃあないな、帰ろうか。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>14これでは先に進めんわい。<line-break>戻るしかないのう。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>15ねえ、<value:$28>、<line-break>これじゃ先に進めないよ。<line-break>戻るしかないね。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>17ちょっと、これはどういうことなの！？<line-break>ここまでは無駄足だったわけ？<line-break>キー！！　さっさと戻るのよー！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>19<value:$28>、すごい穴だよ。<line-break>これじゃあ、先に進めないよぉ。<line-break>ねぇ、戻ろう。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>20…戻るぞ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>11戻るしかなさそうね。<end><if><value:$01><equal>21戻るしかないようだな。<end><if><value:$01><equal>23これでは先に進めないな。<line-break>戻ろう。<end><if><value:$01><equal>27これでは先に進めないな。<line-break>戻ろう、<value:$28>。<end><if><value:$01><equal>26これではさすがに進めぬな。 <line-break>戻るしかなさそうじゃな。<end><if><value:$01><equal>24これでは先に進めませんね。<line-break>戻りましょう、<value:$28>様。<end><if><value:$01><equal>25すごい穴ね。<line-break>これじゃあ、先に進めないよ。<line-break>ねぇ、戻ろう。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>192これでは先に進めません。<line-break>これは戻ったほうがよさそうですね。<end><if><value:$01><equal>193すごい亀裂…。<line-break>まさかここまでするなんて。<line-break>仕方ありません、戻りましょう。<end><if><value:$01><equal>194この亀裂は…。<line-break>やってくれたな海王。<line-break>これでは戻るしかない。<end><if><value:$01><equal>191これはすごいわね。<line-break>…って感心してる場合じゃないんだわ。<line-break>残念だけど、戻るしかないわね。<end><if><value:$01><equal>190これでは先に進めないな。<line-break>引き返すしかなさそうだ。<end>あらら。<line-break>これじゃあ、先に進めないよ。<line-break>すごいね、海王の力って。<line-break>しょうがない、戻ろっか。 <end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2This fissure... So this is what that voice was talking about. It doesn't look like we can go any farther.<end><if><value:$01><equal>3Well, now. That's certainly one bold way to cut off the road. No getting past this, I suppose.<end><if><value:$01><equal>4That's incredible. This may be taking “cutting off the road” a bit too far.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5Gaaah!? Isn't this going a little too far? Nothing for it. Let's turn back.<end><if><value:$01><equal>6We can't go any farther. There's no jumping across a fissure this big. Let's go back, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>7We can't go any farther. We'll have to turn back.<end><if><value:$01><equal>9Oh, come on. It couldn't be any more obvious that we're not getting through here. Want to head back?<end><if><value:$01><equal>12We can't go any farther. Nothing for it. Let's head back.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13...We can go no farther. We're turning back.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10Sis! Look at this. This is completely insane, isn't it? Nothing for it. Let's go back.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14No getting any farther this way. We'll have to turn back.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15Hey, <value:$28>, we can't go any farther. We'll have to turn back.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17Wait, what is this!? Did we come all this way for nothing? Aaargh! Hurry up, we're going back!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19<value:$28>, look at the size of that hole. We can't go any farther. Come on, let's go back.<end><if><value:$01><equal>20...We're going back.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11Looks like we'll have to turn back.<end><if><value:$01><equal>21It seems we have no choice but to turn back.<end><if><value:$01><equal>23We can't go any farther. Let's turn back.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27We can't go any farther. Let's turn back, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>26There's no getting past this. It looks as though we'll have to turn back.<end><if><value:$01><equal>24We cannot go any farther. Please, <value:$28>, let us turn back.<end><if><value:$01><equal>25That's quite a hole. We can't go any farther. Come on, let's go back.<end><if><value:$01><equal>192We cannot go any farther. It would be best to turn back.<end><if><value:$01><equal>193What a fissure... I never thought it would be taken this far. We have no choice. Let us turn back.<end><if><value:$01><equal>194This fissure... You've really done it now, Sea King. We have no choice but to turn back.<end><if><value:$01><equal>191Now that's impressive. ...Wait, this is no time to admire it. It's a shame, but we'll have to turn back.<end><if><value:$01><equal>190We can't go any farther. It looks like we'll have to turn back.<end>Oh, wow. We can't go any farther. The Sea King's power really is something. Nothing for it. Let's head back.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2이 균열…. 아까 목소리가 말한 게 이거구나. 이래선 못 가겠네.<end><if><value:$01><equal>3이건 또 길을 대담하게 끊어 놨구나. 이래선 아무리 해도 못 가겠어.<end><if><value:$01><equal>4이건 대단하네. 길을 끊는 데도 정도가 있지.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5으아아!? 보통 여기까지 하냐? 어쩔 수 없네. 돌아가자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>6이래선 못 가. 이런 균열은 못 뛰어넘어. <value:$28>, 돌아가자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>7이래선 앞으로 갈 수 없네. 돌아가는 수밖에.<end><if><value:$01><equal>9아아, 너무 알기 쉽게 길이 막혔네. 돌아갈까.<end><if><value:$01><equal>12이래선 못 가겠네. 어쩔 수 없이 돌아가자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…이래선 앞으로 못 간다. 돌아간다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10누나! 이것 좀 봐. 정말 막무가내네. 어쩔 수 없지, 돌아가자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14이래선 앞으로 못 간다. 돌아가는 수밖에.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15있잖아, <value:$28>, 이래선 못 가. 돌아가야겠네.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17잠깐, 이게 뭐야!? 여기까지 헛걸음한 거야? 키익!! 빨리 돌아가는 거야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19<value:$28>, 엄청난 구멍이야. 이래선 못 가아. 돌아가자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>20…돌아간다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11돌아가는 수밖에 없겠네.<end><if><value:$01><equal>21돌아가는 수밖에 없겠군.<end><if><value:$01><equal>23이래선 못 가겠군. 돌아가자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27이래선 못 가겠군. 돌아가자, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>26이래선 아무리 해도 못 가겠군. 돌아갈 수밖에.<end><if><value:$01><equal>24이래선 못 가겠네요. 돌아가죠, <value:$28> 님.<end><if><value:$01><equal>25엄청난 구멍이네. 이래선 못 가. 돌아가자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>192이래선 갈 수 없습니다. 돌아가는 게 좋겠습니다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>193엄청난 균열…. 여기까지 할 줄은. 어쩔 수 없어요, 돌아가죠.<end><if><value:$01><equal>194이 균열은…. 해왕, 제대로 해 줬군. 이래선 돌아갈 수밖에.<end><if><value:$01><equal>191대단한데. …감탄할 때가 아니지. 아쉽지만 돌아갈 수밖에.<end><if><value:$01><equal>190이래선 못 가겠군. 돌아갈 수밖에 없겠다.<end>이런. 이래선 못 가. 해왕의 힘 대단하네. 어쩔 수 없지, 돌아갈까.<end>"

## 1710128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>じゃない。<line-break>私に何か？<end>"
EN: "Well, if it isn't <value:$28>. Do you need something from me?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 아니야. 나한테 무슨 용건이야?<end>"

## 1710129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "水の精霊神？<line-break>いえ、知らないわね…。<line-break>　<line-break>私たち海賊はどちらかというと<line-break>水の精霊神っていうより海の神様、<line-break>つまり、海王さまを信仰してるから。<end>"
EN: "The Water Spirit God? No, I can't say I've heard of it. We pirates tend to worship the god of the sea―the Sea King―rather than the Water Spirit God.<end>"
KO: "물의 정령신? 아니, 모르겠네… 우리 해적은 물의 정령신보다 바다의 신, 즉 해왕님을 믿으니까.<end>"

## 1710130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え！？　海王さまの声を？<line-break><value:$28>、あなたって<line-break>すごい冒険をしているのね…。<line-break>海王さまはしぶきの群島の奥、水に<line-break>包まれた島にいると言われているわ。<line-break>まあ、迷信でしょうけどね。<end>"
EN: "What!? You heard the Sea King's voice? <value:$28>, you're having quite an adventure. They say the Sea King dwells on an island enveloped in water, deep within the Spray Archipelago. Though I suppose that's only superstition.<end>"
KO: "뭐!? 해왕님의 목소리를? <value:$28>, 너 정말 대단한 모험을 하고 있구나…. 해왕님은 물보라 군도 깊숙한 곳, 물에 둘러싸인 섬에 있다고 전해져. 뭐, 미신이겠지만.<end>"

## 1710131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "前に話したかもしれないけど、<line-break>しぶきの群島は危険な場所よ。<line-break>行くならとめないけど、気をつけて。<end>"
EN: "I may have told you this before, but the Spray Archipelago is a dangerous place. I won't stop you from going, but be careful.<end>"
KO: "전에 말했을지도 모르지만 물보라 군도는 위험한 곳이야. 간다면 말리진 않겠지만 조심해.<end>"

## 1710132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　無限のソウルを持つ者よ…、　　　<line-break>　　　この先は、空と水とが交わりし世界。<line-break>　　人の子であるあなたでは、これより先に<line-break>　　　　　進むこと、かないませぬ…。<end>"
EN: "Bearer of the Infinite Soul... Beyond here lies a world where sky and water meet. As a child of man, you cannot venture any farther...<end>"
KO: "무한의 소울을 가진 자여…, 이 앞은 하늘과 물이 만나는 세계. 인간의 아이인 당신은 여기서 더 나아갈 수 없습니다….<end>"

## 1710133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　しかし、あなたがこの先へと進むことは　　<line-break>　　　　私の望むところでもあります。<line-break>　　　　　　　私に残された力で<line-break>　　　　あなたの道をひらきましょう…。<end>"
EN: "And yet, I too wish for you to go on. With what power remains to me, I shall open the way for you...<end>"
KO: "하지만 당신이 이 앞을 나아가는 건 제 바람이기도 합니다. 제게 남은 힘으로 당신의 길을 열겠습니다….<end>"

## 1710134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　これでこの先の地も　　　　　　<line-break>　　　あなたを受けいれることでしょう。<line-break>　　　　　　さあ、進みなさい。<line-break>　　そして、おいでなさい。私のもとへ…。<line-break>　　　私は水の精霊神アキュリュース…。<line-break>　　　　　無限のソウルを持つ者よ、<line-break>　　　　　　さあ、進みなさい…。<end>"
EN: "Now the land ahead should accept you. Go forth. And come to me... I am Aqyurius, the spirit god of water... Bearer of the Infinite Soul, go forth...<end>"
KO: "이제 앞으로의 땅도 당신을 받아들일 것입니다. 자, 나아가세요. 그리고 오세요. 제 곁으로…. 저는 물의 정령신 아큐류스…. 무한의 소울을 가진 자여, 자, 나아가세요….<end>"

## 1710135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　愚かなる人の子よ。　　　　　　　<line-break>　我が言葉に耳を傾けず、ここまで来たか…。<line-break>　　大いなる秩序のため、海王の名において<line-break>　　　　　汝を先に進ませはせぬ。<end>"
EN: "Foolish child of man. So you have come this far without heeding my words... For the sake of the great order, and in the name of the Sea King, I shall let you go no farther.<end>"
KO: "어리석은 인간의 아이여. 내 말에 귀 기울이지 않고 여기까지 왔는가…. 위대한 질서를 위해 해왕의 이름으로 너를 앞으로 보내지 않겠다.<end>"

## 1710136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ならば戦うまで！<end>"
EN: "Then it's a fight!<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 싸울 뿐!<end>"

## 1710137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "秩序は大切ですね<end>"
EN: "Order is important, isn't it?<end>"
KO: "질서는 중요하죠<end>"

## 1710138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　愚かなる人の子よ。　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　身の程を知れい！<end>"
EN: "Foolish child of man. Know your place!<end>"
KO: "어리석은 인간의 아이여. 분수를 알아라!<end>"

## 1710139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　愚かな…。<line-break>　　　　　大いなる秩序の価値が<line-break>　　　　　汝にはわからぬのか…。　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　　　我には見える…。<line-break>　　　　　秩序を失いし世界の<line-break>　　　　　混沌にあえぐ様が…。<end>"
EN: "You fool... Can you not understand the value of the great order? I can see it... a world bereft of order, writhing in chaos...<end>"
KO: "어리석구나…. 위대한 질서의 가치를 그대는 모르는가…. 내게는 보인다…. 질서를 잃은 세계가 혼돈 속에서 몸부림치는 모습이….<end>"

## 1710140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　それでよい。　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　秩序こそこの世界において<line-break>　　　　　　もっとも尊きものなり。<line-break>　　　　さあ、汝の世界へ戻るがいい…。<end>"
EN: "That is as it should be. Nothing in this world is more precious than order. Now, return to your world...<end>"
KO: " 그걸로 됐다. 질서야말로 이 세계에서 가장 고귀한 것이니라. 자, 그대의 세계로 돌아가거라….<end>"

## 1710141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　何をしておる。　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　早く引き返すがよい。<line-break>　　　　　　　　いたずらに命を<line-break>　　　　　　縮めることはあるまい。<end>"
EN: "What are you doing? Turn back at once. Do not cut your life short for nothing.<end>"
KO: " 뭘 하고 있느냐. 어서 돌아가거라. 쓸데없이 목숨을 재촉할 필요는 없다.<end>"

## 1710142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "一緒に戦うお仲間以外の方を<line-break>ご案内するわけにはいきません。　　<line-break>　<line-break>どうか、そちらに連れておられる方と<line-break>お別れになられてから<line-break>もう一度ここにいらしてください。<end>"
EN: "I'm afraid I can only guide those who fight alongside you. Please part company with anyone else accompanying you, then return here.<end>"
KO: "함께 싸우는 동료가 아닌 분을 안내할 수는 없습니다. 부디 데리고 계신 분과 헤어진 뒤 다시 여기로 와 주세요.<end>"

## 1710143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、<line-break>アンギルダン様から伝言です。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>ジラーク軍は今のところ動く気配なし。<line-break>どうやら東を攻めている死神カルラに<line-break>歩調をあわせる腹づもりらしい。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, I have a message from Angeerdan. For now, Zsilak's Army shows no sign of moving. It seems they intend to coordinate with Karula the Death God, who is attacking the east.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님, 앙길단 님의 전언입니다. 지라크군은 현재 움직일 기미가 없음. 아무래도 동쪽을 공격 중인 사신 카를라의 움직임에 보조를 맞출 생각인 듯합니다.<end>"

## 1710144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "青竜軍に大きな動きがあった時に<line-break>早急に本陣へ戻ればよいであろう。　<line-break>わしもしばらく旅にでも出るわ。<line-break>…との事です。<line-break>以上、確かにお伝えしました。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "“If the Azure Dragon Army makes any major move, a swift return to the main camp should suffice. I'll go traveling for a while myself.” That was his message. I have relayed it to you in full.<end>"
KO: "청룡군에 큰 움직임이 있을 때 서둘러 본진으로 돌아오면 될 것이다. 나도 잠시 여행이나 떠나겠다. …라고 하셨습니다. 이상, 확실히 전했습니다.<end>"

## 1710145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうして、人は争うのでしょう？<line-break>争いは悲しみを広げていくというのに。<end>"
EN: "Why do people fight when conflict only brings more sorrow?<end>"
KO: "왜 사람들은 싸우는 걸까요? 싸움은 슬픔만 퍼뜨리는데.<end>"

## 1710146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec171-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "イークレムンＶ４<line-break>予備メッセージ５です<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "이크레문 V4 예비 메시지 5입니다<end>"

## 1720001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おお。<value:$28>。<line-break>今から、ジラークにひとつ<line-break>言うてやろうと思っておるところじゃ。<line-break>ちょうどよい。<line-break>お主も来てくれ。<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. I was just about to have a word with Zsilak. Your timing is good. Come with me.<end>"
KO: "오, <value:$28>. 지금 지라크에게 한마디 하러 가려던 참이다. 잘됐군. 너도 와라.<end>"

## 1720002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また、卿か。<end>"
EN: "You again?<end>"
KO: "또 그대인가.<end>"

## 1720003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この状況、いかがするおつもりか？<line-break>このままでは、<line-break>いたずらに時と兵を損じるだけだ。<end>"
EN: "What do you intend to do about this? At this rate, all we'll do is waste time and lose men.<end>"
KO: "이 상황을 어떻게 하실 생각입니까? 이대로라면 공연히 시간과 병력만 잃을 뿐입니다.<end>"

## 1720004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無用な心配だ。<line-break>すでに手は打っている。<line-break>傭兵を雇った。強力な魔術師だ。　<line-break>名をシャリという。<line-break>これをもって湖の大蛇に対応する。<line-break>進軍開始は明朝、以上だ。<end>"
EN: "There's no cause for concern. I've already taken action. I hired a mercenary, a powerful sorcerer named Shalli. He will deal with the great serpent in the lake. The advance begins tomorrow morning. That is all.<end>"
KO: "쓸데없는 걱정이다. 이미 손을 써 두었다. 용병을 고용했지. 강력한 마술사다. 이름은 샤리. 그를 이용해 호수의 큰 뱀에 대응한다. 진군 개시는 내일 아침. 이상이다.<end>"

## 1720005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アンギルダン将軍は<line-break>お帰りだそうだ。<line-break>お連れせよ。<end>"
EN: "I hear General Angeerdan is leaving. Escort him out.<end>"
KO: "앙길단 장군께서 돌아가신다고 한다. 모셔라.<end>"

## 1720006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "申しわけありません、将軍。<line-break>我々も白虎将軍のやり方には<line-break>不満なのですが…。<end>"
EN: "Forgive us, General. We are none too pleased with the White Tiger General's methods either, but...<end>"
KO: "죄송합니다, 장군. 저희도 백호장군의 방식에는 불만이 있습니다만….<end>"

## 1720007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…わかっておる。<line-break>何も言わんでよい。<end>"
EN: "I know. You needn't say anything.<end>"
KO: "…알고 있다. 아무 말도 하지 마라.<end>"

## 1720008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "死竜の洞窟の邪竜に<line-break>人を喰わせようとしておった者と<line-break>名が一致するが…。<end>"
EN: "But the one who tried to feed people to the Evil Dragon in Dead Dragon Cave bore the same name...<end>"
KO: "죽은 용의 동굴에 있는 사룡에게 사람을 먹이려 했던 자와 이름이 같은데….<end>"

## 1720009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんと、シャリとは<line-break>そのような悪事を働く者なのか…。<line-break>　<line-break>その者の目的は<line-break>いったい……。<end>"
EN: "What? Shalli is capable of such wickedness...? What in the world could he be after...?<end>"
KO: "뭐라고, 샤리는 그런 악행을 저지르는 자인가…. 그자의 목적은 대체…….<end>"

## 1720010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃが、わしらは<line-break>わしらにできることをするしかない。<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>、<line-break>明日は早いだろう。今日はもう<line-break>休んでしまうことにしよう。<end>"
EN: "Still, we can only do what is within our power. <value:$28>, we will have an early start tomorrow. Let us get some rest.<end>"
KO: "하지만 우리는 우리가 할 수 있는 일을 할 수밖에 없다. <value:$28>, 내일은 일찍 움직여야겠지. 오늘은 이제 쉬도록 하자.<end>"

## 1720011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "白虎軍、総員乗船！<line-break>敵、アキュリュース守備傭兵団。<line-break>　<line-break>全軍、進め！<end>"
EN: "White Tiger Army, all hands aboard! The enemy is Aqyurius's defending mercenary corps. All forces, advance!<end>"
KO: "백호군, 전원 승선! 적은 아큐류스 수비 용병단이다. 전군, 전진!<end>"

## 1720012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>か…。<line-break>昨日はよく眠れたか？　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>…この戦、どう見る？<line-break>わしは何やらひどく不安じゃ。<line-break>胸騒ぎがしおる…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... Did you sleep well last night? What do you make of this battle? I cannot shake this unease. Something feels terribly wrong.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>인가…. 어제는 잘 잤나? …이번 전투를 어떻게 보나? 나는 왠지 몹시 불안하군. 가슴이 뒤숭숭해….<end>"

## 1720013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "き、来ました！<line-break>例の大蛇です！！<end>"
EN: "I-it's here! That giant serpent!!<end>"
KO: "와, 왔습니다! 그 큰 뱀입니다!!<end>"

## 1720014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、<line-break>ここで僕の出番なわけだね。<end>"
EN: "Well, I suppose this is where I come in.<end>"
KO: "자, 여기서 내가 나설 차례군.<end>"

## 1720015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "紹介する。<line-break>彼が魔道士シャリだ。<end>"
EN: "Allow me to introduce him. This is Shalli, a mage.<end>"
KO: "소개하지. 그가 마도사 샤리다.<end>"

## 1720016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだよ。邪険だなあ。<line-break>僕はただ雇われただけだよ。<line-break>　<line-break>もちろん、自分の仕事には<line-break>全力を尽くすつもりだよ。<end>"
EN: "What? Don't be so cold. I was only hired for the job. Of course, I intend to give it my all.<end>"
KO: "왜 그래, 너무 매정하네. 난 그냥 고용됐을 뿐이야. 물론 내 일에는 최선을 다할 생각이고.<end>"

## 1720017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やあ、<value:$28>。<line-break>まさかこんな所で会えるなんて。<line-break>　<line-break>世界は広いっていうけど<line-break>結構、人の世って狭いね。<end>"
EN: "Hello, <value:$28>. I never imagined we'd meet somewhere like this. They say the world is vast, but the world of men is rather small, isn't it?<end>"
KO: "안녕, <value:$28>. 설마 이런 곳에서 만날 줄이야. 세상은 넓다지만 사람 사는 세상은 꽤 좁네.<end>"

## 1720018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やあ、よろしく。　　　<line-break>君は<value:$28>だよね？<line-break>　<line-break>あはは、知ってるさ。<line-break>たとえ君は僕のこと<line-break>まだ知らなくってもね。<end>"
EN: "Hello. A pleasure to meet you. You're <value:$28>, aren't you? Ahaha, of course I know. Even if you don't know me yet.<end>"
KO: "안녕, 잘 부탁해. 너는 <value:$28> 맞지? 하하, 알고 있어. 네가 아직 나를 모르더라도 말이야.<end>"

## 1720019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕はシャリ。<line-break>人は東方の博士なんて呼ぶんだ。<line-break>よろしくね。<end>"
EN: "I'm Shalli. People call me the Scholar from the East. A pleasure to meet you.<end>"
KO: "나는 샤리. 사람들은 동방의 박사라고 부르지. 잘 부탁해.<end>"

## 1720020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "死竜に人を喰わせとったのは、<line-break>お主か！<end>"
EN: "Was it you who fed people to the death dragon?!<end>"
KO: "사룡에게 사람을 먹인 게 너였느냐!<end>"

## 1720021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何のことぉ？<line-break>いきなり失礼だなぁ、この人。<line-break>　<line-break>まあいいや。<end>"
EN: "What are you talking about? How rude, coming out with that all of a sudden. Ah, whatever.<end>"
KO: "무슨 소리야? 다짜고짜 실례네, 이 사람. 뭐, 됐어.<end>"

## 1720022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "んじゃ、お仕事に<line-break>精を出しちゃおうかな。<end>"
EN: "Well then, I suppose I'd better get to work.<end>"
KO: "그럼 일에 힘 좀 써 볼까.<end>"

## 1720023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "汝、吹きすさぶ風に生まれ、<line-break>汝、まきあがる土と現る。<line-break>　<line-break>暗雲と共にいでよ！<line-break>汝！　雄々しき雷よ！<line-break>我に仇なす者を撃て！！<end>"
EN: "Thou, born of the raging wind, thou who appearest with the whirling earth―come forth with the dark clouds! O valiant thunder, strike down those who stand against me!!<end>"
KO: "그대, 휘몰아치는 바람에서 태어나고, 그대, 솟구치는 흙과 함께 나타나라. 먹구름과 함께 오라! 그대여! 용맹한 천둥이여! 나의 적을 쳐라!!<end>"

## 1720024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ミ、ミズチが…。<end>"
EN: "M-Mizuchi...<end>"
KO: "미, 미즈치가….<end>"

## 1720025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おお…。<line-break>すばらしい…。<end>"
EN: "Oh... Magnificent...<end>"
KO: "오오…. 훌륭하군….<end>"

## 1720026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いかがでございましょうか？<line-break>白虎将軍閣下。<end>"
EN: "Was that to your satisfaction, Your Excellency, White Tiger General?<end>"
KO: "어떠십니까? 백호장군 각하.<end>"

## 1720027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うむ。<end>"
EN: "Yes.<end>"
KO: "음.<end>"

## 1720028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "危険だ、危険だぞ、ジラーク！　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>ミズチは水の精、身を焼いても<line-break>その力はこの世に残るやもしれん。<line-break>それがわからぬお主でもあるまい！？<end>"
EN: "It's dangerous, Zsilak―dangerous! Mizuchi is a spirit of water. Even if you burn its body, its power may yet linger in this world. Do not tell me you fail to understand that!<end>"
KO: "위험하다, 위험해, 지라크! 미즈치는 물의 정령, 몸을 태워도 그 힘은 이 세상에 남을지 모른다. 그걸 모르는 네가 아니잖아!?<end>"

## 1720029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その時はまた<line-break>その力を焼き滅ぼすまで。　<line-break>　<line-break>全軍前進！<line-break>アキュリュースに着岸せよ！<end>"
EN: "Then we shall burn that power out of existence as well. All forces, advance! Make landfall at Aqyurius!<end>"
KO: "그때는 다시 그 힘을 불태워 없앨 뿐이다. 전군 전진! 아큐류스에 상륙하라!<end>"

## 1720030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クッ…。<end>"
EN: "Ngh...<end>"
KO: "큭….<end>"

## 1720031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "む？　なんじゃこの静けさは…？<line-break>迎撃の傭兵も現れんとは。<line-break>　<line-break>まさか、罠か！？<end>"
EN: "Hm? Why is it so quiet...? Not a single mercenary has come out to meet us. Could this be a trap?!<end>"
KO: "음? 이 고요함은 뭐지…? 요격 용병조차 나타나지 않다니. 설마 함정인가!?<end>"

## 1720032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "卿は本当に<line-break>考えの浅い御仁だな。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>罠を仕掛けたのだよ。<line-break>向こうではなく、こちらがね。<end>"
EN: "You truly are a man of little foresight. There is a trap―but we laid it, not the other side.<end>"
KO: "경은 정말 생각이 얕은 사람이군. 함정을 판 건 저쪽이 아니라 이쪽이다.<end>"

## 1720033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんたの話通り、<line-break>傭兵どもをまるめこんでおいたぜ。<line-break>あとはそっちの仕事…そうだよな？<end>"
EN: "Just as you said, I won the mercenaries over. The rest is your job... right?<end>"
KO: "네 말대로 용병들을 구슬려 뒀어. 이제 네 쪽 일만 남은 거지… 맞지?<end>"

## 1720034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その通りだ。<line-break>ご苦労だったな。<end>"
EN: "Precisely. Well done.<end>"
KO: "그래. 수고했다.<end>"

## 1720035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうも。<line-break>また縁があったらよろしく。<end>"
EN: "Much obliged. If our paths cross again, I hope we can do business.<end>"
KO: "고맙습니다. 또 인연이 닿으면 잘 부탁드리죠.<end>"

## 1720036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…クズめ。<end>"
EN: "...Scum.<end>"
KO: "…쓰레기 자식.<end>"

## 1720037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねえ、ジラーク将軍。<line-break>僕の報酬についても<line-break>忘れていないよね？<end>"
EN: "Say, General Zsilak. You haven't forgotten about my payment, have you?<end>"
KO: "저기, 지라크 장군. 내 보수도 잊지 않았지?<end>"

## 1720038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無論だ。<end>"
EN: "Of course not.<end>"
KO: "물론이다.<end>"

## 1720039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "全軍に伝令。<line-break>乗船したまま待機。<line-break>戦闘態勢は維持。<end>"
EN: "Send word to all forces: remain aboard and stand by. Maintain battle readiness.<end>"
KO: "전군에 전령. 승선한 채 대기. 전투태세 유지.<end>"

## 1720040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はっ！<end>"
EN: "Yes, sir!<end>"
KO: "핫!<end>"

## 1720041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "卿とシャリ、そして<line-break>そちらの傭兵は<line-break>私とともに来てもらおう。<line-break>水の神殿の巫女たちに<line-break>降伏を勧告する。<end>"
EN: "You and Shalli will come with me, along with your mercenary escort. We will call upon the priestesses of the Water Temple to surrender.<end>"
KO: "경과 샤리, 그리고 그쪽 용병은 나와 함께 가도록 하지. 물의 신전 무녀들에게 항복을 권고한다.<end>"

## 1720042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無益な争いは不要だ。<line-break>我々はただアキュリュースを<line-break>我が帝国の支配下に置けばよい。<end>"
EN: "There is no need for pointless conflict. We need only bring Aqyurius under the rule of our Empire.<end>"
KO: "무익한 싸움은 필요 없다. 우리는 그저 아큐류스를 우리 제국의 지배 아래 두면 된다.<end>"

## 1720043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無用な戦をせぬのはよいが…。<line-break>しかし、なんじゃ、<line-break>この言い知れぬ不安感は…。<end>"
EN: "Avoiding needless battle is all well and good... But what is this inexplicable sense of unease...?<end>"
KO: "쓸데없는 싸움을 하지 않는 건 좋지만…. 그런데 이 알 수 없는 불안감은 대체….<end>"

## 1720044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いやあ、こうも僕の都合通りに<line-break>事が進んじゃうと、なんだか<line-break>悪いなーって思っちゃうよ。あはは。<end>"
EN: "Everything is going so perfectly for me that I'm starting to feel a little guilty. Ahaha.<end>"
KO: "이야, 일이 이렇게 내 뜻대로만 풀리니 왠지 미안해지기까지 하네. 아하하.<end>"

## 1720045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そこまでです。<end>"
EN: "That's far enough.<end>"
KO: "거기까지입니다.<end>"

## 1720046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大いなる水の精、<line-break>ミズチを手にかけるとは…。<line-break>　<line-break>そこまでして<line-break>このアキュリュースに<line-break>何を望まれる？<end>"
EN: "You would even slay the great water spirit Mizuchi... What could you want from Aqyurius badly enough to do such a thing?<end>"
KO: "위대한 물의 정령 미즈치에게 손을 대다니…. 그렇게까지 해서 이 아큐류스에서 무엇을 바라십니까?<end>"

## 1720047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我々はただ、我らが帝ネメアの　　<line-break>偉大な治世を、あまねく大陸中に<line-break>広めたいと考えるのみだ。<line-break>無用な略奪や戦闘はしない。<line-break>約束しよう。それはこちらとしても<line-break>望むところではない。<end>"
EN: "We seek only to extend the great reign of our emperor, Nemea, across the entire continent. There will be no needless pillaging or bloodshed. You have my word. We have no desire for either.<end>"
KO: "우리는 그저 우리 황제 네메아의 위대한 통치를 대륙 전체에 널리 퍼뜨리려 할 뿐이다. 불필요한 약탈이나 전투는 하지 않겠다. 약속하지. 우리도 그것을 바라지 않는다.<end>"

## 1720048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そちらが我々の意志に賛同し、<line-break>穏やかに帝国の支配下に入る事を<line-break>期待する。<end>"
EN: "We hope you will support our cause and submit peacefully to imperial rule.<end>"
KO: "그쪽이 우리의 뜻에 동의하고 평온하게 제국의 지배 아래 들어오길 기대한다.<end>"

## 1720049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この町は水の精霊神をまつり、<line-break>水の精霊に祈りを捧げる聖地です。　<line-break>　<line-break>その聖地を守る者たちをかどわかし、<line-break>ミズチを殺すような者たちに<line-break>渡すことなどできません！！<end>"
EN: "This town is sacred ground, where we worship the god of the water spirits and offer our prayers to the spirits of water. We cannot surrender it to people who would lead its guardians astray and kill the Mizuchi!<end>"
KO: "이 도시는 물의 정령신을 모시고 물의 정령에게 기도를 바치는 성지입니다. 그 성지를 지키는 자들을 꾀어내고 미즈치를 죽이는 자들에게 넘겨줄 수는 없습니다!!<end>"

## 1720050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "賛同してもらえぬのなら仕方ない。<line-break>沿岸に待機させた兵たちを<line-break>上陸させるまでだ。<end>"
EN: "If you will not agree, so be it. I need only order the troops waiting offshore to land.<end>"
KO: "동의하지 않는다면 어쩔 수 없지. 해안에 대기시킨 병사들을 상륙시키겠다.<end>"

## 1720051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ…。　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
