KO: "큭….<end>"

## 1720052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの、緊迫してるところを<line-break>悪いんだけど、<line-break>僕の報酬、もらってもいいかな？<end>"
EN: "Sorry to interrupt such a tense moment, but could I have my reward now?<end>"
KO: "저, 긴박한 상황에 미안한데 제 보수 받아도 될까요?<end>"

## 1720053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "構わん。<line-break>好きにするがいい。<end>"
EN: "I have no objection. Do as you please.<end>"
KO: "상관없다. 마음대로 해라.<end>"

## 1720054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねえ、君、イークレムンだよね？<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>僕は君に用があるんだ。<line-break>急で悪いんだけど、僕に<line-break>ついてきてくれないかなあ？<end>"
EN: "Hey, you're Iiklmn, right? I have business with you. Sorry this is so sudden, but would you come with me?<end>"
KO: "있지, 너 이크레문 맞지? 난 너한테 볼일이 있어. 갑작스러워서 미안한데 나를 따라와 주지 않을래?<end>"

## 1720055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なっ…！？　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>ジラーク！<line-break>いったいどういう事だ！？<end>"
EN: "What...?! Zsilak! What is the meaning of this?!<end>"
KO: "뭐…!? 지라크! 대체 무슨 일이냐!?<end>"

## 1720056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何を騒ぐことがある？　　　　　　<line-break>卿はあいかわらず<line-break>くだらないことで騒ぐのだな。<line-break>この者が要求した報酬は<line-break>イークレムンという巫女の身柄だ。<line-break>我々には何も問題あるまい。<end>"
EN: "Why all this commotion? As ever, you make a fuss over the most trivial matters. The reward requested was custody of a priestess named Iiklmn. That poses no problem for us.<end>"
KO: "뭘 그리 소란스럽게 굴지? 경은 여전히 시시한 일로 호들갑이군. 이자가 요구한 보수는 이크레문이라는 무녀의 신병이다. 우리에겐 아무 문제도 없지 않은가.<end>"

## 1720058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うわ、ひどい言われようだなあ。　　<line-break>でも、そんなこと言っていいの？<line-break>　<line-break>僕、報酬もらえないなら<line-break>個人的にここで暴れちゃうよ？<line-break>さっきみたいな事もしちゃおうかな？<end>"
EN: "Wow, that's a terrible thing to call me. But are you sure you want to say that? If I don't get my reward, I might just go on a rampage here myself. Maybe I'll do the same thing I did earlier.<end>"
KO: "와, 정말 심한 말이네. 하지만 그런 소리 해도 괜찮아? 나, 보수를 못 받으면 개인적으로 여기서 난동 부릴 건데? 아까 같은 짓도 해 버릴까?<end>"

## 1720059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やあ、君たちは　　　　　　<line-break>さっきの水蛇たちだね？<line-break>　<line-break>僕が憎い？　ならどうする？<line-break>ここで僕とまた戦おっか？<line-break>よーし、負けないぞ！！<end>"
EN: "Hello. You're the water serpents from before, aren't you? Do you hate me? What are you going to do about it? Want to fight me again, right here? All right, I won't lose!<end>"
KO: "여어, 너희는 아까 그 물뱀들이지? 내가 미워? 그럼 어쩔래? 여기서 나랑 또 싸울까? 좋아, 안 질 거야!!<end>"

## 1720060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やめて！　ミズチたち！！<line-break>あなたたちが戦うことはないわ！　<line-break>　<line-break>大丈夫…、私は大丈夫。<line-break>だから、お願い、もう怒らないで。<line-break>もう傷つかないで…。<end>"
EN: "Stop, Mizuchi! You don't have to fight! It's all right... I'll be all right. So please, don't be angry anymore. Don't let yourselves be hurt again...<end>"
KO: "그만해! 미즈치들아!! 너희가 싸울 필요는 없어! 괜찮아… 난 괜찮아. 그러니까 부탁해, 이제 화내지 마. 더는 다치지 마….<end>"

## 1720061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すごいね、イークレムン。<line-break>君はもう水の巫女としての力に<line-break>目覚めつつあるんだね。<end>"
EN: "That's amazing, Iiklmn. You're already beginning to awaken to your power as the Priestess of Water.<end>"
KO: "대단하네, 이크렘. 넌 벌써 물의 무녀로서의 힘에 눈뜨기 시작했구나.<end>"

## 1720062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひとつ、約束してください。　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>私は、あなたについていきます。<line-break>そのかわり、この町と、町の人は<line-break>絶対に傷つけないでください。<end>"
EN: "Promise me one thing. I'll go with you, but in return, you must not harm this town or anyone who lives here.<end>"
KO: "한 가지 약속해 주세요. 저는 당신을 따라가겠습니다. 대신 이 마을과 주민들은 절대로 다치게 하지 말아 주세요.<end>"

## 1720063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかったよん。<line-break>僕が興味あるのは君だけだから<line-break>他なんかどうでもいいや。<line-break>じゃ、行こうか。<end>"
EN: "All right, then! You're the only one I'm interested in, so I don't care about anything else. Come on, let's go.<end>"
KO: "알겠어용. 내가 관심 있는 건 너뿐이니까 다른 건 아무래도 좋아. 그럼 갈까.<end>"

## 1720064

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Lady Iiklmn!<end>"
KO: "이클레문 님!<end>"

## 1720065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すいません、みなさん。<line-break>あとはよろしくお願いします。<line-break>　<line-break>つらいでしょうが<line-break>今は、耐えてください。<line-break>誰も傷つくことのないように。<end>"

## 1720070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml

KO: "어쩌려는 거요? 주작장군 앙길단 공.<end>"

## 1720071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "奴を追うのじゃ！<end>"
EN: "After him!<end>"
KO: "놈을 쫓는 거다!<end>"

## 1720072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なぜだね？<line-break>彼の行動は私との契約に違反しない。<line-break>つまり、何も問題はないのだよ。<line-break>卿の行動には不審な点が多い。<line-break>それ以上、私の意志に反するようなら<line-break>軍規に基づき罰を与える。<end>"
EN: "Why? His actions do not violate his contract with me. In other words, there is no problem. Your own conduct, however, has given me ample cause for suspicion. Defy my will any further, and I will punish you under military law.<end>"
KO: "왜지? 그의 행동은 나와의 계약을 위반하지 않았다. 즉 아무 문제도 없다는 뜻이다. 경의 행동에는 수상한 점이 많다. 더 이상 내 뜻에 거스른다면 군율에 따라 벌하겠다.<end>"

## 1720073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "軍規がいかほどのものか！<line-break>わしはわしが正しいと思う道を行く！<end>"
EN: "To hell with military law! I will follow the path I believe is right!<end>"
KO: "군율이 대수냐! 나는 내가 옳다고 생각하는 길을 간다!<end>"

## 1720074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その言葉、<line-break>叛逆と見なさざるを得んな。　　<line-break>　<line-break>朱雀将軍アンギルダン・ゼイエン！<line-break>その力量と、現在の状況を考え<line-break>この私が今、処罰する！！<end>"
EN: "I have no choice but to regard those words as treason. Vermilion Bird General Angeerdan Zeyen! Given your abilities and our present circumstances, I shall carry out your punishment myself, here and now!<end>"
KO: "그 말은 반역으로 볼 수밖에 없군. 주작장군 앙길단 제이엔! 그 실력과 현재 상황을 고려해 지금 이 자리에서 내가 처벌하겠다!!<end>"

## 1720075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…なぜだ。<line-break>なぜ卿はそれほどまでに<line-break>あの娘にこだわる…。<end>"
EN: "Why...? Why go so far for that girl...?<end>"
KO: "…왜지. 왜 경은 그토록 그 소녀에게 집착하는가…<end>"

## 1720076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すまぬ。<line-break>こんなわしにも守らねばならぬ　　　<line-break>絆があったようでな。<line-break>ここを平和的に支配下に置くという、<line-break>イークレムンとの約束は守ってくれ。<line-break>では、わしは行かせてもらうぞ。<end>"
EN: "Forgive me. It seems even an old man like me has a bond he must protect. Keep your promise to Iiklmn and bring this place peacefully under your rule. Now, I shall be on my way.<end>"
KO: "미안하네. 이런 나에게도 지켜야 할 인연이 있었던 모양이야. 이곳을 평화적으로 지배하겠다는 이크레문과의 약속은 지켜 줘. 그럼 나는 가겠네.<end>"

## 1720077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すまなんだな、<value:$28>。<line-break>この老いぼれの降って湧いた親心に<line-break>お主までつきあわせてしまって。<line-break>今、このようになってしまってから<line-break>ようやく確信を持って言える。<line-break>あの娘は、わしの子じゃ。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry, <value:$28>. I've dragged you into this old fool's sudden fit of fatherly concern. Only now, after all that's happened, can I finally say it with certainty. That girl is my daughter.<end>"
KO: "미안하구나, <value:$28>. 이 늙은이에게 갑자기 생긴 부모 마음에 너까지 어울리게 해서. 이제 이렇게 되고 나서야 확신을 가지고 말할 수 있구나. 그 아이는 내 딸이다.<end>"

## 1720078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これを見ろ。<end>"
EN: "Look at this.<end>"
KO: "이걸 봐.<end>"

## 1720079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "前に話したかの？<line-break>人魚のウロコで作った耳飾りじゃ。<line-break>イークレムンが落としていった…。<line-break>………………。<end>"
EN: "Did I tell you about it before? It's an earring made from a mermaid's scale. Iiklmn dropped it...<end>"
KO: "전에 이야기했던가? 인어의 비늘로 만든 귀걸이다. 이크레문이 떨어뜨리고 갔지…. ……………….<end>"

## 1720080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、すまんが<line-break>今しばらく、わしに力を貸してくれ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, I'm sorry to ask, but lend me your strength a little longer.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 미안하지만 조금만 더 내게 힘을 빌려주게.<end>"

## 1720081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、わしは<line-break>船にいる兵たちに退却を命じてくる。<line-break>またあとで合流じゃ。<end>"
EN: "Then I'll order the soldiers aboard the ship to withdraw. We'll meet up again afterward.<end>"
KO: "그럼 나는 배에 있는 병사들에게 퇴각을 명하고 오겠다. 나중에 다시 합류하지.<end>"

## 1720082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、二手に分かれよう。<line-break>何かあったら、以前お主が話しとった<line-break>猫屋敷で呼び出してくれ。<end>"
EN: "Then let us split up. If anything happens, summon me at the Cat Manor you mentioned before.<end>"
KO: "그럼 둘로 나뉘자. 무슨 일이 있으면 예전에 자네가 말했던 고양이 저택에서 불러 줘.<end>"

## 1720083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "I'll leave this with you. It may provide a clue.<end>"

## 1720084

## 1720085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、ひとまずさらばじゃ。<end>"
EN: "For now, farewell.<end>"
KO: "그럼, 일단 작별이다.<end>"

## 1720086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの…さっきの大きな赤い鎧の方は<line-break>もしや、ルフェイ様の…。<line-break>ああ、なんと数奇な運命でしょう！？<end>"
EN: "Um... that person from earlier, in the large red armor... Could that perhaps have been Lufei's...? Oh, what a strange fate!<end>"
KO: "저… 아까 그 커다란 붉은 갑옷의 분은 혹시 루페이 님의…. 아아, 이 무슨 기구한 운명이란 말입니까!?<end>"

## 1720087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この町は、守られたのですね。<line-break>でも、イークレムン様は…。<end>"
EN: "So the town has been saved. But Iiklmn...<end>"
KO: "이 마을은 지켜졌군요. 하지만 이크레문 님은….<end>"

## 1720088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメアにより西方攻略を命じられた　<line-break>白虎将軍ジラークは、　　　　<line-break>難航不落といわれたアキュリュースを<line-break>ほぼ無傷のまま支配下に収めた。<end>"
EN: "Ordered by Nemea to conquer the west, White Tiger General Zsilak brought the supposedly impregnable Aqyurius under his control almost entirely intact.<end>"
KO: "네메아에게 서방 공략을 명령받은 백호장군 지라크는 난공불락이라 불리던 아큐류스를 거의 피해 없이 지배하에 두었다.<end>"

## 1720089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この作戦に貢献したシャリと名乗る<line-break>傭兵は、報酬として水の巫女の少女を<line-break>要求。彼女を連れていずこかへと<line-break>姿を消した。<end>"
EN: "A mercenary calling himself Shalli, who had contributed to the campaign, demanded the Priestess of Water, a young girl, as his reward. He then vanished with her, bound for parts unknown.<end>"
KO: "이 작전에 공헌한 샤리라는 용병은 보수로 물의 무녀 소녀를 요구했다. 그는 그녀를 데리고 어디론가 사라졌다.<end>"

## 1720090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アンギルダンは　　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>ジラークとたもとを分かち、　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>娘である水の巫女イークレムンを　　　　　　<line-break>取り戻すための旅の道を踏み出した。　　　　<end>"
EN: "Angeerdan broke with Zsilak and set out on a journey to bring back his daughter Iiklmn, the Priestess of Water.<end>"
KO: "앙길단은 지라크와 결별하고 딸인 물의 무녀 이크레문을 되찾기 위한 여행길에 올랐다.<end>"

## 1720091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　一方、ジラークは大陸統一構想に従い　　<line-break>　　　　　大陸北西部への進軍を計画。　　　　<line-break>　　　神聖都市アルノートゥン攻略に向けて　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　準備を始めていた。　　　　<end>"
EN: "Meanwhile, following the plan to unify the continent, Zsilak planned an advance into the northwest and began preparing to capture the sacred city of Alnortun.<end>"
KO: "한편 지라크는 대륙 통일 구상에 따라 대륙 북서부 진군을 계획했다. 성스러운 도시 알노툰 공략을 위해 준비를 시작하고 있었다.<end>"

## 1720092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、邪魔が入ったが<line-break>先程までの話の続きだ。<line-break>　<line-break>我々の支配下に<line-break>入ってはくれないかね？<end>"
EN: "Now, despite that interruption, let us return to our discussion. Will you accept our rule?<end>"
KO: "방해가 들어왔지만 아까 이야기의 계속이다. 우리 지배 아래 들어오지 않겠나?<end>"

## 1720093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な、何をバカなことを…っ！<end>"
EN: "Wh-What nonsense...!<end>"
KO: "무, 무슨 바보 같은 소리를…!<end>"

## 1720094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誰も傷つかないことが　　　　　　<line-break>イークレムン様の望み…。<line-break>　<line-break>あなたがたに従いましょう。<line-break>ただし、街の人々の生活は<line-break>決しておびやかさないでください。<end>"
EN: "Lady Iiklmn wished for no one to be hurt... We will submit to you. But you must never threaten the townspeople's way of life.<end>"
KO: "아무도 다치지 않는 것이 이크레문 님의 바람…. 여러분께 따르겠습니다. 단, 마을 사람들의 생활은 절대로 위협하지 말아 주세요.<end>"

## 1720095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "約束しよう。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>今後の支配については<line-break>後日あらためてお話しする。<line-break>では、失礼。<end>"
EN: "You have my word. We will discuss the details of our rule another day. Now, if you will excuse me.<end>"
KO: "약속하지. 앞으로의 지배에 대해서는 나중에 다시 이야기하자. 그럼 실례하지.<end>"

## 1720096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…年甲斐もなく<line-break>熱くなりすぎた…かの。<line-break>どうやらここが限界らしいわい。<line-break><value:$28>、<line-break>頼む、イークレムンを…いや、<line-break>わしの娘を助けてやってくれ。<end>"
EN: "Perhaps I let myself get too worked up for a man my age... It seems this is as far as I go. <value:$28>, please. Save Iiklmn... No, save my daughter.<end>"
KO: "…나잇값도 못 하고 너무 열을 올렸나 보군. 아무래도 여기까지인 것 같아. <value:$28>, 부탁한다. 이크레문을… 아니, 내 딸을 구해 줘.<end>"

## 1720097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これは…、前に話したかと思うが、<line-break>人魚のウロコで作った耳飾りじゃ。<line-break>なんらかの手がかりになろう。<end>"
EN: "This is... an earring made from a mermaid's scale. I believe I told you about it before. It may provide some clue.<end>"
KO: "이건… 전에 이야기했을지도 모르지만 인어의 비늘로 만든 귀걸이다. 뭔가 단서가 될 거야.<end>"

## 1720098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人魚のイヤリングを手に入れた。<end>"
EN: "Obtained the Mermaid Earrings.<end>"
KO: "인어의 귀걸이를 손에 넣었다.<end>"

## 1720099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お主と出会ってから今日までの日々、<line-break>老いぼれの最期としては大満足じゃ。<line-break>あとは、任せたぞ…。<end>"
EN: "The days since I met you have given this old man more than he could ask for at the end of his life. I leave the rest to you...<end>"
KO: "너를 만나 오늘까지 보낸 나날, 늙은이의 마지막으로는 더없이 만족스럽구나. 나머지는 맡기마….<end>"

## 1720101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの…あの赤い鎧のお方は<line-break>もしや、ルフェイ様の…？<line-break>ああ、なんと悲しい運命でしょう！<end>"
EN: "Um... the person in that red armor... could that be Lufei's...? Oh, what a tragic fate!<end>"
KO: "저… 저 붉은 갑옷을 입은 분은 혹시 루페이 님의…? 아아, 이 얼마나 슬픈 운명인가요!<end>"

## 1720103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメアにより西方攻略を命じられた<line-break>白虎将軍ジラークは、<line-break>難航不落といわれたアキュリュースを<line-break>ほぼ無傷のまま支配下に収めた。<end>"
EN: "Under orders from Nemea to conquer the west, White Tiger General Zsilak brought Aqyurius, said to be impregnable, under his control almost entirely intact.<end>"
KO: "네메아에게 서방 공략을 명령받은 백호장군 지라크는 난공불락이라 불리던 아큐류스를 거의 피해 없이 지배하에 두었다.<end>"

## 1720104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この時、同じ軍でありながら<line-break>ジラークに刃を向けたアンギルダンは、<line-break>その場でジラークにより処刑され、<line-break>過去の名将となった。<end>"
EN: "At that time, Angeerdan turned his blade against Zsilak despite serving in the same army. Zsilak executed him on the spot, consigning the renowned general to the past.<end>"
KO: "이때 같은 군에 있으면서 지라크에게 칼을 겨눈 앙길단은 그 자리에서 지라크에게 처형되어 과거의 명장이 되었다.<end>"

## 1720105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　ジラークは、大陸統一の計画に従い<line-break>　　　　　大陸北西への進軍を計画。<line-break>　　　神聖都市アルノートゥン攻略に向けて　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　準備を始めていた。　　　　<end>"
EN: "In accordance with the plan to unify the continent, Zsilak planned an advance into the northwest and began preparations to capture the Holy City of Alnortun.<end>"
KO: "지라크는 대륙 통일 계획에 따라 대륙 북서부 진군을 계획했다. 신성도시 알노툰 공략을 위해 준비를 시작했다.<end>"

## 1720106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec172-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　ディンガル帝国は、アンギルダンという<line-break>　　　　　優秀な将軍を失いながらも、<line-break>　　　　　その勢いを損なうことなく<line-break>　　　大陸に覇をとなえようとしていた…。<end>"
EN: "Though the Dyneskal Empire had lost an exceptional general in Angeerdan, its momentum remained undiminished as it sought dominion over the continent...<end>"
KO: "딩갈 제국은 앙길단이라는 뛰어난 장군을 잃었음에도 기세를 잃지 않고 대륙에 패권을 떨치려 하고 있었다….<end>"

## 1720107

## 1730001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やあ、こんにちは。<line-break>あなたも、これから　　　　　　　<line-break>旅に出るのですか？<end>"
EN: "Hello there. Are you about to set out on a journey too?<end>"
KO: "안녕하세요. 당신도 이제 여행을 떠나는 건가요?<end>"

## 1730002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか。　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>あ、そうそう、旅の途中で<line-break>物騒な噂を耳にしました。<line-break>最近、リベルダムへ向かう<line-break>街道には、山賊が出るそうですよ。<line-break>あなたも気をつけてください。<end>"
EN: "I see. Oh, that reminds me. I heard a troubling rumor while I was traveling. Apparently, bandits have been showing up on the road to Liberdam lately. You'd best be careful too.<end>"
KO: "그렇군요. 아, 맞다. 여행 중에 흉흉한 소문을 들었습니다. 요즘 리벨덤으로 가는 가도에는 산적이 나온대요. 당신도 조심하세요.<end>"

## 1730003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "た、たたた、わたた、<line-break>たすけてくれぇぇぇ！！<line-break>　<line-break>ばけ、ばけもの…<line-break>化け物が、化け物が<line-break>俺たちのねぐらにぃぃーっ！<end>"
EN: "H-h-h-help! M-monster... There's a monster―a monster in our hideout!<end>"
KO: "도, 도도도, 도와, 도와줘어어!! 괴, 괴물… 괴물이, 괴물이 우리 소굴에에에!<end>"

## 1730004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひぃああ！<line-break>見つかったら殺されるぅぅ。<line-break>たぁぁすけぇてぇぇぇ！！<end>"
EN: "Aaaagh! If I'm found, I'm deeead! Heeelp meee!!<end>"
KO: "히이악! 들키면 죽어어어. 사, 살려 줘어어어!!<end>"

## 1730005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>3どうするんじゃ、<value:$28>？<line-break>山賊とはいえ、放っておくのも<line-break>後味が悪い。行ってみるか？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>4どうするの、<value:$28>？<line-break>やっぱり、助けに行くの？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>5どうするんだ、<value:$28>？<line-break>やっぱ…。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>6あの人、噂の山賊だよね？　　<line-break>きっと、悪い人だよね？<line-break>　<line-break>でも…。<line-break>どうする、<value:$28>？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>13<value:$28>。<line-break>…わざわざ行く気では<line-break>ないだろうな？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>10どうすんだ、姉ちゃん？<line-break>まあ、姉ちゃんのことだから… <end><if><value:$01><equal>14何やら様子がおかしいようじゃな。<line-break>どうする、<value:$28>？<line-break>こやつのねぐらとやらに行ってみるか？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>15<value:$28>、どうしよう？<line-break>化け物だって…。<line-break>行ってみる？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>17<value:$28>！<line-break>まさかとは思うけど、助けに行こう！<line-break>な～んて思ってないでしょうね！？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>19ねぇねぇ、<value:$28>。<line-break>山賊さん、悪い人かもしれないけど、<line-break>助けに行ってあげなくちゃ？　ね？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>20行くのか？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>11どうするの、<value:$28>？<end><if><value:$01><equal>27山賊か？<line-break>様子がおかしい…。<line-break>どうする、<value:$28>？<end><if><value:$01><equal>26化け物とは…<line-break>こやつのねぐらとやらに行ってみるか？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>192どうするのですか？<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>　<line-break>私は貴方に従います。<end><if><value:$01><equal>193あの人って山賊ですよね。<line-break>でも…。<line-break><line-break><value:$28>さん、どうします？<end><if><value:$01><equal>194…言っておくぞ。<line-break>あそこにいる男は…危険すぎる。<end><if><value:$01><equal>191化け物だなんて本当かしら。<line-break>どうするの、<value:$28>。<line-break>山賊を助けにいくの？<end><if><value:$01><equal>190どうする？<line-break>あいつらは山賊だ。<line-break>それでも助けに行くのか？<end>どうするの、<value:$28>？<line-break>この人、噂になってる山賊の一味だよ。<line-break>それでも…。 <end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>3What shall we do, <value:$28>? Bandit or no, it wouldn't sit right to simply walk away. Shall we go?<end><if><value:$01><equal>4What are you going to do, <value:$28>? You're going to help, then?<end><if><value:$01><equal>5What are you going to do, <value:$28>? Just as I thought...<end><if><value:$01><equal>6That's the bandit everyone's been talking about, right? The bandit's probably a bad person, right? But... What should we do, <value:$28>?<end><if><value:$01><equal>13<value:$28>. You're not seriously planning to go, are you?<end><if><value:$01><equal>10What're you gonna do, sis? Though knowing you...<end><if><value:$01><equal>14Something seems strange. What shall we do, <value:$28>? Shall we go see this bandit's so-called lair?<end><if><value:$01><equal>15What should we do, <value:$28>? They say there's a monster... Shall we go and see?<end><if><value:$01><equal>17<value:$28>! Don't tell me you're thinking of going to help! You're not, are you?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19Hey, hey, <value:$28>. The bandit might be a bad person, but we have to go help, don't we? Right?<end><if><value:$01><equal>20You're going?<end><if><value:$01><equal>11What are you going to do, <value:$28>?<end><if><value:$01><equal>27A bandit? Something's not right... What will you do, <value:$28>?<end><if><value:$01><equal>26A monster... Shall we go see this bandit's so-called lair?<end><if><value:$01><equal>192What will you do, <value:$28>? I shall follow your lead.<end><if><value:$01><equal>193That's a bandit, isn't it? But still... What should we do, <value:$28>?<end><if><value:$01><equal>194I'm warning you. That man over there is... far too dangerous.<end><if><value:$01><equal>191I wonder if there really is a monster. What will you do, <value:$28>? Are you going to help the bandit?<end><if><value:$01><equal>190What will you do? Those people are bandits. Are you still going to help?<end>What are you going to do, <value:$28>? This person belongs to that bandit gang everyone's been talking about. Even so...<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>3어쩔 거냐, <value:$28>? 산적이라 해도 내버려 두면 찜찜하구나. 가 볼 텐가?<end><if><value:$01><equal>4어쩔 거야, <value:$28>? 역시 도우러 갈 거야?<end><if><value:$01><equal>5어쩔 거야, <value:$28>? 역시….<end><if><value:$01><equal>6저 사람, 소문난 산적이지? 분명 나쁜 사람이겠지? 그래도…. 어쩔래, <value:$28>?<end><if><value:$01><equal>13<value:$28>. …설마 일부러 갈 생각은 아니겠지?<end><if><value:$01><equal>10어쩔 거야, 누나? 뭐, 누나라면….<end><if><value:$01><equal>14아무래도 상태가 이상한 것 같구나. 어쩔 거냐, <value:$28>? 이 녀석의 소굴이라는 곳에 가 볼 텐가?<end><if><value:$01><equal>15<value:$28>, 어떡하지? 괴물이라는데…. 가 볼래?<end><if><value:$01><equal>17<value:$28>! 설마 하긴 하지만, 도우러 가자! 같은 생각 하는 건 아니겠죠!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>19있잖아 있잖아, <value:$28>. 산적 아저씨가 나쁜 사람일지도 모르지만 도우러 가줘야 하지 않을까? 응?<end><if><value:$01><equal>20갈 건가?<end><if><value:$01><equal>11어쩔 거야, <value:$28>?<end><if><value:$01><equal>27산적인가? 상태가 이상해…. 어쩔 거야, <value:$28>?<end><if><value:$01><equal>26괴물이라…. 이 녀석의 소굴이라는 곳에 가 볼 텐가?<end><if><value:$01><equal>192어떻게 하시겠습니까? <value:$28>. 저는 당신을 따르겠습니다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>193저 사람, 산적이죠. 그래도…. <value:$28>님, 어떻게 하실래요?<end><if><value:$01><equal>194…말해 두지. 저기 있는 남자는… 너무 위험하다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>191괴물이라니 정말일까. 어떻게 할 거야, <value:$28>. 산적을 도우러 갈 거야?<end><if><value:$01><equal>190어쩔래? 저 녀석들은 산적이야. 그래도 도우러 갈 건가?<end>어쩔 거야, <value:$28>? 이 사람, 소문난 산적 패거리야. 그래도….<end>"

## 1730006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "助けに行こう<end>"
EN: "Let's go help.<end>"
KO: "도우러 가자<end>"

## 1730007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "先をいそごう<end>"
EN: "Let's hurry on.<end>"
KO: "서두르자<end>"

## 1730008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>3うむ、お主なら<line-break>そう言うと思っておったわ。<line-break>では、参ろう。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>4うふふ、また面倒なことに<line-break>巻き込まれるのかも！<line-break>ちょっとだけ楽しみ！　きゃっ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>5だよなー。<line-break>ほんっと<value:$28>って<line-break>こういうのに首突っ込むよな。<line-break>よし。<line-break>じゃ、いっちょ行こうぜ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>6うん、そうだよね。<line-break>やっぱり放ってなんておけないよ。<line-break>さあ、行こう！　<value:$28>。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>13まったく…。<line-break>くだらん話だ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>10やっぱそうだよな。<line-break>じゃ、行こうぜ、姉ちゃん。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>14うむ、わかった。<line-break>では行こう。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>15そうだよね。<line-break>放ってなんておけないよ。<line-break>じゃあ、行こう！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>17あ～～、もう！<line-break>なんでそう下等な同情で行動するの？<line-break>信じられなくってよー！！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>19うん！<line-break>じゃ、行こう！！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>20そうか。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>11わかったわ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>27そうか。 <value:$28>が<line-break>言うのなら、行こう。<end><if><value:$01><equal>26うむ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>192わかりました。<line-break>助けに行きましょう。<end><if><value:$01><equal>193<value:$28>さんなら<line-break>そうおっしゃるって思っていました。<line-break>早く助けに行ってあげましょう！<end><if><value:$01><equal>194君なら、そう言うだろうな。<line-break>…わかった。行こう。<end><if><value:$01><equal>191<value:$28>って<line-break>ほんと、おせっかいね。<line-break>でも、そこがいいところなんだけどね。<line-break>じゃ、行きましょ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>190俺もお前と同じ意見だ。<line-break>急いで向かうぞ。<end>そうだよね。<line-break><value:$28>らしいと思うよ。<line-break>じゃ、行こう！　 <end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>3Hm. I thought that was what you'd say. Well then, let us be off.<end><if><value:$01><equal>4Hee hee! We might get caught up in another mess! I'm almost looking forward to it! Eek!<end><if><value:$01><equal>5Yeah, figures. <value:$28> really can't resist getting involved in things like this. All right, let's get going.<end><if><value:$01><equal>6Yeah, you're right. We can't just leave things like this. Come on, <value:$28>. Let's go!<end><if><value:$01><equal>13Honestly... What utter nonsense.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10Yeah, I thought so. Come on, sis. Let's go.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14Very well. Let us go.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15You're right. We can't just leave things like this. Come on, let's go!<end><if><value:$01><equal>17Oh, honestly! Why must you act out of such base pity? I simply cannot believe it!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19Right! Let's go!<end><if><value:$01><equal>20I see.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11All right.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27I see. If <value:$28> says so, then let us go.<end><if><value:$01><equal>26Mm.<end><if><value:$01><equal>192Understood. Let's go and help.<end><if><value:$01><equal>193I thought you'd say that, <value:$28>. Let's hurry and help!<end><if><value:$01><equal>194I thought that was what you'd say... All right, let's go.<end><if><value:$01><equal>191You really are a meddler, <value:$28>. But that's one of your good qualities. All right, let's go!<end><if><value:$01><equal>190I agree with you. Let's hurry.<end>You're right. That's just like you, <value:$28>. Come on, let's go!<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>3음, 너라면 그렇게 말할 줄 알았다. 그럼 가자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>4우후후, 또 귀찮은 일에 휘말릴지도! 조금 기대돼! 꺅.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5그렇지. 정말 <value:$28>는 이런 일에 끼어든다니까. 좋아, 그럼 한번 가 보자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>6응, 그렇지. 역시 내버려 둘 수 없어. 자, 가자! <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13정말…. 하찮은 이야기군.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10역시 그렇지. 그럼 가자, 누나.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14음, 알겠다. 그럼 가자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15그렇지. 내버려 둘 수 없어. 그럼 가자!<end><if><value:$01><equal>17아~~ 정말! 왜 그렇게 하등한 동정심으로 행동하는 거야? 믿을 수가 없어!!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19응! 그럼 가자!!<end><if><value:$01><equal>20그런가.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11알겠어.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27그런가. <value:$28>가 그렇게 말한다면 가자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>26음.<end><if><value:$01><equal>192알겠습니다. 구하러 가죠.<end><if><value:$01><equal>193<value:$28> 씨라면 그렇게 말씀하실 줄 알았어요. 빨리 구하러 가요!<end><if><value:$01><equal>194너라면 그렇게 말하겠지. …알았다. 가자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>191<value:$28>는 정말 참견쟁이네. 그래도 그게 좋은 점이지만. 그럼 가자!<end><if><value:$01><equal>190나도 너와 같은 생각이다. 서둘러 간다.<end>그렇지. <value:$28>답다고 생각해. 그럼 가자!<end>"

## 1730009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふふ。素直に差し出しておけば、<line-break>砦ごと失うこともなかったのに。<line-break>選択を誤ったね、山賊さんたち？<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh. If you'd simply handed it over, you wouldn't have lost your fortress as well. You made the wrong choice, didn't you, bandits?<end>"
KO: "후후후. 얌전히 내놓았더라면 요새까지 잃지는 않았을 텐데. 선택을 잘못했군, 산적 여러분?<end>"

## 1730010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぎゃっ！<end>"
EN: "Gah!<end>"
KO: "꺅!<end>"

## 1730011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぐはっ！<end>"
EN: "Gah!<end>"
KO: "크헉!<end>"

## 1730012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "女性だからといって、<line-break>逃がしてあげたりはしないよ。<line-break>それは不平等というものだからね？<end>"
EN: "I won't let you escape just because you're a woman. That wouldn't be fair, would it?<end>"
KO: "여자라고 해서 놓아주지는 않을 거야. 그건 불평등이라는 거잖아?<end>"

## 1730013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぎゃー！<end>"
EN: "Aaaah!<end>"
KO: "꺄악!<end>"

## 1730014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "坊や。いくつだ？<end>"
EN: "How old are you, boy?<end>"
KO: "꼬마야. 몇 살이지?<end>"

## 1730015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ご、ご、５さ、い…。<end>"
EN: "F-five... years old...<end>"
KO: "다, 다, 다섯 사, 살….<end>"

## 1730016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうかい。<line-break>俺が最初に命を奪われかけたのは、<line-break>６つのときだ。だから…<end>"
EN: "I see. I was six the first time someone tried to take my life. So...<end>"
KO: "그런가. 내가 처음 목숨을 빼앗길 뻔한 건 여섯 살 때였어. 그러니…<end>"

## 1730017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それまでは守られる権利がある。<line-break>坊や。君の命は奪わない。<end>"
EN: "Until then, you have the right to be protected. I won't take your life, boy.<end>"
KO: "그때까지는 보호받을 권리가 있어. 꼬마야. 네 목숨은 빼앗지 않겠다.<end>"

## 1730018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "死ねえっ！<end>"
EN: "Die!<end>"
KO: "죽어라!<end>"

## 1730019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おっと、危ない。<line-break>その程度の腕で<line-break>刃物を振り回してほしくないな。<end>"
EN: "Whoa, careful. I wish you wouldn't go swinging a blade around when that's the best you can do.<end>"
KO: "어이쿠, 위험해. 그 정도 실력으로 칼을 휘두르지는 않았으면 좋겠군.<end>"

## 1730020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺は君らがこの前、旅の坊主から奪った<line-break>貪欲の盾を探しているんだけど？<line-break>君は何も言わないで死ぬのかな？<end>"
EN: "I'm looking for the Shield of Greed you stole from that traveling monk the other day. Are you going to die without telling me anything?<end>"
KO: "난 너희가 전에 여행하던 중에게서 빼앗은 탐욕의 방패를 찾고 있는데? 넌 아무 말도 하지 않고 죽을 셈인가?<end>"

## 1730021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぜっ、ゼグナだ。<line-break>ゼグナの坑道に隠した！<end>"
EN: "Z-Zegna! We hid it in the Zegna mine!<end>"
KO: "제, 제그나다. 제그나 갱도에 숨겼어!<end>"

## 1730023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぐぇ！<end>"
EN: "Guh!<end>"
KO: "끄엑!<end>"

## 1730024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冒険者か。とんだところに来たね。<line-break>６才以上の奴は俺の前に現れた瞬間、<line-break>死が確定するんだ。<end>"
EN: "An adventurer? What an unfortunate place to wander into. Anyone six or older is doomed the moment they appear before me.<end>"
KO: "모험자인가. 엉뚱한 데 왔군. 여섯 살 이상인 놈은 내 앞에 나타나는 순간 죽음이 확정이야.<end>"

## 1730025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺はシスティーナの伝道師、<line-break>黒の祈りのジュサプブロス。<line-break>死と絶望を広めるのが仕事だ。<end>"
EN: "I am a missionary of Sistina, Jusabros of the Black Prayer. My work is to spread death and despair.<end>"
KO: "나는 시스티나의 전도사, 검은 기도의 주사프브로스. 죽음과 절망을 퍼뜨리는 게 내 일이다.<end>"

## 1730026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君のために俺は剣で祈ろう。<line-break>死と絶望の甘美な歌声が<line-break>君を永遠に包みますように、とね。<line-break>じゃあ、さよなら。<end>"
EN: "For you, I shall pray with my sword: may the sweet song of death and despair enfold you for eternity. And now, goodbye.<end>"
KO: "너를 위해 나는 검으로 기도하지. 죽음과 절망의 달콤한 노랫소리가 영원히 너를 감싸길 바라면서. 그럼 잘 가.<end>"

## 1730027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほう。やるね。<end>"
EN: "Oh? Not bad.<end>"
KO: "호오. 제법인데.<end>"

## 1730028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そこの男！　動くな！<end>"
EN: "You there! Don't move!<end>"
KO: "거기 남자! 움직이지 마!<end>"

## 1730029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おや？　新手かな？<end>"
EN: "Oh? More company?<end>"
KO: "어라? 새로 온 녀석인가?<end>"

## 1730030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前……<line-break>エルフ…<line-break>ダークエルフか。<end>"
EN: "You... An Elf... a Dark Elf.<end>"
KO: "너…… 엘프… 다크 엘프인가.<end>"

## 1730031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あーあ、せっかく、隠していたのに。<line-break>やっぱり、同門にはわかるんだね。<line-break>　<line-break>それにしてもこれは不利すぎるな。<line-break>じゃあね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, and I went to all that trouble to keep it hidden. I suppose someone from the same order would notice. Still, I'm at far too much of a disadvantage here. See you.<end>"
KO: "아아, 모처럼 숨기고 있었는데. 역시 같은 문파 사람한테는 들키는구나. 그래도 이건 너무 불리하네. 그럼 안녕.<end>"

## 1730032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "逃げた？<line-break>…まぁ、いいわ。<line-break>今は少しでも被害を抑えないと。<end>"
EN: "Escaped? Oh well. For now, we have to keep the damage to a minimum.<end>"
KO: "도망쳤어? …뭐, 됐어. 지금은 조금이라도 피해를 줄여야 해.<end>"

## 1730033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふぅ。<line-break>生き残りはこれで全部かの？<end>"
EN: "Phew. Is this everyone who survived?<end>"
KO: "후우. 살아남은 건 이게 전부인가?<end>"

## 1730034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えぇ。生き残ったのは子供だけ。<line-break>それも、小さな子ばかり。<line-break>あとは全滅だわ。<end>"
EN: "Yes. Only the children survived, and every one of them is very young. Everyone else was wiped out.<end>"
KO: "그래. 살아남은 건 아이들뿐이야. 그것도 어린아이들뿐. 나머지는 전멸했어.<end>"

## 1730035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "小僧。<line-break>貪欲の盾は、どこだ？<end>"
EN: "Boy. Where is the Shield of Greed?<end>"
KO: "꼬마. 탐욕의 방패는 어디 있지?<end>"

## 1730036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひ…う、う…えぐ。<line-break>うわーん。<end>"
EN: "Hic... uh... sob... Waaaaah!<end>"
KO: "흐…으, 으…흑. 으아앙.<end>"

## 1730037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼリグ！<line-break>何をしているの！<line-break>こんな小さな子を泣かせないで。<end>"
EN: "Zelig! What are you doing? Don't make such a little child cry.<end>"
KO: "젤리그! 뭐 하는 거야! 이런 어린애를 울리지 마.<end>"

## 1730039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "貪欲の盾は、どこにある？<line-break>言え。<end>"
EN: "Where is the Shield of Greed? Tell me.<end>"
KO: "탐욕의 방패는 어디 있지? 말해.<end>"

## 1730040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぜ、ゼグナ…って、<line-break>おじちゃんが…。<end>"
EN: "Z-Zegna... That's what the man said...<end>"
KO: "제, 제그나…라는 아저씨가….<end>"

## 1730041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "盾の在処はわかった。<line-break>ゆくぞ。<end>"
EN: "We know where the shield is. Let's go.<end>"
KO: "방패가 있는 곳은 알아냈다. 가자.<end>"

## 1730042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゆくぞって…ちょっと待って。<line-break>この子たちを置いては行けないわ。<end>"
EN: "Go? Wait a moment. We can't leave these children here.<end>"
KO: "가자니… 잠깐만. 이 아이들을 두고 갈 수는 없어.<end>"

## 1730043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…今は、盾の方が<line-break>優先する。<end>"
EN: "Right now, the shield takes priority.<end>"
KO: "…지금은 방패가 우선이다.<end>"

## 1730044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それは、盾は重要だけど…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>仕方ないわ。<line-break>ドルドラム。<end>"
EN: "The shield is important, but... Fine. Doldrum.<end>"
KO: "그건, 방패는 중요하지만…. 어쩔 수 없네. 돌드람.<end>"

## 1730045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今度は何かな？<end>"
EN: "What is it this time?<end>"
KO: "이번엔 뭐지?<end>"

## 1730046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私とゼリグで盾の所在を<line-break>報告に行くわ。あなたは<line-break>この子たちのことをお願い。<end>"
EN: "Zelig and I will go report the shield's location. I want you to look after these children.<end>"
KO: "나와 젤리그가 방패의 소재를 보고하러 갈게. 당신은 이 아이들을 부탁해.<end>"

## 1730047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やれやれ。<line-break>わしの助けはいらんのと違ったかな？<end>"
EN: "Good grief. I thought you had no need of my help?<end>"
KO: "이런 이런. 내 도움은 필요 없다지 않았나?<end>"

## 1730048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>…わかったわ。<line-break>もう頼まない。<end>"

KO: "………………. …알겠어. 이제 부탁하지 않을게.<end>"

## 1730049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>。あなたに<line-break>こんなことを頼める義理じゃないけど、<line-break>他に方法がないの。<line-break>ロセンにいるカルラの所まで行って、<line-break>ここの子供たちを収容するよう言って。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>. I have no right to ask this of you, but there's no other way. Go to Karula in Rocen and tell her to take these children in.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>. 이런 부탁을 할 처지는 아니지만 다른 방법이 없어. 로센의 칼라에게 가서 여기 아이들을 받아 달라고 전해 줘.<end>"

## 1730050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "神器を争奪していた相手に、<line-break>ものを頼むとは<line-break>器用な奴だな。<end>"
EN: "Asking a favor of someone you were fighting over a sacred artifact takes remarkable skill.<end>"
KO: "신기를 놓고 다투던 상대에게 부탁까지 하다니 재주도 좋네.<end>"

## 1730051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんですって！<end>"
EN: "What did you say?!<end>"
KO: "뭐라고요!<end>"

## 1730052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "怒るな。<line-break>我もそのぐらい器用ならばと<line-break>思ったまでだ。<end>"
EN: "Don't be angry. I only wished I possessed such skill myself.<end>"
KO: "화내지 마라. 나도 그 정도로 재주가 좋았으면 한다고 생각했을 뿐이다.<end>"

## 1730053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やれやれ。<end>"
EN: "Good grief.<end>"
KO: "이런, 이런.<end>"

## 1730054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "戦争になれば、孤児が増える。　　　<line-break>お前さん、そのすべてを<line-break>拾って歩く気かね？<line-break>悪いが、わしらには時間がないのだ。<line-break>ベルゼーヴァ卿も<line-break>次はないと仰せだしな。<end>"
EN: "War creates orphans. Do you mean to go around taking in every last one? I'm sorry, but we have no time. Lord Beelzeva has said there will be no next chance.<end>"
KO: "전쟁이 나면 고아가 늘어난다. 자네, 그 모두를 주워 다닐 셈인가? 미안하지만 우리에겐 시간이 없다. 벨제바 경도 다음은 없다고 하셨으니.<end>"

## 1730055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "先に行かせてもらうぞ。<end>"
EN: "I'll go on ahead.<end>"
KO: "먼저 가겠다.<end>"

## 1730056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>。<line-break>どう？　引き受けてくれるの？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... Will you do it?<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>. 어때? 맡아 줄 거야?<end>"

## 1730057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "引き受ける<end>"
EN: "Accept<end>"
KO: "수락한다<end>"

## 1730058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "引き受けない<end>"

KO: "맡지 않는다<end>"

## 1730059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私だって、孤児をすべて　　　　　　<line-break>救えるなんて、思ってない。<line-break>　<line-break>だけど、目の前に苦しんでいる者が<line-break>いて、それを救える方法があるのに、<line-break>救わないでいるなんて、できない。<end>"
EN: "I don't think I can save every orphan. But when someone is suffering right in front of me and there's a way to help, I can't just stand by and do nothing.<end>"
KO: "나도 모든 고아를 구할 수 있다고는 생각하지 않아. 하지만 눈앞에 고통받는 사람이 있고 구할 방법도 있는데 구하지 않는 건 못 하겠어.<end>"

## 1730060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "とりあえず、<line-break>助けてくれて、ありがとう。<line-break>　<line-break>これを見せれば、カルラは<line-break>理解するはずよ。じゃ、よろしく。<line-break>私はドルドラムたちを追うから。<end>"
EN: "For now, thank you for helping. Show this to Karula and she should understand. I'm counting on you. I have to catch up with Doldrum and the others.<end>"
KO: "일단 구해 줘서 고마워. 이걸 보여 주면 칼라는 이해할 거야. 그럼 부탁해. 나는 돌드람 일행을 쫓을 테니까.<end>"

## 1730061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "言っておきますけど、アタクシは絶対に<line-break>死んでも、行かなくてよー。行くなら、<line-break>アタクシをここに残して行って！<end>"
EN: "Let me make one thing clear: I absolutely refuse to go, even if it kills me! If you're going, leave me here!<end>"
KO: "말해 두겠는데, 이 몸은 죽어도 절대 안 갈 거야! 갈 거면 이 몸은 여기 두고 가!<end>"

## 1730062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょ、ちょっと、待ちなさいよー！<line-break>高貴なアタクシにこの人間の子供たちの<line-break>お守りをさせる気？　非常識よー！<end>"
EN: "H-hey, wait! You mean to make a noble Elf like me babysit these human children? How outrageous!<end>"
KO: "자, 잠깐만 기다려! 고귀한 이 몸한테 이 인간 애들을 돌보라는 거야? 비상식적이야!<end>"

## 1730063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>4Hee hee! We already have our hands full, and now we've taken on another mess. You must simply adore trouble, <value:$28>! Eek!<end>... What a nuisance.<end>"

## 1730065

## 1730066

## 1730068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>3なぜ、引き受けぬ？<line-break>オイフェと敵対しているからか？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>5どうして、引き受けねぇんだよ！<line-break>オイフェと敵対しているからかよ？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>6どうして、引き受けないの？<line-break>オイフェと敵対していることなんて<line-break>今は関係ないじゃない。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>13……何をしたいのだか。<line-break>これではまったくの無駄足だ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>10なんで引き受けないんだよ？<line-break>姉ちゃんなら当然引き受けると<line-break>思ってたのに。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>14なぜ、引き受けんかったんじゃ。<line-break>誰に頼まれようと、<line-break>やるべきことがあるはずじゃぞ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>15どうして、引き受けないの？<line-break>困ってる人を放っておくなんて<line-break>死んだお爺ちゃんなら怒ると思うな。<end><if><value:$01><equal>19どうしてぇ？<line-break>あの子たち、かわいそうだよぉ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>11どうして引き受けないの？<line-break>敵対しているから…？<end><if><value:$01><equal>27…引き受けなかった理由は<line-break>オイフェと敵対しているからか？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>26なぜ、引き受けなかったのじゃ？<line-break>あの子たちはどうなるのじゃ…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>193どうして引き受けないんですか！？<line-break>あの子たちがかわいそうです…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>191なぜ引き受けないの！？<line-break>彼女と敵対してるから？<line-break>そんなの関係ないわ！<line-break>あなたのこと見損なったわ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>190まさか、放っていくのか？<line-break>自分だけの都合で<line-break>あの子たちを見捨ててしまうのか？<end>うーん、なんだかなぁ…。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>これって、誰に頼まれたのか抜きで、<line-break>やらなきゃいけないことだと<line-break>思うけどなぁ、ぼく。 <end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>3Why did you refuse? Is it because you and Aoife are enemies?<end><if><value:$01><equal>5Why the hell did you refuse? Just because you're enemies with Aoife?<end><if><value:$01><equal>6Why won't you do it? Being enemies with Aoife has nothing to do with this.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13What are you trying to accomplish? This entire journey has been for nothing.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10Why didn't you agree? I was sure you would, sis.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14Why did you refuse? No matter who asks, there are some things that simply must be done.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15Why won't you do it? My late grandfather would be angry if we abandoned people in need.<end><if><value:$01><equal>19Why? Those poor children...<end><if><value:$01><equal>11Why won't you do it? Is it because you're enemies...?<end><if><value:$01><equal>27Did you refuse because you and Aoife are enemies?<end><if><value:$01><equal>26Why did you refuse? What will become of those children...?<end><if><value:$01><equal>193Why won't you do it?! Those poor children...<end><if><value:$01><equal>191Why won't you do it?! Because you and Aoife are enemies? That has nothing to do with this! I thought better of you.<end><if><value:$01><equal>190You're not really going to leave them, are you? Will you abandon those children for your own convenience?<end>Hmm... I don't know about this. Regardless of who asked, I think it's something we ought to do.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>3왜 맡지 않는 게냐? 오이페와 적대하고 있어서냐?<end><if><value:$01><equal>5왜 안 맡는 거야! 오이페와 적대하고 있어서냐고?<end><if><value:$01><equal>6왜 안 맡는 거야? 오이페와 적대하고 있는 건 지금 상관없잖아.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13……대체 뭘 하려는 건지. 이래서는 완전히 헛걸음이다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10왜 안 맡는 거야? 누나라면 당연히 맡을 줄 알았는데.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14왜 맡지 않은 게냐. 누구에게 부탁받았든 해야 할 일이 있는 법이다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15왜 안 맡는 거야? 곤란한 사람을 내버려 두면 돌아가신 할아버지가 화내실 것 같아.<end><if><value:$01><equal>19왜에? 저 아이들 불쌍해애.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11왜 안 맡는 거야? 적대하고 있어서…?<end><if><value:$01><equal>27…맡지 않은 이유는 오이페와 적대하고 있어서인가?<end><if><value:$01><equal>26왜 맡지 않은 게냐? 저 아이들은 어떻게 되는 게냐….<end><if><value:$01><equal>193왜 안 맡으시는 거예요!? 저 아이들이 불쌍해요….<end><if><value:$01><equal>191왜 안 맡는 거야!? 그녀와 적대하고 있어서? 그런 건 상관없어! 너한테 실망했어.<end><if><value:$01><equal>190설마 내버려 두고 가는 거냐? 자기 사정만으로 저 아이들을 버리는 건가?<end>음, 뭐랄까…. 이건 누가 부탁했는지는 상관없이 해야 하는 일이라고 생각하는데, 나는.<end>"

## 1730069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>3そうか。いや、お主がそれで<line-break>正しいと思うのならそれでよい。<line-break>では、先を急ごう。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>4意外だわ。面倒なことに<line-break>首突っ込まずにすむなんて。<line-break>ちょっと肩すかしかも！　きゃっ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>5あ、そ、そうなんだ？<line-break>わかった、先を急ごうぜ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>6えーーーっ！？<line-break>う、ううん。なんでもないよ。<line-break>そっか。じゃ、行こう。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>13うむ、行こう。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>10あれ？　意外だな。<line-break>まぁ、いいか。<line-break>じゃ、先を急ごう。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>14ふむ、放っておくというのじゃな？<line-break>わかった。では、先を急ごうかの。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>15え？　行かないの？<line-break>…いや、別に何でもないんだ。<line-break>そうだね、先を急ごう。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>17当然よ。少しはまともな思考が<line-break>できるようになったわね。<line-break>それでは、出発よー！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>19ええーっ！？<line-break>山賊さんたち、放っていくの？<line-break>　<line-break>…ううん、なんでもない。<line-break>行こう、<value:$28>。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>20そうか。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>11そう、わかったわ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>27そう…か。<line-break>行こう、<value:$28>。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>26そう…か。<line-break>では、先を急ごう。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>192行かないのですね。<line-break>わかりました。先を急ぎましょう。<end><if><value:$01><equal>193え！？<line-break>い…いえ、何でもないです。<line-break>ちょっと意外だっただけで…。<line-break>先を急ぎましょう。<end><if><value:$01><equal>194そうか、なら先を急ごう。<end><if><value:$01><equal>191意外だわ。<line-break><value:$28>ならてっきり…<line-break>　<line-break>まあ、いいわ。<line-break>先を急ぎましょ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>190放っておくのか…？<line-break>…わかった。<line-break>先を急ごう。<end>そっか。<line-break>うん、じゃ、先を急ごう。 <end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>3I see. No, if you believe this is right, then so be it. Let us hurry on.<end><if><value:$01><equal>4What a surprise. We get to avoid poking our noses into another mess. It's almost a letdown! Eek!<end><if><value:$01><equal>5O-oh, really? All right, let's hurry on.<end><if><value:$01><equal>6Whaaat?! N-no, it's nothing. Oh. Then let's go.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13Very well. Let us go.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10Huh? Didn't expect that. Oh well. Let's hurry on.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14Hm. So you mean to leave them? Very well. Let us hurry on.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15What? We're not going? No, it's nothing. All right, let's hurry on.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17Naturally. At last, you're beginning to think sensibly. Now, onward!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19What?! We're leaving the bandits? No, it's nothing. Let's go, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>20I see.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11I see. All right.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27I... see. Let's go, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>26I... see. Then let us hurry on.<end><if><value:$01><equal>192So you aren't going. Understood. Let us hurry on.<end><if><value:$01><equal>193What?! N-no, it's nothing. I was just a little surprised... Let's hurry on.<end><if><value:$01><equal>194I see. Then let's hurry on.<end><if><value:$01><equal>191I'm surprised. With you, <value:$28>, I was sure... Never mind. Let's hurry on.<end><if><value:$01><equal>190You're leaving them...? All right. Let's hurry on.<end>I see. All right, then. Let's hurry on.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>3그런가. 아니, 네가 그게 옳다고 생각한다면 됐다. 그럼 서두르자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>4의외네. 귀찮은 일에 끼어들지 않아도 되다니. 조금 맥빠질지도! 꺅!<end><if><value:$01><equal>5아, 그, 그래? 알았어, 서두르자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>6에에엣!? 아, 아니. 아무것도 아니야. 그렇구나. 그럼 가자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13음, 가자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10어라? 의외네. 뭐, 됐어. 그럼 서두르자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14흠, 내버려 둔다는 게냐? 알겠다. 그럼 서두르자꾸나.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15어? 안 가는 거야? …아니, 아무것도 아니야. 그래, 서두르자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17당연하지. 조금은 제대로 생각할 수 있게 됐네. 그럼 출발이야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19에에엣!? 산적들을 두고 가는 거야? …아니, 아무것도 아니야. 가자, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>20그런가.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11그래, 알았어.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27그런…가. 가자, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>26그런…가. 그럼 서두르자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>192안 가시는군요. 알겠습니다. 서두르죠.<end><if><value:$01><equal>193네!? 아… 아니, 아무것도 아니에요. 조금 의외였을 뿐이라…. 서두르죠.<end><if><value:$01><equal>194그런가, 그럼 서두르자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>191의외네. <value:$28>라면 틀림없이… 뭐, 됐어. 서두르자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>190내버려 두는 건가…? …알았다. 서두르자.<end>그렇구나. 응, 그럼 서두르자.<end>"

## 1730070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様。<line-break>どうぞお通りください。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, please go right in.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님. 지나가십시오.<end>"

## 1730071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あら、<value:$28>じゃん。<line-break>元気にしてた？<line-break>　<line-break>え？　オイフェから手紙？<line-break>ふーん。どれどれ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, hey, <value:$28>. How've you been? Huh? A letter from Aoife? Hmm. Let's see.<end>"
KO: "어머, <value:$28>잖아. 잘 지냈어? 응? 오이페에게 편지? 흐음. 어디 보자.<end>"

## 1730072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どこで何をやってるかと思ったら。　<line-break>相変わらず、甘ちゃんね。<line-break>…ま、そこがかわいいんだけどね。<line-break>わかったわ。大したことじゃないし、<line-break>他ならぬ<value:$28>の<line-break>頼みだからね。聞いといたげる。<end>"
EN: "So that's what you've been up to. You're still such a soft touch. That's part of your charm, though. All right. It's no great trouble, and since it's you asking, <value:$28>, I'll see to it.<end>"
KO: "어디서 뭘 하나 했더니. 여전히 물러 터졌네. …뭐, 그런 점이 귀엽긴 하지만. 알았어. 별일도 아니고, 다른 누구도 아닌 <value:$28>의 부탁이니까. 물어봐 줄게.<end>"

## 1730073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、あんまりオイフェの方ばっかり<line-break>くっついていっちゃヤだよ。<line-break>あたしの方もちゃんとかまってよね？<end>"
EN: "But don't go spending all your time tagging along after Aoife. You've got to pay some attention to me too, you know?<end>"
KO: "하지만 너무 오이페 쪽에만 붙어 다니면 싫어. 나한테도 제대로 신경 써 줘, 알았지?<end>"

## 1730074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃ、またね。<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "See you around, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그럼 또 보자, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1730075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オイフェ殿からの書状だと？<line-break>しばらく、待て。<end>"
EN: "A letter from Lady Aoife? Wait a moment.<end>"
KO: "오이페 공의 서신이라고? 잠시 기다려라.<end>"

## 1730076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カルラ様がお会いになるそうだ。<line-break>失礼のないようにな。<end>"
EN: "Lady Karula will see you. Mind your manners.<end>"
KO: "칼라 님께서 만나시겠답니다. 실례 없도록 하십시오.<end>"

## 1730077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "元気ぃ？　ロセンの鐘楼以来よね？<line-break>あの時、言ったよね。<line-break>部下になるか、死ぬかだって。<line-break>そして、あんたは、<line-break>あたしの部下にはならなかった。<line-break>つまり死を選択したのよね？<end>"
EN: "How've you been? We haven't met since the bell tower in Rocen, right? I told you then: become my subordinate or die. And you didn't become my subordinate, which means you chose death, right?<end>"
KO: "잘 지냈어? 로센 종루 이후네? 그때 말했지. 부하가 되거나 죽으라고. 그리고 넌 내 부하가 되지 않았어. 즉 죽음을 택한 거지?<end>"

## 1730078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いくわ！！　　　　　　　　　　　<end>"
EN: "Here I come!!<end>"
KO: "간다!!<end>"

## 1730079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "結構、やるじゃん。<line-break>んで、聞くけどさ。<line-break>あたしのこと、どうしたいの？<end>"
EN: "Not bad. So let me ask: what do you want to do with me?<end>"
KO: "제법 하는데. 그래서 묻겠는데, 나를 어떻게 하고 싶은 거야?<end>"

## 1730080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "死んでもらう<end>"
EN: "I want you dead.<end>"
KO: "죽어 줘야겠다<end>"

## 1730081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "別にどうもしない<end>"
EN: "Nothing in particular.<end>"
KO: "딱히 아무것도 안 한다<end>"

## 1730082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それは、ちょっち<line-break>イヤかな。<line-break>遠慮させてもらうわ。<end>"
EN: "I'd rather not. I'll pass.<end>"
KO: "그건 조금 싫은데. 사양할게.<end>"

## 1730083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "つまみだせ！<end>"
EN: "Throw them out!<end>"
KO: "끌어내!<end>"

## 1730084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はっ！<end>"
EN: "At once!<end>"
KO: "핫!<end>"

## 1730085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふぅん、やさしいんだ。<line-break>…っていうか、<line-break>あたしのこと好きなんでしょ？　<line-break>だったら、あたしの配下に入るのが<line-break>一番じゃない？　<line-break>どぉ？　悪いようにはしないわよ。<end>"
EN: "Hmm, so you're kind. Or maybe you just like me? In that case, wouldn't it be best to join my ranks? What do you say? I won't treat you badly.<end>"
KO: "흐음, 상냥하네. …아니, 나 좋아하는 거지? 그렇다면 내 휘하에 들어오는 게 제일 낫지 않아? 어때? 나쁘게는 안 할게.<end>"

## 1730086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冗談だってば。　　　　<line-break>本気にしないで。<line-break>　<line-break>じゃ、用が終わったなら<line-break>出てってくんないかな？<end>"
EN: "I'm joking. Don't take it seriously. Now, if that's all, could you leave?<end>"
KO: "농담이라니까. 진지하게 받아들이지 마. 그럼 볼일 끝났으면 나가 줄래?<end>"

## 1730087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なあんだ。　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>またそんな答えか。<line-break>つまんないわね。<line-break>悪いけど、出てってくれる？<line-break>自分のものになんないのに<line-break>いつまでも顔見ていたくないの。<end>"
EN: "Aw, that answer again? Boring. Sorry, but could you leave? If you won't be mine, I don't want to keep looking at you.<end>"
KO: "뭐야. 또 그런 대답이야? 재미없네. 미안한데 나가 줄래? 내 것이 되지도 않을 사람 얼굴을 계속 보고 싶진 않아.<end>"

## 1730088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、子供たちの件だけどさ。<line-break>責任もって引き受けとくから。<line-break>それは、心配しないでいいよ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, about the children. I'll take responsibility for them, so you don't need to worry.<end>"
KO: "아, 아이들 일 말인데. 책임지고 맡아 둘게. 그건 걱정 안 해도 돼.<end>"

## 1730089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バカじゃない？<line-break>考えなしでこんなことになってさ。<line-break>ホント、バカ。<line-break>でも、あたし、バカも好きよ。<line-break>ま、オイフェの顔も立てて<line-break>見逃してあげるわ。行きなさい。<end>"
EN: "Are you stupid? Look where acting without thinking got you. Seriously, what an idiot. But I like idiots too. Well, for Aoife's sake, I'll let you go. Go on.<end>"
KO: "바보 아니야? 생각도 없이 이 꼴이 되고. 정말 바보. 하지만 나, 바보도 좋아해. 뭐, 오이페 체면도 세워 줄 겸 봐줄게. 가.<end>"

## 1730090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、子供たちの件もひきうけとくわ。<line-break>ま、恩に感じて、も少し強くなって<line-break>あたしを楽しませてよね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, and I'll take care of the children too. Be grateful, get a little stronger, and come back when you can entertain me.<end>"
KO: "아, 아이들 일도 맡아 둘게. 뭐, 은혜라고 생각하고 좀 더 강해져서 나를 즐겁게 해 줘.<end>"

## 1730091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オイフェ殿からの書状だと？<line-break>しばらく、待て。<end>"
EN: "A letter from Lady Aoife? Wait a moment.<end>"
KO: "오이페 공의 서신이라고? 잠시 기다려라.<end>"

## 1730092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カルラ様がお会いになるそうだ。<line-break>失礼のないようにな。<end>"
EN: "Lady Karula will see you. Mind your manners.<end>"
KO: "칼라 님께서 만나시겠답니다. 실례 없도록 하십시오.<end>"

## 1730093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんたが、オイフェのおつかいね。<line-break>あたしがカルラ。<line-break>ディンガルの東方攻略軍司令。<line-break>でもって青竜将軍。<line-break>ヨロシクぅ。<line-break>じゃ、手紙、見せて。<end>"
EN: "So you're Aoife's messenger. I'm Karula. Commander of Dyneskal's Eastern Campaign Army. And the Azure Dragon General. Nice to meet you. Now, show me the letter.<end>"
KO: "네가 오이페의 심부름꾼이구나. 내가 칼라. 딩갈 동방공략군 사령관, 그리고 청룡장군. 잘 부탁해. 자, 편지 보여 줘.<end>"

## 1730094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あら、<value:$28>じゃん。<line-break>オイフェから手紙預かってるんだって？<line-break>どれどれ…<end>"
EN: "Oh, hey, <value:$28>. I hear you're carrying a letter from Aoife. Let's have a look...<end>"
KO: "어라, <value:$28>잖아. 오이페에게서 편지를 받아 왔다고? 어디 보자….<end>"

## 1730095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふーん。　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>あんたもめんどくさいこと<line-break>やってるみたいだね。<line-break>ま、いいわ。大したことじゃないし。<line-break>ここは、どーんと引き受けちゃう。<line-break>オイフェの顔も立ててやらないとね。<end>"
EN: "Hmm. Looks like you're dealing with some troublesome business. Well, all right. It's nothing major. I'll take the whole thing on. Have to oblige Aoife, too.<end>"
KO: "흐음. 너도 귀찮은 일을 하고 있는 모양이네. 뭐, 좋아. 대단한 일도 아니고. 여기선 시원하게 맡아 주지. 오이페 체면도 세워 줘야 하니까.<end>"

## 1730096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "用件がそれだけなら、<line-break>失礼させてもらっちゃおうかな。<line-break>こう見えて、実は忙しいのよね。<end>"
EN: "If that's all you needed, I think I'll be on my way. Believe it or not, I'm actually quite busy.<end>"
KO: "용건이 그게 전부라면 이만 실례할까. 이래 보여도 사실 바쁘거든.<end>"

## 1730097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんか、あんたとは<line-break>また会えそうな気がするなぁ。<line-break>そんじゃ、またね。<end>"
EN: "I've got a feeling we'll meet again. See you around.<end>"
KO: "왠지 너랑은 또 만날 것 같은 느낌이 드네. 그럼 또 보자.<end>"

## 1730098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんか、あんたとは<line-break>また会えそうな気がするなぁ。<line-break>そんじゃ、またね。<end>"
EN: "I've got a feeling we'll meet again. See you around.<end>"
KO: "왠지 너랑은 또 만날 것 같은 느낌이 드네. 그럼 또 보자.<end>"

## 1730099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "シャリ、失敗したようだな。<end>"
EN: "Shalli, it seems you've failed.<end>"
KO: "샤리, 실패한 모양이군.<end>"

## 1730100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、それは…。<end>"
EN: "Th-that's...<end>"
KO: "그, 그건….<end>"

## 1730101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あのお方はお怒りだ。<end>"
EN: "Our master is furious.<end>"
KO: "그분은 화가 나셨다.<end>"

## 1730102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "い、命ばかりは。<line-break>つ、次こそ必ず…。<end>"
EN: "P-please, spare my life! N-next time, I swear...<end>"
KO: "모, 목숨만은. 다, 다음엔 반드시….<end>"

## 1730103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "失敗は死でつぐなえ。<line-break>シャリ。<end>"
EN: "Atone for your failure with death, Shalli.<end>"
KO: "실패는 죽음으로 갚아라. 샤리.<end>"

## 1730104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジュサぷー！<line-break>助けに来てくれたんだ！<end>"
EN: "Jusapoo! You came to save me!<end>"
KO: "주사푸! 구하러 와 줬구나!<end>"

## 1730105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "...でも、もうこれで君も<line-break>あのお方から追われる身だね。<end>"
EN: "...But now our master will be after you too.<end>"
KO: "…하지만 이제 너도 그분에게 쫓기는 몸이겠네.<end>"

## 1730106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誰がジュサぷーだ。<line-break>それに、あのお方って誰だよ？　　<line-break>　<line-break>俺たちの計画は俺と君、<line-break>ゾフォルの爺様に、救世主。<line-break>たった４人で切り盛りしてんだ。<end>"
EN: "Don't call me Jusapoo. And who is this master, anyway? There are only four of us running this operation: you, me, old man Zofor, and the Savior.<end>"
KO: "누가 주사푸야. 그리고 그분이라니 누구야? 우리 계획은 나와 너, 조포르 할아버지, 구세주. 단 네 명이 꾸려 가는 거라고.<end>"

## 1730107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君も爺様の作った人形を使って、<line-break>くだらない芝居をやっている暇が<line-break>あったら、もっと働けよ。<end>"
EN: "If you've got time to put on stupid little shows with the dolls old man Zofor made, you should be working harder.<end>"
KO: "너도 할아버지가 만든 인형으로 시시한 연극이나 할 시간이 있으면 좀 더 일해.<end>"

## 1730108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょっと、遊んでみただけさ。<line-break>案外、固いこと言うんだな。<end>"
EN: "I was just having a little fun. You're surprisingly uptight.<end>"
KO: "조금 놀아 본 것뿐이야. 의외로 딱딱한 소리를 하네.<end>"

## 1730109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "空中都市は大失敗だったな。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>闇の聖母の封印を守る巫女が１人も<line-break>死ななかったばかりか、逆に１人が<line-break>封印の守りに入ってしまった。<end>"
EN: "The Floating City was a complete failure. Not one of the priestesses guarding the Dark Mother's seal died. Instead, one of them actually became a guardian of the seal.<end>"
KO: "공중도시는 대실패였지. 어둠의 성모 봉인을 지키는 무녀가 한 명도 죽지 않았을 뿐더러 오히려 한 명이 봉인을 지키러 들어가 버렸어.<end>"

## 1730110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "メンゴ。<end>"
EN: "My bad.<end>"
KO: "미안.<end>"

## 1730111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "少しは反省の色を見せてくれ。<line-break>円卓騎士メイアも役に立たないし…。<line-break>　<line-break>ここはひとつ、救世主の<line-break>邪竜アズラゴーザ復活計画に<line-break>協力するか。<end>"
EN: "Show a little remorse. Meia, Knight of the Round Table, hasn't been any use either... Maybe it's time to help the Savior with his plan to revive the Evil Dragon Azuragoza.<end>"
KO: "조금은 반성하는 기색을 보여라. 원탁기사 메이아도 쓸모가 없고…. 이럴 때는 구세주의사룡 아즈라고자 부활 계획에협력해 볼까.<end>"

## 1730112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "邪竜アズラゴーザねぇ…。　　　　　　<line-break>僕、あんまし、<line-break>竜とか偉ぶったのは好きじゃないな。<line-break>それに、救世主が頼んだ冒険者が<line-break>誘拐任務中、いけにえにするエルフに<line-break>手ェつけて自殺させちゃったんでしょ？<end>"
EN: "The Evil Dragon Azuragoza, huh... I've never much liked dragons or anything else that acts high and mighty. Besides, the adventurer the Savior hired for the kidnapping laid hands on the Elf intended for sacrifice and drove her to suicide, didn't he?<end>"
KO: "사룡 아즈라고자라…. 난 별로 용이나 잘난 체하는 녀석은 좋아하지 않아. 게다가 구세주가 부탁한 모험자가 유괴 임무 중 제물로 쓸 엘프에게 손을 대서 자살하게 했다며?<end>"

## 1730113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まったく、満足に仕事もできない<line-break>素人なんかに頼むから…。<line-break>これ、どーするのよ？。<end>"
EN: "That's what happens when you rely on amateurs who can't even do their jobs properly... So what do we do now?<end>"
KO: "정말이지, 제대로 일도 못 하는 초보한테 부탁하니까…. 이거 어쩔 거야?<end>"

## 1730114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その姉が闇におちていい感じなんだ。<line-break>死の獅子のもとで修行も積んでな。<line-break>きっと邪竜復活に使えるはずだ。<end>"
EN: "Her older sister has fallen into darkness and is shaping up nicely. She's even trained under the Lion of Death. I'm sure we can use her to revive the Evil Dragon.<end>"
KO: "그 누나가 어둠에 빠져서 꽤 괜찮은 상태야. 죽음의 사자 밑에서 수행도 했고. 분명 사룡 부활에 쓸 수 있을 거다.<end>"

## 1730115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ところで、ひとつきいていいかな？<end>"
EN: "...By the way, can I ask you something?<end>"
KO: "…그런데 하나 물어봐도 될까?<end>"

## 1730116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はい、どうぞ。<end>"
EN: "Certainly. Go ahead.<end>"
KO: "네, 여기요.<end>"

## 1730117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんで、わざわざ奴らのとなりに<line-break>部屋を取ってる？<end>"
EN: "Why did you deliberately take a room right next to theirs?<end>"
KO: "왜 일부러 녀석들 옆방을 잡은 거야?<end>"

## 1730118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "灯台もと暗しってね。<line-break>敵情視察もかねてる。<end>"
EN: "The best hiding place is right under their noses. It also lets me keep an eye on them.<end>"
KO: "등잔 밑이 어둡다잖아. 적정 시찰도 겸하고 있어.<end>"

## 1730119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "本当に？<end>"
EN: "Really?<end>"
KO: "정말로?<end>"

## 1730120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ごめん。おもしろがって<line-break>何も考えずにここにいます。<end>"
EN: "...Sorry. I just thought it would be funny. I didn't put any thought into it.<end>"
KO: "…미안. 재미있어 보여서 아무 생각 없이 여기 있어.<end>"

## 1730121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、いい子だ。<line-break>邪竜が起きたら見物に来るといい。<line-break>人形遊びよりは、退屈しのぎになる。<end>"
EN: "Heh. Good boy. Come watch when the Evil Dragon awakens. It should stave off boredom better than playing with dolls.<end>"
KO: "후후, 착한 아이군. 사룡이 깨어나면 구경하러 오면 돼. 인형놀이보다는 심심풀이가 되겠지.<end>"

## 1730122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "楽しみにしてるよ。<line-break>ジュサぷー。<end>"
EN: "I can't wait, Jusapoo.<end>"
KO: "기대하고 있을게. 주사푸.<end>"

## 1730123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さんですね。<line-break>オイフェ様より伝言です。　　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "You're <value:$28>, correct? I have a message from Lady Aoife.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 씨시군요. 오이페 님께서 전언을 남기셨습니다.<end>"

## 1730124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "先に依頼した　　　　　　　　　<line-break>山賊の孤児救済の件、<line-break>　<line-break>いくら待っても<line-break>らちが明かないようなので<line-break>別の手だてを講じた。<end>"
EN: "“Regarding the bandit orphans I asked you to rescue: it seemed waiting any longer would get us nowhere, so I made other arrangements.<end>"
KO: "먼저 의뢰한 산적 고아 구제 건은 아무리 기다려도 진전이 없어서 다른 방법을 마련했다.<end>"

## 1730125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "孤児はすでに助け出したので、　<line-break>依頼の件は忘れてくれて結構。<line-break>…とのことです。<end>"
EN: "The orphans have already been rescued, so you may forget about the request.” That is her message.<end>"
KO: "고아들은 이미 구출했으니 의뢰 건은 잊어도 좋다…. 라고 합니다.<end>"

## 1730126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、それからもうひとつ…。　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Th-there was one more thing…<end>"
KO: "그, 그리고 하나 더….<end>"

## 1730127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんたなんて二度と信用しない！<line-break>この薄情者っ！<end>"
EN: "“I'll never trust you again! You heartless wretch!”<end>"
KO: "너 같은 건 두 번 다시 안 믿어! 이 매정한 인간!<end>"

## 1730128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あの…これも<line-break>オイフェ様からの伝言でして。　<line-break>必ず伝えるようにと…。<line-break>まあ、そちらにもいろいろと<line-break>言い分はあるでしょうが…。<line-break>では、私はこれで。<end>"
EN: "Ah, um… that was also a message from Lady Aoife. She told me to make sure I passed it on. I'm sure you have your side of the story, but… Well, I'll be going now.<end>"
KO: "아, 그… 이것도 오이페 님의 전언입니다. 반드시 전하라고…. 뭐, 그쪽에도 여러 할 말은 있겠지만…. 그럼 저는 이만.<end>"

## 1730129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "部下になる<end>"
EN: "Become her subordinate.<end>"
KO: "부하가 된다<end>"

## 1730130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "部下にならない<end>"
EN: "Refuse to become her subordinate.<end>"
KO: "부하가 되지 않는다<end>"

## 1730131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うぐ…ひっく、ひっく…。<line-break>このおねえちゃん、恐いけど　<line-break>やさしいよぉ……。<end>"
EN: "Ugh... hic, hic... This lady's scary, but she's nice...<end>"
KO: "흑… 훌쩍, 훌쩍…. 이 언니, 무섭지만 착해요오…….<end>"

## 1730132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "も、もう、<line-break>恐くなんてないんだから。<line-break>泣いてなんていないんだから！<line-break>泣いてなんて…泣いてなんて…<line-break>ぶえ～ん、恐かったよ～～。<end>"
EN: "I-I'm not scared anymore, okay? And I'm not crying! I'm not crying... I'm not... Waaaah! I was scared!<end>"
KO: "이, 이제 무섭지 않거든. 울지도 않았거든! 울지… 울지… 으아앙, 무서웠어~~.<end>"

## 1730133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "見つけたぞ。ジュサプブロス。<end>"
EN: "I've found you, Jusabros.<end>"
KO: "찾았다. 주사프브로스.<end>"

## 1730134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "見た顔がいると思ったら…。<line-break>元気そうで何よりだ、ジリオン。<line-break>ところで俺に何か用かな？<end>"
EN: "I thought I recognized someone... I'm glad to see you looking well, Zillion. Now, what business do you have with me?<end>"
KO: "낯익은 얼굴이 있나 했더니…. 건강해 보여 다행이군, 질리온. 그런데 나한테 무슨 볼일이지?<end>"

## 1730135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しらばっくれるな。<line-break>同化の髪飾りを返してもらうぞ。<end>"
EN: "Don't play innocent. Give back the Hair Ornament of Assimilation.<end>"
KO: "시치미 떼지 마. 동화의 머리장식을 돌려받겠다.<end>"

## 1730136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec173-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1740002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わ…わたしのせい…<line-break>わたしのせい…なんです…。<end>"

KO: "제… 제 탓… 제 탓이에요….<end>"

## 1740003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わたし…、わたし、もう<line-break>どうすれば…いい…のか…。<end>"
EN: "I... I don't know what to do anymore...<end>"
KO: "나… 나, 이제 어떻게 해야… 할지….<end>"

## 1740004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノエル！　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>突然、いなくなるなんて！<line-break>…おどかさないで。<end>"
EN: "Noel! You can't just disappear like that! Don't scare me.<end>"
KO: "노엘! 갑자기 사라지다니! …놀라게 하지 마.<end>"

## 1740005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わ、わたし…。<end>"
EN: "I-I...<end>"
KO: "저, 저는….<end>"

## 1740006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0ボウヤ、ごめんなさいね。<line-break>この子、ちょっと混乱してるのよ。<end>子猫ちゃん、ごめんなさいね。<line-break>この子、ちょっと混乱してるのよ。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0I'm sorry, young man. She's a little confused right now.<end>I'm sorry, kitten. She's a little confused right now.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0꼬마야, 미안해. 이 아이가 좀 혼란스러운가 봐.<end>아가씨, 미안해. 이 아이가 좀 혼란스러운가 봐.<end>"

## 1740007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "You've heard about Nemea, haven't you?<end>"

## 1740008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1740010
