jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう、ネメアは死んだわ。<end>"
EN: "Yes. Nemea is dead.<end>"
KO: "그래, 네메아는 죽었어.<end>"

## 1740011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ネメアは死んだわ。<end>"
EN: "Nemea is dead.<end>"
KO: "…네메아는 죽었어.<end>"

## 1740012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わたしのせい…なんです…！<line-break>わたしが…ドジだったから…。<end>"
EN: "It was my fault! I... I was so clumsy...<end>"
KO: "제 탓…이에요…! 제가… 덤벙대서….<end>"

## 1740013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねえ、落ち着いて。<line-break>あれはネメアが望んでしたことだわ。<end>"
EN: "Come on, calm down. Nemea chose to do it.<end>"
KO: "진정해. 그건 네메아가 원해서 한 일이야.<end>"

## 1740014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あたしたち、ゼグナ鉱山で　　　　　　<line-break>偶然ネメアを見かけたの。<line-break>それで、あとを追ったわけ。<line-break>そしたらネメアはジュサプブロスと<line-break>出くわして戦い始めたの。その時<line-break>ジュサプブロスがこっちに気付いて…。<end>"
EN: "We happened to spot Nemea at Zegna Mine, so we followed him. Then he ran into Jusabros, and they started fighting. That was when Jusabros noticed us...<end>"
KO: "우리, 제그나 광산에서 우연히 네메아를 봤어. 그래서 뒤를 쫓았지. 그랬더니 네메아가 주사프브로스와 마주쳐 싸우기 시작했어. 그때 주사프브로스가 이쪽을 눈치채고….<end>"

## 1740015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じ、次元のはざまを開いて…　<line-break>わたし…そこに…<line-break>落とされそうに…なって…。<line-break>そ、そしたら…ネメアさんが…<line-break>助けてくれて…<line-break>で、でも…でも…！<end>"
EN: "J-Jusabros opened the Interdimensional Rift... and I... I was about to be thrown in... Then Nemea... Nemea saved me... B-but... but...!<end>"
KO: "차, 차원의 틈을 열어서… 저를… 거기로… 떨어뜨리려 해서… 그, 그랬더니 네메아 씨가… 구해 줘서… 그, 그런데…!<end>"

## 1740016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメアがこの子に気を取られたすきに、<line-break>ジュサプブロスは、<line-break>ネメアを次元のはざまに落とした…。<line-break>ネメアは次元のはざまに消えたわ。<line-break>あたしたちの目の前で…。<end>"
EN: "While Nemea was distracted by her, Jusabros cast him into the Interdimensional Rift. Nemea vanished into the Rift... right before our eyes.<end>"
KO: "네메아가 이 아이에게 정신이 팔린 틈에 주사프브로스는 네메아를 차원의 틈새로 떨어뜨렸어…. 네메아는 차원의 틈새로 사라졌어. 우리 눈앞에서….<end>"

## 1740017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメアさんを死なせてしま…た…。<line-break><value:$28>さん…<line-break>い、いったい…どうすれば…？<end>"
EN: "I let Nemea die... <value:$28>... w-what am I supposed to do?<end>"
KO: "네메아 씨를 죽게 만들고 말았어…. <value:$28>님… 대, 대체… 어떡하면…?<end>"

## 1740018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノエル、わかるかしら？　　　　　　<line-break>ネメアは、こうなることを知ってて、<line-break>それでもノエルを助けたのよ。<line-break>今、ノエルが自分を責めたら<line-break>ノエルを救ったネメアは<line-break>全然救われないわ。<end>"
EN: "Noel, don't you understand? Nemea knew this would happen, and he saved you anyway. If you blame yourself now, then Nemea's sacrifice will have been for nothing.<end>"
KO: "노엘, 알겠어? 네메아는 이렇게 될 걸 알고도 노엘을 구한 거야. 지금 노엘이 자기를 탓하면 노엘을 구한 네메아는 전혀 구원받지 못해.<end>"

## 1740019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あたしが何を言っても<line-break>ダメみたいね。<end>"
EN: "Nothing I say seems to be getting through to her.<end>"
KO: "내가 무슨 말을 해도 소용없는 모양이네.<end>"

## 1740020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0ボウヤ、お願い。<line-break>しばらくノエルのそばにいてあげて。<line-break>　<line-break>ボウヤなら、この子を<line-break>なぐさめてあげられると思うの。<end>お願いよ、子猫ちゃん。<line-break>しばらくノエルのそばにいてあげて。<line-break>　<line-break>あなたなら、この子を<line-break>なぐさめてあげられると思うの。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Please, young man. Stay with Noel for a while. I think you might be able to comfort her.<end>Please, kitten. Stay with Noel for a while. I think you might be able to comfort her.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0꼬마야, 부탁이야. 잠시 노엘 곁에 있어 줘. 너라면 이 아이를 위로해 줄 수 있을 것 같아.<end>부탁이야, 아가씨. 잠시 노엘 곁에 있어 줘. 너라면 이 아이를 위로해 줄 수 있을 것 같아.<end>"

## 1740021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう…ございました。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>さんのおかげで、<line-break>あの…、少し落ち着きました。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. Thanks to you, <value:$28>, I've... calmed down a little.<end>"
KO: "감사…합니다. <value:$28>님 덕분에 저… 조금 진정됐어요.<end>"

## 1740022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ…、あまり遅くなると<line-break>カフィンが心配しますから。<end>"
EN: "I... I shouldn't stay too long, or Coffin will worry.<end>"
KO: "아… 너무 늦으면 카핀이 걱정할 테니까요.<end>"

## 1740023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう大丈夫です。<line-break>ホントに、ホントに<line-break>ありがとうございました。<end>"
EN: "I'm all right now. Thank you. Truly, thank you so much.<end>"
KO: "이제 괜찮아요. 정말, 정말 감사합니다.<end>"

## 1740024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さぁ、行きましょう、ノエル。<line-break>レイヴンもあなたを捜しているわ。<end>"
EN: "Come on, Noel. Let's go. Raven's looking for you too.<end>"
KO: "자, 가요 노엘. 레이븐도 당신을 찾고 있어요.<end>"

## 1740025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0ねえ、ボウヤも何か言ってあげて。<end>ねえ、子猫ちゃんも何か言ってあげて。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Come on, young man. You say something to her too.<end>Come on, kitten. You say something to her too.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0얘, 꼬마도 뭐라고 좀 해 줘.<end>얘, 아가씨도 뭐라고 좀 해 줘.<end>"

## 1740026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメアはきっと生きている<end>"
EN: "I'm sure Nemea is still alive.<end>"
KO: "네메아는 분명 살아 있어<end>"

## 1740027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "泣いたって、死んだ人間は戻らない<end>"
EN: "Crying won't bring the dead back.<end>"
KO: "울어도 죽은 사람은 돌아오지 않아.<end>"

## 1740028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "で、でも…　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>そうですね…。<line-break>生きているかも…しれませんよね。<line-break>ありがとうございます。<end>"
EN: "B-but... Yes, you're right... He might still be alive. Thank you.<end>"
KO: "그, 그래도… 그렇네요…. 살아 있을지도… 모르잖아요. 고마워요.<end>"

## 1740029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "助かったわ。<line-break>…今のノエルには、<line-break>そう言ってあげるのが一番ね。<end>"
EN: "That helped. Right now, that's exactly what Noel needs to hear.<end>"
KO: "살았네. …지금 노엘에게는 그렇게 말해 주는 게 제일 좋겠어.<end>"

## 1740030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…わかってます…。<line-break>わかってる…けど…。<end>"
EN: "I know... I do know, but...<end>"
KO: "…알고 있어요…. 알고는… 있지만….<end>"

## 1740031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大したもんね。<line-break>ナーシェスだって<line-break>あそこまでは言えるかしら？<end>"
EN: "That was quite something. I wonder if even Narscess could have said that much.<end>"
KO: "대단하네. 나셰스라도 저 정도까지 말할 수 있으려나?<end>"

## 1740032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…さん…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>… 씨….<end>"

## 1740033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノエル！　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>こっそり部屋を抜け出すなんて！<line-break>…おどかさないで。<end>"
EN: "Noel! Don't sneak out of your room like that! You gave me such a fright.<end>"
KO: "노엘! 몰래 방을 빠져나가다니! …놀라게 하지 마.<end>"

## 1740034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんな夜中まで、<line-break>付き合わせてしまって<line-break>…ごめんなさい。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry I kept you up with me so late.<end>"
KO: "이렇게 한밤중까지 함께 있게 해서… 미안해요.<end>"

## 1740035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう大丈夫です。<line-break>それじゃ、あの…おやすみなさい。<end>"
EN: "I'm all right now. Well, um... good night.<end>"
KO: "이제 괜찮아요. 그럼 저기… 안녕히 주무세요.<end>"

## 1740036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さぁ、もう遅いわ。<line-break>今日は休みましょう。<end>"
EN: "Come on, it's late. Let's get some rest.<end>"
KO: "자, 이제 늦었어. 오늘은 쉬자.<end>"

## 1740037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>聞きたいことがある！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, I have something to ask you!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 묻고 싶은 게 있다!<end>"

## 1740038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やれやれ。<line-break>それが人にものを尋ねるときの<line-break>態度かのう。<end>"
EN: "Good grief. Is that any way to ask someone a question?<end>"
KO: "원 참. 그게 남에게 뭘 물어볼 때의 태도냐.<end>"

## 1740039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメア様のことよ。<line-break>知らないとは言わせない。<line-break>あなたの知ってることを教えて。<end>"
EN: "It's about Lord Nemea. Don't tell me you know nothing. Tell me what you do know.<end>"
KO: "네메아 님 이야기야. 모른다고 하진 마. 당신이 아는 걸 말해 줘.<end>"

## 1740040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…じゃあ、ネメア様は、<line-break>ゼグナで次元のはざまに<line-break>落とされたというのね？<end>"
EN: "So Lord Nemea was cast into the Interdimensional Rift at Zegna?<end>"
KO: "…그럼 네메아 님은 제그나에서 차원의 틈으로 떨어졌다는 거네?<end>"

## 1740041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "行くわ。<end>"
EN: "I'm going.<end>"
KO: "갈게.<end>"

## 1740042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "行く？<line-break>どこへ行くのだ？<end>"
EN: "Going? Where?<end>"
KO: "간다고? 어디로 가는 거지?<end>"

## 1740043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "天地千年を見通す巫女エアの所よ。<line-break>ネメア様は以前、エルズのエアを<line-break>訪ねると言ってた。<line-break>だから、エアに会って<line-break>ネメア様がそこで何を知ったのか<line-break>確かめるの。<end>"
EN: "To Er, the priestess whose sight spans a thousand years of heaven and earth. Lord Nemea once said he was going to visit her in Elz. So I'll meet with Er and find out what he learned there.<end>"
KO: "천지 천 년을 내다보는 무녀 에아가 있는 곳이야. 네메아 님은 전에 엘즈의 에아를 찾아간다고 했어. 그러니 에아를 만나 네메아 님이 거기서 무엇을 알았는지 확인하는 거야.<end>"

## 1740044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしは反対じゃな。<line-break>今、うかつに動くのは危険すぎる。<line-break>　<line-break>闇の者が動き出しているのだ。<line-break>奴らにねらわれぬよう、<line-break>おとなしくするのが得策じゃろうて。<end>"
EN: "I am against it. It is far too dangerous to make any rash moves now. The Dark Ones are on the move. Our wisest course is to lie low and avoid drawing their attention.<end>"
KO: "나는 반대다. 지금 섣불리 움직이는 건 너무 위험해. 어둠의 자들이 움직이기 시작했어. 놈들의 표적이 되지 않도록 얌전히 있는 게 상책일 게다.<end>"

## 1740045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どこにいても、危険なのは同じだわ！<line-break>ネメア様を一時的にしろ、死…<line-break>いいえ、行方不明にしている奴らよ。<line-break>むしろ、なぜネメア様が神器を<line-break>集めようとしたのか、<line-break>それを知らない方が危険よ。<end>"
EN: "It's just as dangerous wherever we are! They're the ones who, even if only for a time, killed Lord Nemea―no, made him disappear. If anything, it's more dangerous not to know why Lord Nemea was trying to gather the divine artifacts.<end>"
KO: "어디에 있든 위험한 건 마찬가지야! 네메아 님을 잠시나마 죽… 아니, 행방불명으로 만든 녀석들이라고. 오히려 네메아 님이 왜 신기를 모으려 했는지 모르는 게 더 위험해.<end>"

## 1740046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ネメア様が何を聞いたか<line-break>わかっても、<line-break>何を考えたのかわかるのか？<line-break>…我は行かぬ。<line-break>行くというのなら、<line-break>抜ける。<end>"
EN: "Even if we learn what Lord Nemea heard, will that tell us what he was thinking? I will not go. If you insist on going, count me out.<end>"
KO: "…네메아 님이 무엇을 들었는지 알아도 무엇을 생각했는지는 알 수 있나? …나는 가지 않겠다. 간다면 빠지겠다.<end>"

## 1740047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やれやれ。<line-break>不器用な奴よ。<end>"
EN: "Good grief. What an awkward fellow.<end>"
KO: "이런. 서투른 녀석이군.<end>"

## 1740048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、わしもゼリグと同意見だ。<line-break>ネメア様の意図は知るべくもない。<line-break>　<line-break>だが、我々はその信念に<line-break>邪念はなかったと信じておる。<line-break>いまさら、確認の必要はない。<end>"
EN: "Still, I agree with Zelig. We have no way of knowing Lord Nemea's intentions. But we believe his convictions were free of any base motive. There is no need to seek confirmation now.<end>"
KO: "하지만 나도 젤리그와 같은 생각이다. 네메아 님의 의도는 알 수 없어. 그래도 그 신념에 사심은 없었다고 믿는다. 이제 와 확인할 필요는 없어.<end>"

## 1740049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかった。<line-break>もう、いいわ。<end>"
EN: "All right. Forget it.<end>"
KO: "알았어. 이제 됐어.<end>"

## 1740050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、突然だけど<line-break>私の護衛をしてもらえる？<line-break>どうしてもエルズのエアに会いたいの。<line-break>どう？<line-break>引き受けてもらえる？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, I know this is sudden, but will you escort me? I have to see Er in Elz. What do you say? Will you do it?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 갑작스럽지만 내 호위를 맡아 줄래? 꼭 엘즈의 에아를 만나고 싶어. 어때? 맡아 줄 수 있어?<end>"

## 1740051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "引き受ける<end>"
EN: "Accept<end>"
KO: "수락한다<end>"

## 1740052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "引き受けない<end>"
EN: "Decline<end>"
KO: "맡지 않는다<end>"

## 1740053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう。ありがとう。<line-break>じゃあ、さっそく出発よ。<line-break>冒険に出る時に合流しましょう。<end>"
EN: "You will? Thank you. Then let's leave at once. We'll meet up when you next set out on an adventure.<end>"
KO: "그래. 고마워. 그럼 바로 출발이야. 모험을 떠날 때 합류하자.<end>"

## 1740054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やれやれ。　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>あれくらいに一直線だと、<line-break>気持ちいいぐらいだわい。<line-break>言っても聞かんのだから、<line-break>仕方がないかの。<line-break>オイフェのこと、頼んだぞ。<end>"
EN: "Good grief. Aoife is so single-minded, it's almost refreshing. There's no changing her mind, so I suppose it cannot be helped. Take care of her for me.<end>"
KO: "허허. 저 정도로 한길만 달리면 오히려 속이 시원하군. 말해도 듣지 않으니 어쩔 수 없지. 오이페를 부탁하네.<end>"

## 1740055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう。仕方ないわ。<line-break>私は、ひとりでも行く。<end>"
EN: "Oh. Fine, then. I'll go alone.<end>"
KO: "그래. 어쩔 수 없지. 나는 혼자라도 갈 거야.<end>"

## 1740056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やれやれ。<line-break>あれくらいに一直線だと、<line-break>気持ちいいぐらいだわい。<end>"
EN: "Good grief. That one is so single-minded, it's almost refreshing.<end>"
KO: "이런. 저 정도로 일직선이면 오히려 시원할 정도군.<end>"

## 1740057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ、来たか。<line-break><value:$28>よ。<line-break>そして、オイフェよ。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh. So you have come, <value:$28>. And you as well, Aoife.<end>"
KO: "후후후, 왔구나. <value:$28>이여. 그리고 오이페여.<end>"

## 1740058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…なるほど、千年を見通すエア。<line-break>私がここに来るのも、私が何者かも<line-break>私以上によく知ってるわけね。<end>"
EN: "I see. Er, who can see a thousand years ahead. You know better than I do that I would come here―and even what I am.<end>"
KO: "…과연, 천 년을 내다보는 에아. 내가 여기 올 것도, 내가 누구인지도 나보다 더 잘 알고 있다는 거네.<end>"

## 1740059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "汝らが抱いている問いも、<line-break>その答えも知っておる。<line-break>死の獅子、ネメアについてであろう。<end>"
EN: "I know the question you bear, and its answer. It concerns Nemea, the Lion of Death, does it not?<end>"
KO: "그대들이 품은 질문도, 그 답도 알고 있다. 죽음의 사자 네메아에 관한 것이겠지.<end>"

## 1740060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかっているのなら、<line-break>早く教えて！<line-break>こっちは急いでいるの。<end>"
EN: "If you know, then tell us! We haven't got time to waste.<end>"
KO: "알고 있으면 빨리 말해줘! 우린 급하다고.<end>"

## 1740061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よかろう、汝に教えようぞ。<line-break>ネメアがわらわを訪ねてきたのは<line-break>自らの運命を確認するためじゃ。<line-break>すなわち、ネメアの身体に<line-break>破壊神ウルグが降臨するという<line-break>宿命から逃れられるか否かを。<end>"
EN: "Very well. I shall tell you. Nemea came to me to confirm his own fate: whether he could escape the destiny that would see Ulugh, the God of Destruction, descend into his body.<end>"
KO: "좋다, 네게 알려 주마. 네메아가 나를 찾아온 것은 자신의 운명을 확인하기 위해서였다. 즉 네메아의 몸에 파괴신 울그가 강림한다는 숙명에서 벗어날 수 있는지 없는지를.<end>"

## 1740062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そなたに破壊神が宿る…。<line-break>それは抗えぬ運命だ。<line-break>…私は奴にそう答えた。<line-break>それから、奴はその運命と<line-break>戦い続けておるのじゃ。<end>"
EN: "“The God of Destruction shall dwell within you. That fate cannot be defied.” That was the answer I gave him. Ever since, he has continued to fight against that fate.<end>"
KO: "그대에게 파괴신이 깃든다…. 그것은 거스를 수 없는 운명이다. …나는 그에게 그렇게 답했다. 그 뒤로 그는 그 운명과 계속 싸우고 있지.<end>"

## 1740063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメア様に破壊神が宿る…？<line-break>でたらめを言うな！<end>"
EN: "The God of Destruction will dwell within Lord Nemea...? Don't talk nonsense!<end>"
KO: "네메아 님에게 파괴신이 깃든다고…? 헛소리하지 마!<end>"

## 1740064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…でたらめではない。<line-break>ただの定めじゃ。<line-break>変えることのできぬ、な。<end>"
EN: "It is not nonsense. It is simply fate, and it cannot be changed.<end>"
KO: "…엉터리는 아니다. 그저 정해진 운명이지. 바꿀 수 없는, 말이야.<end>"

## 1740065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "変えることができない？<end>"
EN: "It can't be changed?<end>"
KO: "바꿀 수 없다고?<end>"

## 1740066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ということは、<line-break>ネメア様が死んではいない…<line-break>そういうことか！？<end>"
EN: "Then Lord Nemea isn't dead... Is that what you're saying?!<end>"
KO: "…그렇다면 네메아 님은 죽지 않았다… 그런 뜻인가!?<end>"

## 1740067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それは自身で確かめるのじゃ。<line-break>そなたらはそなたらとして戦い、<line-break>求めるがよい。<line-break>さらばじゃ、強き魂たちよ。<end>"
EN: "That is for you to discover yourselves. Fight in your own way, and seek. Farewell, strong souls.<end>"
KO: "그건 스스로 확인하거라. 너희는 너희답게 싸우고 구하도록 해라. 잘 있어라, 강한 영혼들이여.<end>"

## 1740068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>じゃないですか。<line-break>実は最近、大陸南東の断層から　　　<line-break>強い妖気が放たれているのです。<line-break>あの辺りは邪竜アズラゴーザが<line-break>封じられている場所なんです。それで<line-break>調査に行こうと思っていたんです。<end>"
EN: "Well, if it isn't <value:$28>. Lately, a powerful demonic aura has been emanating from a fault line in the southeast of the continent. That is where the Evil Dragon Azuragoza is sealed away, so I was planning to investigate.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 아닙니까. 사실 최근 대륙 남동쪽 단층에서 강한 요기가 뿜어져 나오고 있습니다. 그 일대는 사룡 아즈라고자가 봉인된 곳입니다. 그래서 조사하러 갈 생각이었습니다.<end>"

## 1740069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "できれば、あなたたちも<line-break>ついてきてくれませんか？<end>"
EN: "If you can, would you come with me?<end>"
KO: "가능하면 당신들도 함께 와 주시겠어요?<end>"

## 1740070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な～に言ってやがる。<line-break>手伝わせるために、奴らが来るのを<line-break>待ってたくせに…。<end>"
EN: "Oh, give it a rest. You were waiting for them to show up so you could rope them into helping...<end>"
KO: "무슨 소리야. 도와주게 하려고 녀석들이 오길 기다렸으면서….<end>"

## 1740071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすくす。すいません。<line-break>まぁ、そういうわけです。<line-break>いいですか？<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. My apologies. Well, there you have it. Will you come?<end>"
KO: "후후. 미안합니다. 뭐, 그런 사정입니다. 괜찮겠습니까?<end>"

## 1740072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございます。　　　　　<line-break>では、行きましょう。<line-break>　<line-break>とは言っても、私はこんな姿なので<line-break>戦闘のお手伝いはできませんがね。<line-break>よろしくお願いしますよ。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. Then let us be off. That said, given my present form, I will not be able to help in a fight. I shall be counting on you.<end>"
KO: "감사합니다. 그럼 갑시다. 그렇다고 해도 저는 이런 모습이라 전투에는 도움을 드릴 수 없지만요. 잘 부탁드립니다.<end>"

## 1740073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし…、お前も<line-break>いいようにこき使われてるな…。<line-break>少しだけ、同情してやるぜ。<end>"
EN: "Still... looks like you're being worked to the bone too. You have my sympathy. A little of it, anyway.<end>"
KO: "하지만… 너도 참 마음대로 부려 먹히고 있군…. 조금은 동정해 주지.<end>"

## 1740074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…遅かった、です。<end>"
EN: "…It was too late.<end>"
KO: "…늦었어요.<end>"

## 1740075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…邪竜、<line-break>アズラゴーザが復活しました。<end>"
EN: "…The Evil Dragon Azragorza has returned to life.<end>"
KO: "…사룡 아즈라고자가 부활했습니다.<end>"

## 1740076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いつ、断層から出てきても<line-break>おかしくありません。<end>"
EN: "It could emerge from the fault at any moment.<end>"
KO: "언제 단층에서 튀어나와도 이상하지 않습니다.<end>"

## 1740077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのときは、<line-break>アキュリュースの二の舞です。<end>"
EN: "Then it will be another Aqyurius.<end>"
KO: "그때는 아큐류스의 전철을 밟게 됩니다.<end>"

## 1740078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "邪竜が本当に出てきたら…。<end>"
EN: "If the Evil Dragon really does emerge…<end>"
KO: "사룡이 정말 나타난다면….<end>"

## 1740079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…といっても、相手は竜。<line-break>どうしようもないですかね。<end>"
EN: "…Then again, the enemy is a dragon. Perhaps nothing can be done.<end>"
KO: "…그래도 상대는 용이네요. 어쩔 수 없으려나.<end>"

## 1740080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やあ、どこに行くんだい？　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>あれー？　確かこの先には<line-break>邪竜を封印した祭壇しか<line-break>ないはずなんだけどなあ…。<end>"
EN: "Hey, where are you going? Huh? I thought the only thing up ahead was the altar where the Evil Dragon was sealed away...<end>"
KO: "야, 어디 가는 거야? 어라? 분명 이 앞에는 사룡을 봉인한 제단밖에 없을 텐데….<end>"

## 1740081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あはは、やっぱり君　　　　　　<line-break>邪竜に会いたいんでしょ？<line-break>会って、戦いたいんでしょ？<line-break>だったら、その願い、<line-break>僕がかなえてあげるよ！！<line-break>さあ、邪竜をどうしたいんだい？<end>"
EN: "Ahaha! I knew it―you want to meet the Evil Dragon, don't you? You want to meet it and fight it, right? Well, I'll make that wish come true for you!! So, what do you want to do with the Evil Dragon?<end>"
KO: "아하하, 역시 너 사룡을 만나고 싶은 거지? 만나서 싸우고 싶은 거지? 그렇다면 그 소원, 내가 이루어 줄게!! 자, 사룡을 어떻게 하고 싶어?<end>"

## 1740082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "復活させたい<end>"
EN: "I want to revive it.<end>"
KO: "부활시키고 싶다<end>"

## 1740083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "復活なんてゴメンだ<end>"
EN: "No way I'm reviving it.<end>"
KO: "부활 따윈 사양이야<end>"

## 1740084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うーん、いい返事だね。<line-break>やっぱり人間、素直が一番だよね。<line-break>　<line-break>それじゃ、ちょっと失礼して…。<end>"
EN: "Mmm, good answer. Honesty really is the best policy. Well then, if you'll excuse me for a moment...<end>"
KO: "음, 좋은 대답이네. 역시 사람은 솔직한 게 최고지. 그럼 잠깐 실례할게….<end>"

## 1740085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さ、何が起こったかわかるよね？　　<line-break>あとは君が自分の目で確かめてよ。<line-break>　<line-break>邪竜、つよいよー。<line-break>君が負けたら世界は破滅しちゃうね。<line-break>そんじゃ、ガンバってねー。<end>"
EN: "You know what just happened, don't you? Now go see for yourself. The Evil Dragon is really strong. If you lose, the whole world will be destroyed. So, good luck!<end>"
KO: "자, 무슨 일이 일어났는지 알겠지? 나머지는 네 눈으로 직접 확인해. 사룡, 강하다고~. 네가 지면 세계는 멸망하겠지. 그럼 힘내~.<end>"

## 1740086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えー、なんでー！？　　　　　　<line-break>そんなのつまんないよー。<line-break>　<line-break>ねえ、もっかい考えてみてよ。<line-break>邪竜、復活させたいよね？　ね？<end>"
EN: "What? Why not!? That's no fun. Come on, think it over one more time. You want to bring the Evil Dragon back, don't you? Don't you?<end>"
KO: "에이, 왜!? 그런 건 재미없어. 다시 한번 생각해 봐. 사룡을 부활시키고 싶지? 그렇지?<end>"

## 1740087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "本当は復活させたい<end>"
EN: "I really do want to revive it.<end>"
KO: "사실은 부활시키고 싶다<end>"

## 1740088

## 1740089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちぇ、意外とガンコなんだね。<line-break>つまんないの。<line-break>じゃあ、何しにここまできたのさ？<line-break>もういいよ。<line-break>邪竜より面白いこと探すから。<line-break>じゃあねー。<end>"
EN: "Aw, you're more stubborn than I thought. What a bore. Then what did you come all this way for? Oh, forget it. I'll go find something more interesting than the Evil Dragon. Bye-bye!<end>"
KO: "쳇, 의외로 고집이 세네. 재미없어. 그럼 뭐 하러 여기까지 온 거야? 됐어. 사룡보다 재미있는 걸 찾을 테니까. 안녕.<end>"

## 1740090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やあ、また来たんだね。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>ま～ったく、<line-break>君ってホントに<line-break>素直じゃないよね。<end>"
EN: "Hey, you're back. Honestly, you really can't just be honest, can you?<end>"
KO: "안녕, 또 왔구나. 정말 너도 참 솔직하지 못하네.<end>"

## 1740091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう、素直に言っちゃいなよ。　　　<line-break>邪竜を復活させたい、<line-break>そんでもって、戦いたいって。<line-break>そうでもなきゃ、こんなに何度も<line-break>ここに来たりはしないよね？<line-break>さ、言ってみ。お願いします、って。<end>"
EN: "Come on, just admit it. You want to bring the Evil Dragon back and then fight it. Why else would you keep coming here over and over? Go on, say it: “Please.”<end>"
KO: "이제 솔직히 말해 봐. 사룡을 부활시키고 싶다, 그리고 싸우고 싶다고. 아니면 이렇게 몇 번이나 여기 오진 않았겠지? 자, 말해 봐. 부탁합니다, 하고.<end>"

## 1740094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1740095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はいはい。　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>君はそう言うだろうと思ったよ。<line-break>　<line-break>ま、気が向いたらまた来てよ。<line-break>ボクの方はいつでも準備ＯＫだから。<end>"
EN: "Yeah, yeah. I figured you'd say that. Well, come back if you feel like it. I'm ready whenever you are.<end>"

## 1740096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　我はアズラゴーザ。　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　破壊神によりこの世に生を受け<line-break>　　　　破壊と絶望とをもたらす者なり。<line-break>　　不遜にも我が眠りを覚ますのは何者か？<end>"
EN: "I am Azuragoza. Born into this world by the God of Destruction, I am the bringer of destruction and despair. Who dares awaken me from my slumber?<end>"
KO: "나는 아즈라고자. 파괴신에 의해 이 세상에 태어나 파괴와 절망을 가져오는 자다. 불손하게도 내 잠을 깨우는 자는 누구냐?<end>"

## 1740097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　無限のソウルの持ち主か…。　　　　<line-break>　　　　　 起き抜けに力を得るには<line-break>　　　　　　　ちょうどよいわ…。<line-break>　　　　　うぬのソウル、もらうぞ！<end>"
EN: "So, you possess the Infinite Soul... Just what I need to gain power upon awakening... I will take your Soul!<end>"
KO: "무한의 소울 소유자인가…. 막 깨어난 몸에 힘을 얻기엔 딱 좋군…. 네 소울, 받아 가마!<end>"

## 1740098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　バ、バカな…。　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　たかが…たかが人の子ごときに<line-break>　　　　この我が…竜が…敗れるなど…<line-break>　　　　　　　　ガアアッ！！<end>"
EN: "I-impossible... That a mere... a mere human could defeat me... a dragon... Gaaaah!!<end>"
KO: "바, 바보 같은… 고작… 고작 인간의 자식 따위에게 이 내가… 용이… 패하다니… 가아악!!<end>"

## 1740100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1740101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "…케류네이아, 네 활을 넘겨받고 싶어.<end>"

## 1740102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いきなり入ってきて<line-break>何を言うかと思えば…。<line-break>ダークエルフに貸す弓などないわ。<line-break>そもそも、どうしてあなたが<line-break>閃光の弓を知っているの。<end>"

KO: "갑자기 들어와서 무슨 말을 하나 했더니…. 다크엘프에게 빌려줄 활은 없어. 애초에 네가 왜 섬광의 활을 알고 있지?<end>"

## 1740104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それに、それほどの弓なら<line-break>私のように腕を備えた者が<line-break>持っている方が効果を発揮できる。<end>"
EN: "Besides, a bow like that will be far more effective in the hands of someone with my skill.<end>"
KO: "게다가 그 정도의 활이라면 나처럼 실력을 갖춘 사람이 가지고 있는 편이 더 효과를 발휘할 수 있어.<end>"

## 1740105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんですって！<line-break>私の腕があなたに劣るというの！？<end>"
EN: "What did you say? You think you're a better archer than I am?!<end>"
KO: "뭐라고! 내 실력이 당신보다 못하다는 거야!?<end>"

## 1740106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんな所でのうのうと過ごしている<line-break>ハーフエルフごときが、<line-break>私に勝てるはずがないわ。<end>"
EN: "A mere Half-Elf who spends her days lounging around a place like this could never best me.<end>"
KO: "이런 곳에서 태평하게 지내는 하프엘프 따위가 나를 이길 리 없어.<end>"

## 1740107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "戦うことしか能のないダークエルフが<line-break>そう考えるのは無理もないけど、<line-break>あとで泣いても遅いわよ。<end>"

KO: "싸우는 것밖에 모르는 다크엘프가 그렇게 생각하는 것도 무리는 아니지만, 나중에 울어도 늦어.<end>"

## 1740109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんて子供じみた争いなのかしら。<line-break>聞くにたえなくてよ、まったく。<end>"
EN: "What a childish squabble. Honestly, I can't bear to listen to another word.<end>"
KO: "정말 유치한 싸움이군요. 도저히 듣고 있을 수가 없어요.<end>"

## 1740110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ダークエルフだのハーフエルフだの　　<line-break>そんなくだらない話は私がいないときに<line-break>やっていただきたいわ。<line-break>ようは個人の才能ではなくて？<line-break>種族を云々言うなんて自分に自信がない<line-break>輩の言いわけに過ぎなくてよ。<end>"
EN: "Kindly save this foolish talk of Dark Elves and Half-Elves for when I'm not here. Surely what matters is individual talent? Harping on race is nothing more than an excuse made by those with no confidence in their own abilities.<end>"
KO: "다크엘프니 하프엘프니 하는 그런 시시한 얘기는 제가 없을 때 해 주시겠어요? 중요한 건 개인의 재능 아닌가요? 종족을 들먹이는 건 자기 자신이 없는 자들의 변명일 뿐이에요.<end>"

## 1740111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これから戦いに行こうというときに<line-break>種族が違うから武器を貸したくないだの<line-break>使いたくないだの、レベルが低過ぎよ。<line-break>あー、もー、アタクシの高貴な時間を<line-break>こんな下等な争いでつぶすなんて<line-break>もったいなさ１００万倍よー！<end>"
EN: "You're about to go into battle, yet you're squabbling over not wanting to lend or use a weapon just because you're different races. That's far too petty. Oh, honestly! Wasting my noble time on such a lowly quarrel is a million times too wasteful!<end>"
KO: "이제 싸우러 가려는 판에 종족이 다르다고 무기를 빌려주기 싫다느니 쓰기 싫다느니, 수준이 너무 낮잖아. 아, 정말! 이 몸의 고귀한 시간을 이런 하찮은 싸움에 쓰다니 아까움이 백만 배야!<end>"

## 1740115

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1740116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1740117

## 1740121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1740123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメア様は生きているわ。<line-break>そして、破壊神復活に必要な<line-break>闇の神器さえ持っていれば、<line-break>ネメア様は私たちの前に<line-break>きっと、現れるはず。<line-break>さぁ、神器を渡して。<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "パルシェン…？<line-break>全エルフを束ねる長…。<line-break>この猫が、そうなのか？<end>"
EN: "Parshen...? The leader of all the Elves... is this cat really Parshen?<end>"
KO: "파르셴…? 모든 엘프를 통솔하는 장… 이 고양이가 그런 존재인가?<end>"

## 1740136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すいません。<value:$28>。<line-break>隠していたわけでもないのですが、<line-break>名乗るほどのこともないと思ったので。<line-break>ま、その話はあとです。<end>"
EN: "Sorry, <value:$28>. I wasn't trying to hide it. I simply didn't think there was any need to introduce myself. Anyway, we can discuss that later.<end>"
KO: "미안합니다, <value:$28>. 숨기려던 건 아니고 굳이 이름을 댈 정도는 아니라고 생각해서요. 뭐, 그 이야기는 나중에 하죠.<end>"

## 1740137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたは何を企んでいるのですか？<end>"
EN: "What are you plotting?<end>"
KO: "당신은 무엇을 꾸미고 있습니까?<end>"

## 1740138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ハイエルフのあんたならわかるだろ？<line-break>人間どもからこの世界を取り戻すには<line-break>あのお方に復活してもらうしかない。<line-break>で、復活の方法を知るために<line-break>邪竜でも起こそうかな…<line-break>と考えたわけだ。<end>"
EN: "You're a High Elf, so you must understand. The only way to reclaim this world from the humans is through the return of the one we revere. So I thought I'd wake an Evil Dragon and find out how resurrection works.<end>"
KO: "하이엘프인 너라면 알겠지? 인간들에게서 이 세계를 되찾으려면 그분이 부활하는 수밖에 없어. 그래서 부활 방법을 알기 위해 사룡이라도 깨워 볼까… 생각한 거지.<end>"

## 1740139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、出番だ。<end>"
EN: "Now it's your turn.<end>"
KO: "자, 출番이다.<end>"

## 1740140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼリグ！<line-break>どうしてここに…？<end>"
EN: "Zelig! What are you doing here...?<end>"
KO: "젤리그! 왜 여기에…?<end>"

## 1740141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ど、どうし…て…。<end>"
EN: "W-why...?<end>"
KO: "어, 어째…서….<end>"

## 1740142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼリグ！<line-break>血迷うたか！！<end>"
EN: "Zelig! Have you lost your senses?!<end>"
KO: "젤리그! 정신이 나간 거냐!!<end>"

## 1740143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な、なんと！？<end>"
EN: "What?!<end>"
KO: "뭐, 뭐라고!?<end>"

## 1740144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "がは…っ！<end>"

KO: "크학…!<end>"

## 1740145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よし、じゃあ、行こう。<line-break>救世主が待ってる。<end>"
EN: "Good. Let's go. The Savior is waiting.<end>"
KO: "좋아, 그럼 가자. 구세주가 기다리고 있어.<end>"

## 1740146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…よもや、このわしが<line-break>一発でやられるとはな…。<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>……。<line-break>オイフェと……<line-break>…ゼリグを…頼む。<end>"
EN: "Who would have thought that I could be felled with a single blow...? <value:$28>... Take care of Aoife... and Zelig... for me.<end>"
KO: "…설마 이 내가 한 방에 당할 줄이야…. <value:$28>…… 오이페와…… …젤리그를… 부탁한다.<end>"

## 1740147
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。薄情なようですが<line-break>今は急いで邪竜アズラゴーザの<line-break>復活を止めに行かなくてはなりません。<line-break>封印は、この断層の奥にある<line-break>洞窟の中です。<line-break>行きましょう。<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... This may seem heartless, but we must hurry to stop the resurrection of the Evil Dragon Azuragoza. The seal is inside a cave in the depths of this fault. Let us go, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 매정하게 들릴지 모르지만 지금은 서둘러 사룡 아즈라고자의 부활을 막으러 가야 합니다. 봉인은 이 단층 안쪽 동굴에 있습니다. 갑시다. <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1740149
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのダークエルフが<line-break>邪竜へのいけにえか…。<end>"
EN: "So that Dark Elf is to be sacrificed to the Evil Dragon...<end>"
KO: "그 다크 엘프가 사룡에게 바칠 제물인가….<end>"

## 1740150
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、仕上げといこう。<end>"
EN: "Now, let's finish this.<end>"
KO: "자, 마무리하자.<end>"

## 1740151
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やれやれ、こんな所まで来ちゃうのか。<line-break>やっぱり、ちゃんと始末しとかないと<line-break>あとで困るね。<line-break>よし、ゼリグ、もうひと仕事だ。<line-break>彼らを、殺してしまってくれ。<end>"
EN: "Oh dear, you made it all the way here. I really should dispose of you properly, or you'll only cause trouble later. All right, Zelig. One more job. Kill them.<end>"
KO: "이런, 이런 데까지 오는군. 역시 제대로 처리해 두지 않으면 나중에 곤란하겠어. 좋아, 젤리그, 한 번 더 일해 줘. 저들을 죽여.<end>"

## 1740152
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぐ…っ！<end>"
EN: "Ghh...!<end>"
KO: "크…윽!<end>"

## 1740153
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>か…。<line-break>　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>…すまぬ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... Forgive me.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>인가…. …미안하네.<end>"

## 1740154
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま。簡単にタネを明かすと<line-break>そいつが頭の中まで筋肉バカだったから<line-break>さっきまで操らせてもらってたわけだ。<line-break>そこそこ便利だったんだけど<line-break>最後の最後で使えなかったな。<end>"
EN: "Well, to let you in on the trick, that fellow had nothing but muscle between his ears, so I was controlling him until just now. He was useful enough, but when it mattered most, he let me down.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 간단히 비밀을 밝히자면 저 녀석이 머릿속까지 근육 바보라 아까까지 조종하게 해 줬거든. 제법 편리했지만 마지막에는 쓸모없었네.<end>"

## 1740155
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ならば<line-break>使えないついでに<line-break>貴様らの計画も使えなくしてやろう。<line-break>ふぬぁ！！<line-break>うおおおオオオオ！<end>"
EN: "Then if I'm no use to you, I'll make sure your plans are no use either. Hnnngh!! Rrraaaaaagh!<end>"
KO: "…그렇다면 못 쓰는 김에 너희 계획도 못 쓰게 만들어 주마. 흐누아!! 우오오오오오오!<end>"

## 1740156
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何する気だい？<line-break>筋肉人形君。<end>"
EN: "What are you up to, Mr. Muscle Puppet?<end>"
KO: "뭘 할 생각이지? 근육 인형 군.<end>"

## 1740157
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "邪竜、復活はさせん。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>ぬんっ！！<end>"
EN: "I will not let the Evil Dragon be revived. Hnnngh!!<end>"
KO: "사룡을 부활시키진 않겠다. 흐읍!!<end>"

## 1740158
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "魂無き力は、虚ろ。　　　<line-break>力無き魂は、虚ろ。<line-break>　<line-break>力のみを求める我もまた、<line-break>虚ろだったのやもしれぬ。<end>"
EN: "Power without a soul is hollow. A soul without power is hollow. Perhaps I, who sought power alone, was hollow as well.<end>"
KO: "영혼 없는 힘은 공허하다. 힘 없는 영혼도 공허하다. 힘만을 좇던 나 역시 공허했던 것인지도 모르겠군.<end>"

## 1740159
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さらば、だ。<end>"
EN: "Farewell.<end>"
KO: "잘 있어라.<end>"

## 1740160
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クッ…、<line-break>これでは、祭壇を使っての<line-break>邪竜復活は不可能か。<end>"
EN: "Damn... So the altar can no longer be used to revive the Evil Dragon.<end>"
KO: "큭… 이래서는 제단을 이용한 사룡 부활은 불가능한가.<end>"

## 1740161
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また、別な方法を考えないとな。<line-break>とりあえずここは一度退こう。<line-break>俺も彼らを始末してから行くよ。<end>"
EN: "We'll have to devise another method. For now, we should withdraw. I'll follow once I've disposed of them.<end>"
KO: "또 다른 방법을 생각해야겠군. 일단 여기서는 한 번 물러나자. 나도 그들을 처리하고 갈게.<end>"

## 1740162
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、そうさせてもらおう。<line-break>ジュサプブロス、お前も気をつけろ。<end>"
EN: "Very well. I'll do that. Take care, Jusabros.<end>"
KO: "그럼 그렇게 하지. 주사프브로스, 너도 조심해라.<end>"

## 1740163
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また、別な方法を考えないとな。<end>"
EN: "We'll have to devise another method.<end>"
KO: "또 다른 방법을 생각해야겠군.<end>"

## 1740164
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて。<end>"
EN: "Now then.<end>"
KO: "자.<end>"

## 1740165
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なぁ、<line-break>死に場所の希望とかない？<line-break>　<line-break>俺は断然、太陽の下だね。<line-break>照りつける太陽の下。<end>"
EN: "So, got any preference where you die? Me, I'd choose somewhere under the sun. Under the blazing sun.<end>"
KO: "있잖아, 죽을 장소로 원하는 곳 없어? 난 단연 태양 아래야. 내리쬐는 태양 아래.<end>"

## 1740166
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "生まれた時からダークエルフ。　　　　<line-break>家族して、暗い穴ぐらの中で、<line-break>おびえてふるえながら生きてたよ。<line-break>でも、あの日、兄貴が俺を誘ったんだ。<line-break>とっておきの場所に行こうって<line-break>笑いながら俺を引っ張っていった。<end>"
EN: "I'd been a Dark Elf since the day I was born. My family and I lived together in a dark hole, cowering and trembling. But that day, my brother asked me to come with him. Grinning, he said he knew somewhere special and pulled me along.<end>"
KO: "태어날 때부터 다크엘프였어. 가족과 함께 어두운 굴속에서 겁에 질려 떨며 살았지. 그런데 어느 날 형이 날 불렀어. 아주 좋은 곳에 가자며 웃으면서 날 끌고 갔지.<end>"

## 1740167
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それが外だった。　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>太陽の光がまぶしくて<line-break>明るくて、暖かくて、清潔だった。<line-break>兄貴はその光の中で死んだよ。<line-break>ダークエルフ狩りのエルフに見つかって<line-break>笑顔のまま射抜かれた。<end>"
EN: "That place was outside. The sunlight was dazzling: bright, warm, and clean. My brother died in that light. An Elf hunting Dark Elves found him and put an arrow through him while he was still smiling.<end>"
KO: "그곳이 바깥이었다. 햇빛은 눈부시고 밝고 따뜻하고 깨끗했어. 형은 그 빛 속에서 죽었지. 다크엘프 사냥을 하던 엘프에게 들켜 웃는 얼굴 그대로 화살에 꿰뚫렸어.<end>"

## 1740168
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺のあの日の記憶はそこで終わりだ。　<line-break>胸を射られて、次に気がついた時には<line-break>家族はみんな、肉の塊になってた。<line-break>…２００年も前、俺が６歳の時の話さ。<line-break>でも、あの太陽の光は忘れられない。<line-break>気持ちいい感覚が記憶に残ってる。<end>"
EN: "That's where my memories of that day end. I was shot in the chest, and when I next came to, my whole family had been reduced to lumps of flesh. That was two hundred years ago, when I was six. But I've never forgotten that sunlight. I still remember how good it felt.<end>"
KO: "내 그날 기억은 거기서 끝이야. 가슴에 화살을 맞고, 다음에 정신을 차렸을 때 가족들은 모두 고깃덩이가 되어 있었지. …200년도 전, 내가 여섯 살 때 이야기야. 그래도 그 태양빛은 잊을 수 없어. 기분 좋았던 감각이 아직 기억에 남아 있거든.<end>"

## 1740169
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なぁ？<line-break>死に場所の希望とかない？<line-break>希望がなけりゃ、ここで殺すよ？<end>"
EN: "So? Any preference about where you die? If not, I'll kill you here.<end>"
KO: "있잖아? 죽을 장소에 희망 같은 건 없어? 없으면 여기서 죽일게?<end>"

## 1740170
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちぇ…、やだなあ。<line-break>結局こんな暗い所で死ぬのか…。<line-break>　<line-break>もし生まれ変わりとかがあるなら<line-break>今度は、太陽の下で……。<end>"
EN: "Damn... I don't like this. So I'm going to die in a dark place after all... If there is such a thing as rebirth, next time... beneath the sun...<end>"
KO: "쳇… 싫다. 결국 이런 어두운 곳에서 죽는 건가…. 다시 태어날 수 있다면 다음엔 태양 아래에서…….<end>"

## 1740171
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ちょっと複雑な気分ですね。<line-break>ですが、みなさんのおかげで<line-break>邪竜の復活を防ぐことはできました。<line-break>ひとまず、私の家へ戻って、<line-break>これからのことを考えましょう。<line-break>これで終わりではないでしょうし。<end>"
EN: "I have rather mixed feelings about this. Still, thanks to all of you, we prevented the Evil Dragon's revival. For now, let's return to my home and consider what to do next. I doubt this is over.<end>"
KO: "…조금 복잡한 기분이네요. 하지만 여러분 덕분에 사룡의 부활은 막을 수 있었습니다. 우선 제 집으로 돌아가 앞으로의 일을 생각해 봅시다. 이걸로 끝은 아닐 테니까요.<end>"

## 1740172
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、結構ですよ。<line-break>私の家まで転移しますから。<end>"
EN: "Oh, there's no need. We'll teleport to my home.<end>"
KO: "아, 괜찮습니다. 제 집까지 전이할 테니까요.<end>"

## 1740173
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、いきますよ。<end>"
EN: "Here we go.<end>"
KO: "그럼, 갑니다.<end>"

## 1740174
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>ここは…！？<end>"
EN: "...Where am I!?<end>"
KO: "………………. 여기는…!?<end>"

## 1740175
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私の家ですよ。　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>一応、キュアはかけておきましたが<line-break>体の調子はいかがですか？<end>"
EN: "This is my home. I cast Cure on you as a precaution. How are you feeling?<end>"
KO: "제 집입니다. 일단 큐어는 걸어 두었습니다만 몸 상태는 어떠십니까?<end>"

## 1740176
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…悪くないわ。<line-break>さすがはエルフの長<line-break>パルシェン様、といったところね。<line-break>それより、ゼリグは！？<line-break>ドルドラムはどこにいるの！？<end>"
EN: "...Not bad. Just what I'd expect from Parshen, leader of the Elves. But enough of that. What about Zelig!? Where is Doldrum!?<end>"
KO: "…나쁘지 않네. 역시 엘프의 장 파르셴 님이라고 해야겠군. 그보다 젤리그는!? 돌드람은 어디 있어!?<end>"

## 1740177
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょっと待って！　　　<line-break>どういうこと！？<line-break>　<line-break>お父さんに向かって<line-break>あのエルフの長<line-break>パルシェンだなんて…。<end>"
EN: "Wait! What is going on!? You just called my father Parshen, the leader of the Elves...<end>"
KO: "잠깐만! 무슨 소리야!? 아버지를 두고 저 엘프의 장 파르셴이라니….<end>"

## 1740178
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ、そうですけど。<line-break>私がパルシェンで間違いありません。<line-break>何か、いけませんか？<end>"
EN: "Yes, that's right. I am indeed Parshen. Is there something wrong with that?<end>"
KO: "네, 맞는데요. 제가 파르셴이 맞습니다. 무슨 문제라도 있나요?<end>"

## 1740179
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な、何かいけませんかって…！？<line-break>どうして、今まで隠してたの？<end>"
EN: "S-Something wrong with it...!? Why did you hide it all this time?<end>"
KO: "뭐, 뭐가 문제냐니…!? 왜 지금까지 숨기고 있었어?<end>"

## 1740180
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "隠してなんていませんよ。<line-break>ハイエルフのパルシェンなんですか？<line-break>と聞かれれば、はい、と答えましたよ。<end>"
EN: "I never hid it. If you had asked whether I was Parshen the High Elf, I would have said yes.<end>"
KO: "숨긴 적은 없어요. 하이엘프 파르셴이냐고 물으면 네, 라고 대답했어요.<end>"

## 1740181
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルフは私たちハーフエルフを差別し、<line-break>しいたげてきた…そのエルフの長が…<line-break>私を育てて…、それが…！<end>"
EN: "The Elves have discriminated against and oppressed us Half-Elves... Their leader... raised me... But that...!<end>"
KO: "엘프는 우리 하프엘프를 차별하고 억압해 왔어…. 그런데 그 엘프의 장이… 나를 키웠고… 그게…!<end>"

## 1740182
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですね。　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>そんなエルフの長があなたを<line-break>この賢者の森で育てる。<line-break>…それは偽善じゃないか？<line-break>何度もその疑問が頭に浮かびましたよ。<line-break>ですけどね、ケリュネイア。<end>"
EN: "Yes. The leader of those same Elves, raising you here in Sage's Forest... Isn't that hypocrisy? I asked myself that many times. But, Ceryneia...<end>"
KO: "그렇군요. 그런 엘프의 우두머리가 당신을 이 현자의 숲에서 키웠다…. 그건 위선 아닌가? 몇 번이나 그런 의문이 들었습니다. 하지만 말이지요, 케류네이아.<end>"

## 1740183
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "考えていても、ものはよくなりません。<line-break>水が岩に穴をうがつように、少しずつ、<line-break>たゆまずに進む必要があるのです。<line-break>私も神様じゃありませんからね、<line-break>手探りで生きています。<line-break>そして、あなたも私と生きてくれてる。<end>"
EN: "Thinking alone will change nothing. As water wears a hole through stone, we must move forward little by little, without faltering. I am no god. I, too, am feeling my way through life. And you have chosen to share that life with me.<end>"
KO: "생각만 한다고 상황이 좋아지진 않습니다. 물이 바위에 구멍을 내듯 조금씩, 끊임없이 앞으로 나아가야 합니다. 나도 신은 아니니까요. 더듬어 가며 살아갑니다. 그리고 당신도 나와 함께 살아 주고 있고요.<end>"

## 1740184
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私の考え方、私の理想、私の言動、　　<line-break>あなたはそれを、承知した上で、<line-break>一緒に生きてくれている。<line-break>ケリュネイア、そのことが<line-break>私がエルフの長、パルシェンだったら、<line-break>すべて、変わってしまうのですか？<end>"
EN: "You know how I think, what I believe in, and how I conduct myself, yet you still choose to live alongside me. Ceryneia, does the fact that I am Parshen, leader of the Elves, change all of that?<end>"
KO: "제 사고방식, 제 이상, 제 말과 행동을 당신은 다 알고도 함께 살아 주고 있습니다. 케류네이아, 제가 엘프의 장 파르셴이었다면 그 모든 게 달라지는 겁니까?<end>"

## 1740185
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "とりこんでるところを<line-break>悪いんだけど、私の質問に<line-break>答えてもらえないかしら。　　　<line-break>ゼリグはどうして私を襲ったの？<line-break>ドルドラムはどうしたの？<line-break>どうして私はここにいるの？<end>"
EN: "Sorry to interrupt, but would you mind answering my questions? Why did Zelig attack me? What happened to Doldrum? Why am I here?<end>"
KO: "바쁜 중에 미안하지만 내 질문에 답해 주지 않을래? 젤리그는 왜 나를 공격했어? 돌드람은 어떻게 됐어? 왜 나는 여기 있는 거야?<end>"

## 1740186
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、すいませんでした。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>話すと、少し長くなるし、<line-break>とても悲しくなると思いますが、<line-break>それでも構いませんか？<end>"
EN: "Ah, forgive me. It is a rather long story, and I fear you will find it very painful. Do you still wish to hear it?<end>"
KO: "아, 죄송합니다. 이야기하면 조금 길고 아주 슬퍼질 것 같은데, 그래도 괜찮으세요?<end>"

## 1740187
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…と、まあ、こんな感じです。<end>"
EN: "...And that is more or less what happened.<end>"
KO: "…뭐, 대충 이런 느낌입니다.<end>"

## 1740188
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう…。<line-break>ふたりとも…、死んだの……。<line-break>　<line-break>話してくれて、ありがとう。<line-break>本当のことを知らないよりは<line-break>気が楽になったわ。<end>"
EN: "I see... So they both... died... Thank you for telling me. Knowing the truth is easier than not knowing.<end>"
KO: "그래…. 둘 다… 죽었구나……. 말해 줘서 고마워. 진실을 모르는 것보단 마음이 편해졌어.<end>"

## 1740189
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか…。　　　　　　　　<line-break>強いのですね、あなたは。<line-break>　<line-break>でも、あなたのパーティーはもう<line-break>あなたしか残っていません。<line-break>これからどうするおつもりですか？<end>"
EN: "I see... You are very strong. But you are now the only member of your party left. What do you intend to do?<end>"
KO: "그렇습니까…. 강하시군요, 당신은. 하지만 당신의 파티에는 이제 당신만 남았습니다. 앞으로 어쩔 생각입니까?<end>"

## 1740190
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>私をあなたの仲間に<line-break>入れてくれないかしら？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, would you let me join you?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 나를 네 동료로 받아주지 않을래?<end>"

## 1740191
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇の神器を持つあなたたちといれば<line-break>いつかネメア様に会える日が来るはず。<line-break>　<line-break>それに、ゼリグを操ってた奴らも<line-break>きっとまた、あなたの前に現れるはず。<line-break>仇は、とりたいわ。<end>"
EN: "If I stay with you, the bearers of the Dark Artifacts, I should eventually have a chance to meet Lord Nemea. And those who were controlling Zelig are sure to appear before you again. I want revenge.<end>"
KO: "어둠의 신기를 가진 너희와 함께 있으면 언젠가 네메아 님을 만날 날이 올 거야. 그리고 젤리그를 조종한 녀석들도 분명 다시 네 앞에 나타날 테지. 복수하고 싶어.<end>"

## 1740192
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "足手まといには、ならないはずよ。<line-break>どうかしら、<value:$28>？<end>"
EN: "I shouldn't be a burden. What do you say, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "발목을 잡지는 않을 거야. 어때, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 1740193
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいよ、仲間だ<end>"
EN: "Sure. You're one of us.<end>"
KO: "좋아, 동료야<end>"

## 1740194
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前は敵だ<end>"
EN: "You're an enemy.<end>"
KO: "너는 적이다<end>"

## 1740195
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう。<line-break>よろしく頼むわ。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. I'm counting on you.<end>"
KO: "고마워. 잘 부탁할게.<end>"

## 1740196
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そう、<line-break>ここで呼び出すのね。<line-break>わかったわ。<end>"
EN: "...I see. So this is where you're summoning me. Very well.<end>"
KO: "…그래, 여기서 불러내는 거구나. 알았어.<end>"

## 1740197
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう…。　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>なら、仕方ないわね。<line-break>　<line-break>じゃあ、私は私で動くことにするわ。<line-break>ありがとう、<value:$28>。<line-break>さようなら。<end>"
EN: "I see... Then it can't be helped. I'll go my own way. Thank you, <value:$28>. Farewell.<end>"
KO: "그래…. 그렇다면 어쩔 수 없네. 그럼 난 내 방식대로 움직일게. 고마워, <value:$28>. 안녕.<end>"

## 1740198
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、<value:$28>。邪竜の断層で<line-break>あの男が言っていたように、今回の話は<line-break>いにしえの神々をも巻き込みそうです。<line-break>そこで、あなたがあなたとして<line-break>正しい選択、行動ができるように<line-break>世界の成り立ちを知るとよいでしょう。<end>"
EN: "Now then, <value:$28>. As that man said at Evil Dragon Rift, it seems this affair may embroil even the gods of old. You would do well to learn how the world came to be, so that you may make the choices and take the actions that are right for you.<end>"
KO: "자, <value:$28>. 사룡의 단층에서 그 남자가 말했듯 이번 일은 옛 신들까지 휘말릴 듯합니다. 당신이 당신답게 올바른 선택과 행동을 할 수 있도록 세계가 어떻게 생겨났는지 알아 두는 게 좋겠군요.<end>"

## 1740199
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まだ訪れていないのならば、<line-break>エンシャントの北西、古の樹海に<line-break>エルフの聖地を探してみてください。<line-break>そこで原初のエルフが<line-break>あなたに語るでしょう。<line-break>耳を傾けてください。<end>"
EN: "If you have not already been there, seek the sacred land of the Elves in the Ancient Forest, northwest of Ancient. There, the primordial Elf will speak to you. Listen well.<end>"
KO: "아직 가 보지 않았다면 엔샨트 북서쪽, 고대의 수해에서 엘프의 성지를 찾아보세요. 그곳에서 태초의 엘프가 당신에게 이야기할 겁니다. 귀를 기울여 주세요.<end>"

## 1740200
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私も、立ち止まるわけにはいかないわ。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>ネメア様のため、<line-break>ドルドラムとゼリグのため、<line-break>そして、自分自身のために…。<end>"
EN: "I can't afford to stand still, either. For Lord Nemea, for Doldrum and Zelig―and for my own sake...<end>"
KO: "나도 멈춰 있을 순 없어. 네메아 님을 위해, 돌드람과 젤리그를 위해, 그리고 나 자신을 위해….<end>"

## 1740201
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は少しケリュネイアと話をします。<end>"
EN: "I'll speak with Ceryneia for a moment.<end>"
KO: "저는 케류네이아와 잠시 이야기하겠습니다.<end>"

## 1740202
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私…、<line-break>今は混乱して、何がなんだか…。<end>"
EN: "I... I'm so confused right now. I don't know what's going on...<end>"
KO: "저… 지금은 혼란스러워서 뭐가 뭔지….<end>"

## 1740203
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いつまで、グズグズと…！<line-break>あなた、ホントにエアの所まで<line-break>連れていってくれる気あるの？<line-break>もういいわ！<line-break>私はひとりで行く。<line-break>さよなら。<end>"
EN: "How long are you going to keep dragging your feet? Are you really going to take me to Er or not? Forget it! I'll go alone. Goodbye.<end>"
KO: "언제까지 꾸물거릴 거야…! 정말 에아가 있는 곳까지 데려다줄 생각은 있는 거야? 이제 됐어! 난 혼자 갈게. 안녕.<end>"

## 1740204
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの、<value:$28>。<line-break>戦っていない私が言うのもなんですが、<line-break>私たちは、急がなければなりません。<end>"
EN: "Um, <value:$28>. I know I'm not the one doing the fighting, but we need to hurry.<end>"
KO: "저기, <value:$28>. 싸우지 않은 제가 할 말은 아니지만, 우리는 서둘러야 합니다.<end>"

## 1740205
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これでいくらか<line-break>疲れは取れたと思います。<line-break>　<line-break>では、行きましょう。<end>"
EN: "That should have eased the fatigue somewhat. Now, let's go.<end>"
KO: "이걸로 조금은 피로가 풀렸을 겁니다. 그럼 가시죠.<end>"

## 1740208
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジュサプブロス…。<line-break>同化の髪飾りを返してもらおうか。<end>"
EN: "Jusabros... I'll have the Hair Ornament of Assimilation back now.<end>"
KO: "주사프브로스…. 동화의 머리장식을 돌려받겠다.<end>"

## 1740209
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "執念深い男だね。<line-break>それとも、それが<line-break>イーシャへの想いの強さかな？<line-break>もっとも、<line-break>暑苦しいその想いこそが<line-break>彼女には、いい迷惑かもしれないがな。<end>"
EN: "You're a persistent one. Or is that how strong your feelings for Iria are? Then again, she may find that overbearing devotion of yours nothing but a nuisance.<end>"
KO: "집념이 강한 남자군. 아니면 그게 이샤를 향한 마음의 강함인가? 뭐, 그 뜨거운 마음 자체가 그녀에겐 민폐일지도 모르지만.<end>"

## 1740210
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだと！？<end>"
EN: "What did you say?!<end>"
KO: "뭐라고!?<end>"

## 1740211
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっくっくっ。若いねぇ。<line-break>でもまあ、６才未満には見えないから<line-break>まとめて因縁の後始末といこうか。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh. So young. Still, you don't look under six, so I may as well settle all our old scores at once.<end>"
KO: "큭큭큭. 젊구나. 그래도 뭐, 여섯 살 미만으로는 안 보이니 한꺼번에 악연을 정리해 볼까.<end>"

## 1740212
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "同化の髪飾りは返してもらう。<end>"
EN: "I'm taking back the Hair Ornament of Assimilation.<end>"
KO: "동화의 머리장식은 돌려받겠다.<end>"

## 1740213
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "仕方ないね…。くれてやるよ。<line-break>いくら闇の神器でも<line-break>あの世までは持っていけないからな。<end>"
EN: "Guess I have no choice... You can have it. Even if it is a Dark Artifact, I can't take it with me to the next world.<end>"
KO: "어쩔 수 없군…. 가져가. 아무리 어둠의 신기라도 저승까지 가져갈 수는 없으니까.<end>"

## 1740214
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec174-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オイフェメッセージＶ７終端<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "오이페 메시지 V7 끝<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1750001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おおぉぉい！　主人！<line-break>すまんけんど、酒の追加を<line-break>持ってきてくれんかのう？<end>"
EN: "Heeey! Boss! Sorry to bother you, but could you bring me some more booze?<end>"
KO: "어이이이! 주인장! 미안하지만 술 좀 더 가져다주지 않겠나?<end>"

## 1750002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はい、はい。<line-break>あとで持っていきますから。<end>"
EN: "All right, all right. I'll bring you some later.<end>"
KO: "네, 네. 나중에 가져다드릴게요.<end>"

## 1750003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "とっておきの小部屋も、すっかり<line-break>あの老人専用になってしまったな、<line-break>トホホホ…<end>"
EN: "And now my prized little room has become that old man's private retreat. Oh, the misery...<end>"
KO: "아껴 둔 작은 방도 완전히 그 노인 전용이 되어 버렸군, 흐흐흑….<end>"

## 1750004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここは、わしの貸し切りじゃー　<line-break>おもーとったけど、<line-break>そーじゃなかったんじゃのう。<line-break>こがいな隠し部屋に気付くとは、<line-break>われも勘がええのー。<line-break>名前はなんてゆーんね？<end>"
EN: "I thought I had this place all to myself, but I suppose not. Sharp of you to find a hidden room like this. What's your name?<end>"
KO: "여긴 내가 빌려 쓴 곳인 줄 알았는데 아니었구먼. 이런 숨겨진 방을 알아채다니 자네도 감이 좋네. 이름이 뭔가?<end>"

## 1750005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>ゆーんか。<line-break>わしはロッシマじゃ。<line-break>よろしくのう。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, is it? I'm Rossima. Good to meet you.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>라고 하는가. 나는 롯시마다. 잘 부탁하네.<end>"

## 1750007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "われ…。<line-break>ええ体しとるのう。<end>"
EN: "You... You've got quite the build.<end>"
KO: "너…. 몸이 참 좋구나.<end>"

## 1750008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "か、かんちがいしんさんな。<line-break>やらしー意味じゃないんでぇ。<line-break>われ、冒険者じゃろう？<end>"
EN: "D-don't get the wrong idea. I didn't mean anything indecent. You're an adventurer, aren't you?<end>"
KO: "오, 오해하지 마시게. 야한 뜻이 아니랑께. 자네, 모험가잖여?<end>"

## 1750009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冒険者をやっとって、<line-break>楽しいか？<end>"
EN: "Do you enjoy being an adventurer?<end>"
KO: "모험자를 하면서 즐겁나?<end>"

## 1750010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "楽しいです<end>"
EN: "It's fun.<end>"
KO: "즐겁습니다<end>"

## 1750011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "つまらないです<end>"
EN: "It's boring.<end>"
KO: "재미없어요<end>"

## 1750012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そりゃあ結構なことじゃ。　　　　<line-break>冒険はロマンじゃけえのー。<line-break>　<line-break>じゃけど、まだ、ほんまの楽しみは<line-break>知らんじゃろう。<end>"
EN: "Well, that's grand. Adventure is the stuff of romance, after all. But you haven't discovered the real fun of it yet, have you?<end>"
KO: "그거 좋은 일이구먼. 모험은 낭만이니까. 하지만 아직 진짜 재미는 모르는 모양이군.<end>"

## 1750013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほーか、つまらんか。<line-break>なるほどのう。<line-break>じゃが、冒険者を続けるんじゃろ？<end>"
EN: "Oh, so you find it dull, eh? Hmm. But you're going to carry on as an adventurer, aren't you?<end>"
KO: "그런가, 재미없나. 그렇군. 그래도 모험자는 계속할 거지?<end>"

## 1750014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほんなら、楽しいほうが<line-break>ええじゃろうのう。<end>"
EN: "Then you might as well enjoy yourself, eh?<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 즐거운 쪽이 낫겠지.<end>"

## 1750015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしが、冒険の本当のおもしろさを<line-break>教えてやろうかい。<line-break>われ、今、暇なんか？<end>"
EN: "How about I show you what makes adventuring truly exciting? You got some time to spare right now?<end>"
KO: "내가 모험의 진짜 재미를 가르쳐 줄까? 너, 지금 한가하냐?<end>"

## 1750016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "暇です<end>"
EN: "I'm free.<end>"
KO: "한가합니다<end>"

## 1750017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "忙しいです<end>"
EN: "I'm busy.<end>"
KO: "바쁩니다<end>"

## 1750018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほんなら、行こうか。<line-break>仲間をつれて<line-break>わしについてきんさい。<end>"
EN: "Well then, let's be off. Gather your companions and follow me.<end>"
KO: "그럼 가볼까. 동료들을 데리고 나를 따라오게.<end>"

## 1750019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここはのう、昔、盗人虎の穴と<line-break>呼ばれとった所よ。<end>"
EN: "Long ago, this place was called the Thieves' Den.<end>"
KO: "여긴 말이지, 옛날에 도적 호랑이굴이라고 불리던 곳이야.<end>"

## 1750020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここにいろいろな罠を仕掛けといた。<line-break>それをうまく突破して、<line-break>試練の証を見つけんさい。<line-break>われの実力を<line-break>見させてもらうけぇのう。<end>"
EN: "I've set all sorts of traps in here. Make your way past them and find the trial token. Let's see what you're capable of.<end>"
KO: "여기에 여러 함정을 설치해 뒀다. 잘 돌파해서 시련의 증표를 찾아 봐라. 네 실력을 보겠다.<end>"

## 1750021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほーか、暇か！<line-break>そんなら、わしについてきんさい。<line-break>…と言いたいとこなんじゃが。<line-break>ディテクターのスキルを使えんと<line-break>話にならん。宝箱を開けられんと<line-break>試練にならんからなあ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, so you're free! Then follow me―or so I'd like to say. But there's no point unless you can use the Detector skill. It wouldn't be much of a trial if you couldn't open the treasure chests.<end>"
KO: "그려, 한가한가! 그럼 나를 따라오게. …라고 하고 싶지만 디텍터 스킬을 못 쓰면 이야기가 안 돼. 보물상자를 못 열면 시련이 되질 않으니까.<end>"

## 1750022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなにあせりんさんなや。　　　<line-break>楽しみはとっておくもんでぇ。<line-break>そうじゃな…。<line-break>もうすこしギルドで仕事をして<line-break>グレモリィのソウルを宿しんさい。<line-break>わしはここで待っとるけぇ。<end>"
EN: "No need to rush. Fun's best saved for later. Let's see... Do a little more work for the Guild and acquire Gremory's Soul. I'll wait here.<end>"
KO: "그렇게 서두르지 말게나. 즐거움은 아껴 두는 법이여. 그래…. 길드에서 일을 좀 더 하고 그레모리의 소울을 품도록 하게. 난 여기서 기다릴 테니.<end>"

## 1750023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほんまか…。　　　　　　　　<line-break>そんならしょうがないの。<line-break>わしはここで待っとるけぇ。<line-break>われが暇になったらきんさい。<line-break>冒険のおもしろさを<line-break>教えてやるけんの。<end>"
EN: "Is that so... Well, it can't be helped. I'll wait here, so come back when you're free. Then I'll show you what makes adventuring so much fun.<end>"
KO: "정말인가…. 그렇다면 어쩔 수 없지. 나는 여기서 기다릴 테니 네가 한가해지면 와라. 모험의 재미를 알려 주마.<end>"

## 1750024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どしたんね？<line-break>実は暇じゃったんかいの？<end>"
EN: "What's the matter? Were you free after all?<end>"
KO: "왜 그러는가? 사실 한가했던 게냐?<end>"

## 1750025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おお、われか！<line-break>どうねぇ、暇になったかいの？<end>"
EN: "Oh, it's you! Well, are you free now?<end>"
KO: "오오, 자네인가! 어때, 좀 한가해졌나?<end>"

## 1750026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "暇です<end>"
EN: "I'm free.<end>"
KO: "한가합니다<end>"

## 1750032

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1750033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Is that so... Well, no help for it, then. I'll wait here. Come back when you're free, and I'll show you what makes adventuring so much fun.<end>"

## 1750034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "怪しいと思うたら、調べてみにゃあ<line-break>始まらんで。気い抜いたら死じゃ。<end>"
EN: "If something looks suspicious, check it out. Let your guard down and you're dead.<end>"
KO: "수상하다 싶으면 조사해 봐야 시작이지. 방심하면 죽는 거야.<end>"

## 1750035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "われ、どこ行く気やぁ！<line-break>まだ、試練の証は手に<line-break>入れとらんじゃろが！　逃げるんか！<end>"
EN: "Where do you think you're going? You haven't found the proof of the trial yet! Are you running away?<end>"
KO: "자네, 어디 가는 겨! 아직 시련의 증표도 못 얻었잖여! 도망치는 겨!<end>"

## 1750036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "宝箱に罠はつきもんじゃけぇのう。<line-break>成功したからゆうて<line-break>気を抜きんさんなよ。<end>"
EN: "Treasure chests come with traps. Don't let your guard down just because you succeeded.<end>"
KO: "보물상자엔 함정이 붙어 있기 마련이여. 성공했다고 방심하지 말라고.<end>"

## 1750037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これが呪いガスじゃったら<line-break>どうする気じゃ。<line-break>パーティーが全滅するんでぇ！<end>"
EN: "What if that had been curse gas? Your whole party would've been wiped out!<end>"
KO: "이게 저주 가스라면 어쩔 셈이냐. 파티가 전멸한다고!<end>"

## 1750038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そがーな罠はのう、<line-break>はずせて当然なんじゃけぇ。<end>"
EN: "You ought to be able to disarm a trap like that.<end>"
KO: "그런 덫쯤은 말이지, 풀 수 있어야 당연한 거야.<end>"

## 1750039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "このアホたれが！<line-break>猛毒の矢じゃったら、<line-break>われ死んどったぞ。<end>"
EN: "You fool! If that had been an arrow tipped with deadly poison, you'd be dead.<end>"
KO: "이 멍청아! 맹독 화살이었으면 넌 죽었어.<end>"

## 1750040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そがーな罠はのう、コツさえ<line-break>つかめば簡単なんでぇ。<end>"
EN: "A trap like that is easy once you know the trick.<end>"
KO: "그런 함정은 요령만 알면 간단해.<end>"

## 1750041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありゃりゃー。運が悪けりゃ、<line-break>われの体はバラバラじゃあ。<line-break>そがーに早死にしたいんかのう。<end>"
EN: "Oh, dear. With a bit less luck, you'd have been torn to pieces. Are you really that eager to die young?<end>"
KO: "어이쿠. 운 나쁘면 자네 몸이 산산조각 나겠어. 그렇게 일찍 죽고 싶은 겐가.<end>"

## 1750042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "罠を発見した。<end>"
EN: "Trap detected.<end>"
KO: "함정을 발견했다.<end>"

## 1750043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そりゃーのう、<line-break>床からトゲが飛び出す罠よね。<line-break>気づいてよかったのう。<end>"
EN: "That one sends spikes shooting up from the floor. Good thing you spotted it.<end>"
KO: "그렇구먼, 바닥에서 가시가 튀어나오는 함정이여. 알아차려서 다행이구먼.<end>"

## 1750044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その罠は、<line-break>スイッチを切らんと解除できんで。<line-break>スイッチを探しんさい。<end>"
EN: "You can't disarm that trap until you turn off the switch. Go find it.<end>"
KO: "그 함정은 스위치를 끄지 않으면 해제할 수 없어. 스위치를 찾아봐.<end>"

## 1750045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "われ、どこに眼つけとんじゃ！<line-break>床の色が違うじゃろが！<line-break>死にたいんか！<end>"
EN: "Where the hell are you looking?! The floor's a different color! You trying to get yourself killed?!<end>"
KO: "너 어디에 눈을 달고 다니는 거냐! 바닥 색이 다르잖아! 죽고 싶냐!<end>"

## 1750046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "床からトゲの罠を発見した。<line-break>しかし、解除してあるようだ。<end>"
EN: "A spike trap was found in the floor, but it appears to have been disarmed.<end>"
KO: "바닥에서 가시 함정을 발견했다. 하지만 이미 해제되어 있는 것 같다.<end>"

## 1750047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "竜の像の口の中にスイッチを見つけた<line-break>スイッチを押してみますか？<end>"
EN: "You found a switch inside the dragon statue's mouth. Would you like to press it?<end>"
KO: "용 석상의 입 안에서 스위치를 발견했다. 스위치를 눌러보겠습니까?<end>"

## 1750048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "押さない<end>"
EN: "Don't press it<end>"
KO: "누르지 않는다<end>"

## 1750049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "押す<end>"
EN: "Press it<end>"
KO: "누른다<end>"

## 1750050

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1750051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これで、あの罠が解除できた<line-break>わけじゃのう。<line-break>さっそく見に行ってみんさい。<end>"

## 1750052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml

## 1750054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml

## 1750067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やーっとここまで来たのう。<line-break>もう一息でぇ。<end>"
EN: "Finally made it this far, eh? Just a little farther now.<end>"
KO: "드디어 여기까지 왔구먼. 이제 한 걸음 남았어.<end>"

## 1750068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よっしゃ、よう試練を乗り越えたの。<line-break>そいじゃあ、外へ出ようかの。<end>"
EN: "Well done! You made it through the trial. Let's head outside, then.<end>"
KO: "좋아, 시험을 잘 넘겼군. 그럼 밖으로 나가자.<end>"

## 1750069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうね？　楽しかったじゃろ？<line-break>今日の失敗は、明日への糧じゃけぇ。<line-break>同じ失敗は、もうせんことよ。<line-break>われのようなひよっこはのぉ、<line-break>一歩一歩調べながら進むぐらいの<line-break>心構えでおらんといけんで。<end>"
EN: "Well? Had fun, didn't you? Today's mistake is a lesson for tomorrow, so don't make the same mistake again. A greenhorn like you needs to check the way ahead and take things one step at a time.<end>"
KO: "어땠나? 재미있었지? 오늘의 실패는 내일의 밑거름이여. 같은 실수는 이제 하지 말게. 자네 같은 풋내기는 한 걸음 한 걸음 살피면서 나아갈 각오가 있어야 혀.<end>"

## 1750070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほんじゃあ、<line-break>ロストールに帰ろうかのう。<end>"
EN: "Well then, shall we head back to Rostorl?<end>"
KO: "그럼 로스톨로 돌아갈까나.<end>"

## 1750071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしの試練をよう乗り越えた。<line-break>われは鍛えがいがありそうじゃ。<line-break>わしゃあ、楽しみじゃ。<end>"
EN: "You did well to overcome my trial. I reckon you'll be worth training. I'm looking forward to it.<end>"
KO: "내 시련을 잘 이겨냈구먼. 자넨 단련할 맛이 나겠어. 난 기대가 되는군.<end>"

## 1750072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また、宝探しに連れてっちゃるよ。<line-break>暇があったら来んさい。<line-break>わしはいつもの場所で飲んどるけぇ。<line-break>じゃけど、もうちっと鍛えて<line-break>からにしんさいよ。<line-break>次の迷宮は今回より手強いからのう。<end>"
EN: "I'll take you treasure hunting again sometime. Come by when you've got a spare moment. I'll be drinking in my usual spot. But get a bit more training under your belt first. The next labyrinth will be tougher than this one.<end>"
KO: "또 보물 찾으러 데려가 주지. 시간 나면 와. 난 늘 있던 곳에서 마시고 있을 테니. 다만 조금 더 단련하고 와라. 다음 미궁은 이번보다 더 어려울 테니까.<end>"

## 1750073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec175-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう！　よう来たのう。<line-break>じゃが、すまん。<line-break>今日は宝探しに行けんのじゃ。<line-break>わしゃあ昨晩から飲んどって<line-break>頭がガンガンするんよ。<line-break>ほんまにすまんのう。<end>"
EN: "Ah, good to see you! Sorry, though. I can't go treasure hunting today. I've been drinking since last night, and my head's pounding. I'm truly sorry.<end>"
KO: "오! 잘 왔구나. 하지만 미안하다. 오늘은 보물 찾으러 못 가. 어젯밤부터 마셔서 머리가 지끈거려. 정말 미안하구나.<end>"

## 1760004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec176-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほんなら、行こうか。<line-break>仲間をつれて<line-break>わしについてきんさい。<end>"
EN: "All right, let's be off. Gather your companions and follow me.<end>"
KO: "그럼 가볼까. 동료들을 데리고 나를 따라오게.<end>"

## 1760005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec176-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここの罠はだいたい解除してあるけぇ<line-break>初心者には打ってつけなんよ。<line-break>ほとんど宝は残っとりゃせんがの。<end>"
EN: "I've disarmed most of the traps around here, so it's just the place for a beginner. Mind you, there's hardly any treasure left.<end>"
KO: "여기 함정은 대체로 해제해 놔서 초보자에게 딱 좋아. 보물은 거의 안 남았지만.<end>"

## 1760006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec176-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほいじゃー、行こうかの。<end>"
EN: "Well then, let's be off.<end>"
KO: "그럼, 가 볼까.<end>"

## 1760007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec176-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "モンスターと戦闘になっても　　<line-break>わしゃあ手伝わんけぇ。<line-break>自分の身は自分で守りんさいよ。<end>"
EN: "If you end up in a fight with monsters, don't expect me to help. You'll have to look after yourself.<end>"
KO: "몬스터와 싸우게 돼도 난 도와주지 않을 거야. 자기 몸은 자기가 지키게나.<end>"

## 1760008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec176-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしゃーこのとおり、　　　　　　<line-break>右目は見えんし<line-break>左手は義手じゃけえのぅ。<line-break>自分を守るんで手一杯なんよねぇ。<line-break>じゃけぇ勘弁してちょうだいや。<end>"
EN: "As you can see, I'm blind in my right eye, and my left hand's a prosthetic. I've got my hands full just looking after myself, so you'll have to excuse me.<end>"
KO: "나는 보다시피, 오른눈은 안 보이고 왼손은 의수라서 말이야. 내 몸 지키는 것만으로도 벅차다네. 그러니 좀 봐주게나.<end>"

## 1760009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec176-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こりゃあダメじゃぁ思うたら、<line-break>迷わず逃げんさい。そんときゃあ、<line-break>わしも一緒に逃げるけぇ。<end>"
EN: "If it looks hopeless, don't hesitate to run. I'll run with you.<end>"
KO: "이건 안 되겠다 싶으면 망설이지 말고 도망쳐. 그땐 나도 같이 도망갈 테니.<end>"

## 1760010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec176-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほいじゃー、行こうかの。<end>"
EN: "Well then, let's be off.<end>"
KO: "그럼, 가 볼까.<end>"

## 1760011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec176-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほーか、暇か！<line-break>じゃけど、われ、ほんまに鍛えたんか？<line-break>こないだとあんまりかわっとらんで。<line-break>今のわれの実力じゃったら<line-break>行ってもしょうがないわい。<line-break>もうちっと修行しんさい。<end>"
EN: "Oh, so you've got time to spare! But have you really been training? You haven't changed much since last time. At your current level, there's no point in going. Train a little more first.<end>"
KO: "그려, 한가한가! 그런데 자네 정말 수련했나? 지난번과 별로 달라진 게 없는데. 지금 실력으로 가 봐야 소용없어. 조금 더 수련하게.<end>"

## 1760012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec176-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほんまか…。<line-break>そんならしょうがないの。<line-break>わしはここで待っとるけぇ。　<line-break>われが暇になったらきんさい。<line-break>冒険のおもしろさを<line-break>教えてやるけんの。<end>"
EN: "Really...? Well, it can't be helped. I'll wait here. Come back when you've got some time, and I'll show you how much fun adventuring can be.<end>"
KO: "정말인가…. 그렇다면 어쩔 수 없지. 나는 여기서 기다릴 테니 네가 한가해지면 와라. 모험의 재미를 알려 주마.<end>"

## 1760013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec176-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "われ、どこ行く気やぁ！<line-break>まだ、宝は手に入れとらんじゃろが！<line-break>手ぶらで帰るつもりなんか！<end>"
EN: "Where do you think you're going? You still haven't got the treasure! Are you planning to go back empty-handed?<end>"
KO: "어디 가려는 거냐! 아직 보물도 못 얻었잖아! 빈손으로 돌아갈 셈이냐!<end>"

## 1760014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec176-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だいぶ慣れてきたんかのう。<line-break>ぎごちなさが、のーなってきたで。<end>"
EN: "You're getting the hang of it. You're not nearly so clumsy anymore.<end>"
KO: "제법 익숙해진 모양이구먼. 어색함이 많이 없어졌어.<end>"

## 1760015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec176-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何をやっとるんじゃ！<line-break>もうちょっと慎重にやれんのんか。<line-break>われの失敗で仲間も傷つくんでぇ。<end>"
EN: "What are you doing? Can't you be a little more careful? When you make a mistake, your companions get hurt too.<end>"
KO: "뭐 하는 거냐! 좀 더 신중하게 못 하겠어? 네 실패 때문에 동료까지 다친다고.<end>"

## 1760016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec176-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "竜の像の口の中に<line-break>赤いスイッチを見つけた<line-break>スイッチを押してみますか？<end>"
EN: "You found a red switch inside the dragon statue's mouth. Would you like to try pressing it?<end>"
KO: "용 조각상의 입 안에서 빨간 스위치를 찾았다. 스위치를 눌러 보겠습니까?<end>"

## 1760017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec176-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Don't press it<end>"
KO: "누르지 않는다<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec176-part99.toml
