KO: "<value:$28>, 카를라는?<end>"

## 1780047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうか…わかった。　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>お前さんもよくやってくれた。<line-break>胸を張ってロストールに帰ろうぜ。<line-break>ご苦労さん。<end>"
EN: "...I see. All right. You did well, too. Let's go back to Rostorl with our heads held high. Good work.<end>"
KO: "…그래… 알았다. 자네도 잘해 줬어. 가슴 펴고 로스톨로 돌아가자. 수고했네.<end>"

## 1780048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はぁ…はぁ…。<line-break>もう…いいよ、<value:$28>。<line-break>…手が、汚れちゃう。<end>"
EN: "Hah... hah... That's enough, <value:$28>. You'll... get your hands dirty.<end>"
KO: "하아… 하아…. 이제… 됐어, <value:$28>. …손이 더러워지잖아.<end>"

## 1780049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふ。<line-break>なんか…こうしてもらってると　<line-break>安心する…ね…。<line-break>…きれいよね、あなたの指…<line-break>私…きっと…生まれ変わっても、<line-break>あなたの指を好きになると思う…。<end>"
EN: "Hehe. Somehow... when you do this, I feel safe... Your fingers are so beautiful... I think... even if I were born again, I'd love your fingers...<end>"
KO: "후후. 왠지… 이렇게 해 주고 있으면 안심돼… 있네…. …예쁘지, 네 손가락… 나… 분명 다시 태어나도 네 손가락을 좋아하게 될 것 같아….<end>"

## 1780050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ゴメンね。<line-break>…指、私の血で…汚しちゃって…。<end>"
EN: "...I'm sorry. I got... my blood on your fingers...<end>"
KO: "…미안해. …손가락을 내 피로… 더럽혔네….<end>"

## 1780051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あぁ…<value:$28>…。<line-break>かぁ、さん…、せんせぇ…、<line-break>カ…ル…さ…。<end>"
EN: "Ah... <value:$28>... Mo...ther... Teach...er... La...dy... Ka...ru...<end>"
KO: "아아… <value:$28>…. 엄, 마…, 선생님…, 카…르…라….<end>"

## 1780052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>。<line-break>カルラは…？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>. Where's Karula...?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>. 카를라는…?<end>"

## 1780053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…カルラじゃないな。<line-break>その娘…。<end>"
EN: "...That's not Karula. That girl...<end>"
KO: "…카를라는 아니군. 그 소녀는….<end>"

## 1780054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか…。<line-break>守ったんだな、命を懸けて。<line-break>…仕方ないな。<end>"
EN: "I see... You risked your life to protect her. Well, nothing to be done about it.<end>"
KO: "그런가… 목숨을 걸고 지킨 거군. …어쩔 수 없지.<end>"

## 1780055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "つらい思いをさせちまったな。<line-break>…すまない。<end>"
EN: "I put you through something terrible. I'm sorry.<end>"
KO: "힘든 일을 겪게 했구나. …미안하다.<end>"

## 1780056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はぁ…はぁ…。<line-break>か、カルラさま…<line-break>もうしわけ…ありません…。<end>"
EN: "Hah... hah... L-Lady Karula... I'm... sorry...<end>"
KO: "하아… 하아…. 카, 카를라 님… 죄송…합니다….<end>"

## 1780057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あなたの指…きれいよね…。<line-break>あいつの指に…そっくり…。<line-break>私の…す、きだった…。<end>"
EN: "...Your fingers... are so beautiful... Just like that person's fingers... I... loved...<end>"
KO: "…당신 손가락… 예쁘네…. 그 사람 손가락과… 꼭 닮았어…. 내가… 조, 좋아했던….<end>"

## 1780058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…かぁ、さん…<line-break>…せんせぇ…。<end>"
EN: "...Mo...ther... Teach...er...<end>"
KO: "…엄마… …선생님….<end>"

## 1780059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>。<line-break>カルラは…？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>. Where's Karula...?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>. 카를라는…?<end>"

## 1780060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうか。　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>…戦争だからな。<line-break>人が死ぬのは当たり前だ。<line-break>だが、当たり前じゃ割り切れない。<line-break>心は悲しむもんだ。<end>"
EN: "...I see. It's war. People die―that's only to be expected. But knowing that doesn't make it any easier. The heart still grieves.<end>"
KO: "…그런가. …전쟁이니까. 사람이 죽는 건 당연하지. 하지만 당연하다고 납득되진 않아. 마음은 슬퍼하는 법이야.<end>"

## 1780061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "落ちついたら来てくれ。<line-break>俺は軍をまとめておく。<end>"
EN: "Come find me once you've collected yourself. I'll get the army back in order.<end>"
KO: "진정되면 와 줘. 나는 군을 정리해 두겠다.<end>"

## 1780062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごふ…か、カルラ様…<line-break>もうしわけ…ありません…。<end>"
EN: "Guh... L-Lady Karula... I'm... sorry...<end>"
KO: "커헉… 카, 칼라 님… 죄송…합니다….<end>"

## 1780063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どう…か、ごぶじ…で…。<line-break>…かぁ、さん…<line-break>…せんせぇ…。<end>"
EN: "Please... stay safe... Mo...ther... Teach...er...<end>"
KO: "부디… 무사…하시길…. …어머니… …선생님….<end>"

## 1780064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "助っ人ありがとさん。<line-break>おかげで勝つことができた。<end>"
EN: "Thanks for the help. Couldn't have won without you.<end>"
KO: "도와줘서 고맙다. 덕분에 이길 수 있었어.<end>"

## 1780065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カルラを討ち取れなかったのは、<line-break>仕方ないさ。気にするな。<line-break>ま、これも運命だったってことだ。<line-break>問題は、次のカルラの攻撃を<line-break>どう防ぐかだ。<end>"
EN: "Karula wasn't taken down, but that couldn't be helped. Don't worry about it. Call it fate, I suppose. The question now is how to stop her next attack.<end>"
KO: "칼라를 쓰러뜨리지 못한 건 어쩔 수 없어. 신경 쓰지 마. 이것도 운명이었던 거겠지. 문제는 다음 칼라의 공격을 어떻게 막느냐다.<end>"

## 1780066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "レムオンが協力してくれれば、<line-break>少しは道が開けるんだがな。<end>"
EN: "If Lemghon would cooperate, we might have a way forward.<end>"
KO: "레무온이 협력해 준다면 조금은 길이 열릴 텐데.<end>"

## 1780068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうした、いきなり。<end>"
EN: "What's this all of a sudden?<end>"
KO: "왜 그래, 갑자기.<end>"

## 1780069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お耳に入れておきたいことがあります。<line-break>極めて重大な話です。<end>"
EN: "There's something you need to know. It's extremely serious.<end>"
KO: "귀에 넣어 두셨으면 하는 이야기가 있습니다. 매우 중대한 이야기입니다.<end>"

## 1780070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかった。<line-break><value:$28>、すまないが…<end>"
EN: "All right. <value:$28>, I'm sorry, but...<end>"
KO: "알았다. <value:$28>, 미안하지만….<end>"

## 1780071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いえ、<value:$28>にも<line-break>聞いてもらった方がいいわ。<line-break>だから、<value:$28>も残って。<end>"
EN: "No, <value:$28> should hear this too. So you stay as well, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "아니, <value:$28>도 같이 듣는 게 좋아. 그러니까 <value:$28>도 남아.<end>"

## 1780072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まずは、レムオン様なのですが、<line-break>姿が見えないのです。<end>"
EN: "First, there's Lord Lemghon. He's nowhere to be found.<end>"
KO: "우선 레무온 님인데요, 모습이 보이지 않습니다.<end>"

## 1780073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだと？<end>"
EN: "What did you say?<end>"
KO: "뭐라고?<end>"

## 1780074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エリス様の指示で、<line-break>ずっと動向を見守っていたのだけど、<line-break>突然、姿を消してしまったの。<line-break>ゼネテス様が出陣して、<line-break>何日も経たずにね。<end>"
EN: "Lady Eris had ordered us to keep watch on him, but he suddenly vanished, only a few days after you marched out, Lord Xenetes.<end>"
KO: "에리스 님 지시로 계속 동향을 지켜보고 있었는데 갑자기 모습을 감췄어. 제네테스 님이 출진하고 며칠 지나지도 않아서.<end>"

## 1780075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それだけではありません。<line-break>ティアナ王女まで、<line-break>姿を消されたのです。<end>"
EN: "That's not all. Princess Tiana has disappeared as well.<end>"
KO: "그뿐만이 아닙니다. 티아나 왕녀님까지 모습을 감추셨습니다.<end>"

## 1780076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それだけではありません。<line-break>アトレイア王女まで、<line-break>姿を消されたのです。<end>"
EN: "That's not all. Princess Atleia has disappeared as well.<end>"
KO: "그뿐만이 아닙니다. 아트레이아 왕녀님까지, 자취를 감추셨습니다.<end>"

## 1780077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "妙な話だな…。<line-break>レムオンがさらったとも思えん。<line-break>何か心当たりはないのか？<end>"
EN: "That's strange... I can't imagine Lemghon abducted her. Do you have any idea what might have happened?<end>"
KO: "이상한 얘기군…. 레무온이 납치했다고도 생각되지 않아. 뭔가 짐작 가는 건 없나?<end>"

## 1780078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私たちは、王宮の人間の動きならば、<line-break>ほぼすべて把握していたつもりよ。<line-break>でも、ひとりだけわからない奴がいた。<end>"
EN: "We thought we knew almost everything the people in the palace were doing. But there was one person whose movements we couldn't account for.<end>"
KO: "우리는 왕궁 사람들의 움직임이라면 거의 전부 파악하고 있다고 생각했어. 하지만 단 한 명, 알 수 없는 녀석이 있었지.<end>"

## 1780079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "シャリです。<line-break>ゼネテス様の出陣のあとも、<line-break>王宮でその姿を見た者がいますが、<line-break>私たちもそれ以上のことは、<line-break>わかっていません。<end>"
EN: "Shalli. He was seen in the palace even after Lord Xenetes marched out, but we know nothing beyond that.<end>"
KO: "샤리입니다. 제네테스 님이 출진한 뒤에도 왕궁에서 그 모습을 본 사람이 있지만, 저희도 그 이상은 알지 못합니다.<end>"

## 1780080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、何か知らないの？<line-break>今回の件の人たちとは、<line-break>少なからず関わりがあるでしょう？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, do you know anything? You've had at least some dealings with the people involved in all this, haven't you?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 뭔가 아는 거 없어? 이번 일에 관련된 사람들과 적지 않게 얽혀 있잖아?<end>"

## 1780081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "きな臭くなってきたな。<line-break>シャリってのは、破壊神の復活を<line-break>もくろんでる奴らの仲間だって話だ。<end>"
EN: "This is beginning to smell of trouble. I hear Shalli is one of those plotting to resurrect the God of Destruction.<end>"
KO: "수상한 냄새가 나기 시작했군. 샤리라는 녀석은 파괴신 부활을 노리는 자들의 동료라고 들었어.<end>"

## 1780082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんですって？<line-break>そんな情報をいったいどこで？<end>"
EN: "What? Where did you get information like that?<end>"
KO: "뭐라고요? 그런 정보는 대체 어디서?<end>"

## 1780083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前らの兄貴さ。<line-break>気になってたんでな、<line-break>調べてもらった。<line-break>あいつの情報なら間違いないだろう。<end>"
EN: "From your brother. It had been bothering me, so I asked him to look into it. His information should be reliable.<end>"
KO: "너희 형 이야기다. 신경 쓰여서 조사해 봤지. 그 녀석 정보라면 틀림없을 거다.<end>"

## 1780084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前さんから聞いた<line-break>ネメアのことも気になるしな。<line-break>　<line-break>どうやら、ことは、<line-break>国と国のケンカなんていう次元を<line-break>越えたレベルまでいってそうだな。<end>"
EN: "And what you told me about Nemea has been troubling me too. It looks as though this has gone far beyond a quarrel between nations.<end>"
KO: "네게 들은 네메아 이야기도 신경 쓰이고. 아무래도 이 일은 나라와 나라의 싸움 따위의 차원을 넘어선 수준까지 간 것 같군.<end>"

## 1780085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もっとでかいもんが、<line-break>ずっと歴史の裏で<line-break>動いていたってわけだ。<end>"
EN: "Something much bigger has been at work behind the scenes throughout history.<end>"
KO: "더 큰 무언가가 줄곧 역사의 뒤에서 움직이고 있었다는 거야.<end>"

## 1780086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>お前の今までの冒険のことを、<line-break>あとで話してくれないか？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, will you tell me about your adventures later?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 네가 지금까지 겪은 모험 이야기를 나중에 들려주지 않겠나?<end>"

## 1780087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、まったく…。<line-break>飽きのこない人生だねぇ。<end>"
EN: "Honestly... What a life. Never a dull moment.<end>"
KO: "하지만 참…. 질리지 않는 인생이군.<end>"

## 1780088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カルラは、ゼネテスの機略の前に敗れた。<line-break>だが、青竜軍の兵力は、依然として<line-break>ロストール軍を大きく上回っていた。<line-break>　<line-break>ゼネテスの勝利とは関係なく、<line-break>勝機はいまだカルラにあった。<line-break>しかし、青竜軍は進軍しなかった。<end>"
EN: "Karula had been defeated by Xenetes's stratagem. Even so, the Azure Dragon Army still vastly outnumbered Rostorl's forces. Despite Xenetes's victory, Karula still had a chance to prevail. Yet the Azure Dragon Army did not advance.<end>"
KO: "카를라는 제네테스의 계략에 패했다. 하지만 청룡군의 병력은 여전히 로스톨군을 크게 웃돌고 있었다. 제네테스의 승리와 관계없이 승기는 아직 카를라에게 있었다. 그러나 청룡군은 진군하지 않았다.<end>"

## 1780089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメア死す。<line-break>ゼネテスの放った<line-break>偽情報を裏付けるかのごとく、<line-break>　<line-break>ゼグナ鉱山で<line-break>闇の神器を探索したあと、<line-break>青竜軍に合流するはずだった<line-break>ネメアが、ついに戻らなかったのである。<end>"
EN: "Nemea is dead. As if to bear out the false report spread by Xenetes, Nemea―who was to join the Azure Dragon Army after searching Zegna Mine for the Artifact of Darkness―never returned.<end>"
KO: "네메아 사망. 제네테스가 흘린 거짓 정보를 뒷받침하듯, 제그나 광산에서 어둠의 신기를 탐색한 뒤 청룡군에 합류할 예정이던 네메아는 끝내 돌아오지 않았다.<end>"

## 1780090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　一方、ロストールでは、<line-break>　レムオンが突然姿を消したことをきっかけに<line-break>　　　　国内の戦力が、再び統合され、<line-break>　青竜軍と戦えるだけの態勢が取り戻された。<line-break>　　こうして、ネメアを失ったディンガルと<line-break>　　　国内の統合に成功したロストールは、<line-break>　　　　再びにらみ合いの状態に入った。<end>"
EN: "Meanwhile, in Rostorl, Lemghon's sudden disappearance prompted the country's forces to reunite, restoring its ability to face the Azure Dragon Army in battle. And so Dyneskal, having lost Nemea, and Rostorl, having succeeded in unifying its forces at home, once again settled into a standoff.<end>"
KO: "한편 로스톨에서는 레무온이 갑자기 모습을 감춘 것을 계기로 국내 전력이 다시 통합되어 청룡군과 맞설 수 있는 태세를 되찾았다. 이렇게 네메아를 잃은 딩갈과 국내 통합에 성공한 로스톨은 다시 서로 대치하는 상태에 들어갔다.<end>"

## 1780091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "消えたレムオンとお姫さまが　　　　　<line-break>気になるが、ともかくこっちは、<line-break>カルラとやり合える態勢になった。<line-break>だが裏では、ロストールとディンガルの<line-break>争いよりも、もっとやっかいなことに<line-break>なってるのかもしれんな。<end>"
EN: "Lemghon and the princess have vanished, and that worries me. For now, though, we're ready to take on Karula. But something even more troublesome than the conflict between Rostorl and Dyneskal may be unfolding behind the scenes.<end>"
KO: "사라진 레무온과 공주님이 신경 쓰이지만, 어쨌든 이쪽은 카를라와 맞붙을 태세를 갖췄다. 하지만 뒤에서는 로스톨과 딘갈의 싸움보다 훨씬 더 골치 아픈 일이 벌어지고 있는 것 같군….<end>"

## 1780092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストール王宮は<line-break>しばらく戒厳令下に入る。<line-break>だから自由には会えなくなるが、<end>"
EN: "Rostorl Palace will be under martial law for a while, so we won't be able to meet whenever we want. But...<end>"
KO: "로스톨 왕궁은 당분간 계엄령에 들어간다. 그래서 자유롭게 만날 수는 없겠지만,<end>"

## 1780093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec178-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もし、お前さんが、<line-break>俺の力を必要とするならば、<line-break>いつでも呼び出してくれ。<line-break>俺には、<line-break>お前さんが関わってることの方が、<line-break>重大事だって思えるんでね。<end>"
EN: "If you ever need my help, just call for me. As far as I'm concerned, whatever you're involved in matters more.<end>"
KO: "네가 내 힘을 필요로 한다면 언제든 불러 줘. 나한테는 네가 관련된 일이 더 중대한 일처럼 느껴지거든.<end>"

## 1790001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec179-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、伯爵様。王妃様が<line-break>謁見の間に来るようにとのことでした。<end>"
EN: "Ah, Count. Her Majesty the Queen has asked you to come to the audience chamber.<end>"
KO: "아, 백작님. 왕비님께서 알현실로 오라고 하셨습니다.<end>"

## 1790002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec179-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、謁見の間まで<line-break>ご案内いたします。<end>"
EN: "Allow me to escort you to the audience chamber.<end>"
KO: "그럼 알현실까지 안내해 드리겠습니다.<end>"

## 1790003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec179-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今日、そなたたちを集めたのは、<line-break>エリエナイ公、そなたに<line-break>問いただしたいことがあったからだ。<line-break>エリエナイ公レムオンよ。<line-break>そなた、ダルケニスではないか？<end>"
EN: "I called you all here today because there is something I must ask you, Duke of Elienai. Lemghon, Duke of Elienai, are you a Darkenith?<end>"
KO: "오늘 그대들을 모은 건 엘리에나이 공, 그대에게 따져 묻고 싶은 게 있었기 때문이다. 엘리에나이 공 레무온이여. 그대, 다르케니스가 아닌가?<end>"

## 1790004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec179-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…何をご冗談を。<end>"
EN: "...You must be joking.<end>"
KO: "…무슨 농담을.<end>"

## 1790005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec179-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我が手の者が、<line-break>そなたが銀髪赤眼で血を求め、<line-break>吼えておったところを見ておる。<end>"
EN: "One of my agents saw you with silver hair and red eyes, howling for blood.<end>"
KO: "내 수하가 네가 은발에 붉은 눈으로 피를 갈구하며 울부짖는 모습을 보았다.<end>"

## 1790006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec179-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ダルケニスだったってことは、<line-break>そりゃ、前の新月の晩のことだよな、<line-break>叔母貴。<end>"
EN: "If you're saying he was a Darkenith, you mean on the night of the last new moon, right, Aunt?<end>"
KO: "…다르케니스였다는 건 지난번 신월의 밤 얘기지, 이모.<end>"

## 1790007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec179-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その通りだ。<end>"
EN: "Precisely.<end>"
KO: "그 말대로다.<end>"

## 1790008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec179-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "叔母貴の言う手の者ってのは、<line-break>もしかして、ツェラシェルか？<end>"
EN: "This agent of yours―would that happen to be Zeraschel?<end>"
KO: "숙모가 말한 수하라는 게 혹시 체라셸인가?<end>"

## 1790009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec179-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そいつは、変だな。<line-break>俺はその晩、そのツェラシェルと<line-break>一緒だったんですよ。<end>"
EN: "That's odd. I was with Zeraschel that night.<end>"
KO: "그건 이상하군. 나는 그날 밤 그 체라셸과함께 있었거든요.<end>"

## 1790010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec179-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…その言葉に偽りはないか？<line-break>ファーロス総司令。<end>"
EN: "...Is that the truth, Commander-in-Chief Pharos?<end>"
KO: "…그 말에 거짓은 없나? 파로스 총사령관.<end>"

## 1790011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec179-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "千年樹に賭けて真実です、陛下。<line-break>それにノーブル伯も一緒だったしな。<line-break>な、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "I swear by the Thousand-Year Tree, Your Majesty, it's true. The Count of Noble was there too. Isn't that right, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "천년수에 맹세코 사실입니다, 폐하. 노블 백작도 함께 있었고요. 그렇지, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1790012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec179-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか。ノーブル伯と総司令が<line-break>そう申すなら、間違いあるまい。<line-break>エリエナイ公もとんだ嫌疑であったな。<end>"
EN: "Then there can be no mistake, if both the Count of Noble and the Commander-in-Chief vouch for it. It seems the Duke of Elienai has been falsely accused.<end>"
KO: "그런가. 노블 백작과 총사령관이 그렇게 말한다면 틀림없겠지. 에리에나이 공도 괜한 의심을 받았군.<end>"

## 1790013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec179-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…陛下、気分が悪いので、<line-break>失礼いたします。<end>"
EN: "Your Majesty, I am feeling unwell. Please excuse me.<end>"
KO: "…폐하, 몸이 좋지 않아 먼저 실례하겠습니다.<end>"

## 1790014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec179-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "恩を売る気か？<end>"
EN: "Are you trying to put me in your debt?<end>"
KO: "생색내려는 거냐?<end>"

## 1790015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec179-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなんじゃねぇさ。<end>"
EN: "That's not why I did it.<end>"
